Net effect: the student works normally for up to ~1 h after the reset (JWT expiry is the default 3600 s — docs/platform/audit/2026-11-08-research.md:23, nothing in setup.md changes it), then page navigations are redirected by proxy.ts:60-63 and API calls get 401 from app/api/jobs/[id]/route.ts:25 (and the generate route via the same AuthError). (3) The polling bug is confirmed: GenerateClient.ts:50-59 fetchJob returns null for ANY !response.ok (401, 403, 404, 503…), and useLiveJob (GenerateClient.ts:73-97) treats null as "try again in 5 s" with no give-up — the card used by GenerateJobCard.tsx:131 and GalleryGrid.tsx:51 shows the active status forever and keeps hitting the function (one dead tab = 720 pointless invocations/hour). The unauthorized message (messages.ts:40 via platformErrorMessage, GeneratePanel.tsx:477, QuizForm.tsx:100) has no link. Corrections to the colleague's framing: (a) frequency is lower than claimed — Supabase sessions on one device last indefinitely (research.md:28), so a student who stays on the same laptop never needs a reset; the overlap "logged in AND reset" arises with phone+laptop students, a student moving seats while the old seat's browser is still logged in, or a teacher resetting pre-emptively. (b) Fix item (4) is already implemented: StudentList.tsx:151-160 shows login and password once, right after the reset. (c) The job itself is not lost: after re-login, the lesson page (LessonTaskTools.tsx:201/214) and gallery (GalleryGrid.tsx:51) re-mount useLiveJob and advanceJob finishes/refunds the job; only the stale tab is affected. (d) The same finding exists as TUX-06 (low) in docs/platform/audit/2026-11-08-findings.md:494 — merge them. Scenario at 10: at most one student per reset, bounced to /login at an arbitrary moment within the hour, with a stuck 'в работе' card in the old tab; no money or data at risk. At 50: a few resets per workshop are plausible, each affecting one student the same way; plus every logged-out tab keeps polling at 5 s. Severity stays medium mainly because of the never-terminating poll loop, which also triggers on every other persistent non-OK (global signOut from another tab — see AUTH-6, 404 after a job is removed, 503 not_configured), not just on password reset; the reset-specific part alone would be low. Confidence: high (verified in local node_modules and upstream source).
- **При 10:** A student forgets the password after a break, the teacher resets it while the student's laptop is still logged in: the laptop works for up to an hour, then suddenly redirects to /login; any pending video card never updates. Confusing but one student.
- **При 50:** Several resets per workshop are likely (lost cards). Same effect for each; the teacher does not know that a reset implies a forced re-login, and students report 'the site threw me out' during generation.
- **Как чиним (уточнённый план):** (1) components/platform/GenerateClient.ts:50-59 — make fetchJob return a discriminated result: { ok: true, job } | { ok: false, status: number, code?: string } (code from the JSON body). In useLiveJob (lines 73-97): on status 401/403/404 stop polling (set a local `stopped` state with the code) — to avoid reacting to a one-off race during cookie refresh, stop only after two consecutive 401s or after one 401 followed by a re-check 5 s later; on 5xx/network keep polling but back off (5 s → 10 s → 20 s, cap 30 s, reset to 5 s on success). Expose the stop reason so GenerateJobCard/GalleryGrid render «Сессия закончилась — войдите заново, задача продолжится» with <Link href={`/login?next=${encodeURIComponent(location.pathname + location.search)}`}> (the existing LoginForm reads ?next and login/actions.ts safeNext already allows /app paths), and for 404 «Задача удалена». Do NOT call onSettled for these — the job is not settled. (2) Render errorMessages.unauthorized with the same login link where notices are shown: GeneratePanel.tsx:477, QuizForm.tsx:100, FormError.tsx:7 (a small <AuthNotice code=…/> helper that checks code === 'unauthorized'). (3) Teacher UI: app/(platform)/teach/groups/[id]/StudentList.tsx:149 — extend the hint to «Новый пароль покажем один раз. Старый перестанет работать, и все устройства ученика выйдут из кабинета в течение часа — пусть войдёт заново с новым паролем.»; add the same sentence to docs/platform/setup.md in the teacher section. (4) Drop the colleague's item 4 — StudentList.tsx:151-160 already shows the new password immediately. (5) Optional, if immediate revocation is preferred for security: nothing to change — GoTrue already revokes; if instead the owner wants the student NOT to be kicked out, there is no admin API option (UpdatePassword(tx, nil) is unconditional), so document the behaviour rather than fight it. (6) Merge with TUX-06 in docs/platform/audit/2026-11-08-findings.md:494 and record under «Найдено при проверке» in docs/course/770-status.md as one medium item. Side effects checked: stopping on 401 cannot strand a real job because reopening any page after login re-mounts useLiveJob and advanceJob resumes; backoff on 5xx slightly delays completion visibility during gateway outages (acceptable, and reduces Vercel invocations); the two-consecutive-401 guard covers the multi-tab refresh race described in research.md:29.
- **Сложность:** small

### BP-4 · medium · Guest messages and leads (names, contacts, free text) are stored forever — no retention, no deletion when the bot retires or the group ends
- **Файлы:** supabase/migrations/20261101200000_init.sql:226-253, lib/server/bot/store.ts:299-306, lib/server/bot/telegram-flow.ts:218-231, lib/server/bot/lifetime.ts:233-250, app/api/b/[slug]/route.ts:76-83
- **Вердикт скептика:** Confirmed from code (confidence: high on facts, medium on severity weighting).

What the code actually does:
- supabase/migrations/20261101200000_init.sql:226-253 — bot_messages (content ≤4000, conversation_id, ip_hash) and bot_leads (jsonb ≤4096 bytes with name/contact/free text) have no TTL column, no retention index, no scheduled job. The only automatic deletion is `on delete cascade` from bots → profiles → auth.users (init.sql:44, 200, 228, 246), and there is NO code path anywhere that deletes a profile or auth user: `grep` finds only `auth.admin.createUser` and `updateUserById` (app/(platform)/teach/actions.ts:233, 308); teachers can only set `disabled`. So in practice bot data is never deleted.
- lib/server/bot/store.ts:299-306 — `deleteConversation` is the sole `.delete()` on bot tables, used only by `resetPreview` for the owner's own preview conversation (app/(platform)/app/bot/actions.ts:268-273).
- lib/server/bot/telegram-flow.ts:94-107 (the finding cites 218-231; the file is 107 lines — line numbers in the finding are wrong but the claim holds) — `retireTelegram` only calls deleteWebhook + `wipeTelegram(bot.id)` + audit; messages and leads untouched. lifetime.ts (80 lines, finding cites 233-250 — also wrong lines) is pure date math; it deletes nothing.
- app/api/b/[slug]/route.ts:76-83 — `BOT_SESSION_COOKIE` with `maxAge: 30 * 24 * 3600`, confirmed.
- app/(platform)/app/bot/export/page.tsx:62-64 explicitly promises retired bots' owners: «Заявки по-прежнему во вкладке «Заявки»» — i.e. keeping leads after retirement is current *intended* behaviour, not an oversight. This matters for the fix (see below).
- content/privacy.ts:69 says the platform section of the privacy policy «появится», and :79-81 states only «храню, пока мы общаемся о курсе» — nothing about guest chat logs or leads, so the finding's claim that /privacy cannot honestly state a retention period is accurate.
- docs/decisions.md:108-110 already lists this as open question #3 for the owner («Данные заявок… удаляются через 30 дней после вечера») — the gap is known and undecided, not implemented.

Mitigations already in place that soften (but do not refute) the finding: for owners under 18 the «Контакт» field is dropped (lib/server/bot/leads.ts:114,129) and free-text lead fields are rejected if they contain phone/e-mail/address (leads.ts:160, brain.ts:242 `hasPersonalData`); guest input is capped at 1000 chars (route.ts:26 BOT_LIMITS.messageMax) and the SQL insert does `left(p_content, 4000)` (20261105100000_security_hardening.sql:96). So the finding's fix item «truncate stored guest content to 1000» is already effectively true for role='user' rows and should be dropped.

Scenario check: at 10 users on Monday nothing breaks and no money is at risk — this is a storage-limitation/GDPR Art. 5(1)(e) debt plus a manual-SQL-only path for a parent's erasure request. At 50 users × multiple centers the volume grows linearly and the owner still has no UI to honour deletion; a leaked service key exposes everything (service_role has full grants on bot_leads, init.sql:363-375). That matches «medium = degraded (compliance/trust), not lesson-breaking». Severity medium is correct; not high because no student sees an error and no credits are lost.

Problems with the proposed fix (hence correctedFix): (1) «delete all messages/leads when retireTelegram or 'бот переехал' first fires» contradicts the product promise in export/page.tsx:64 and the owner's own proposal in decisions.md:110 (30 days after the event, not at retirement); (2) piggy-backing a sweep on `insert_bot_message` puts a potentially large DELETE inside the hot path that already holds `pg_advisory_xact_lock` per bot/sender (hardening.sql:58-61) — exactly the lock students contend on during the lesson; (3) it ignores that Supabase ships pg_cron on every plan including Free (Dashboard → Integrations → Cron), so «no cron» applies only to Vercel Hobby — a DB-side schedule is simpler and more reliable than a lazy sweep from serverless routes.
- **При 10:** After the course, 10 bots × a few hundred guest messages and dozens of leads with contacts stay in the EU database indefinitely; the /privacy page cannot honestly state a retention period; a deletion request from a parent requires manual SQL.
- **При 50:** 50 bots per workshop, sold to other centers: tens of thousands of guest messages and thousands of leads accumulate across centers with no owner able to delete them from the UI; one leaked service key exposes all of it.
- **Как чиним (уточнённый план):** 1. Decide retention first (docs/decisions.md §7.3 is still open): proposal — guest bot_messages 30 days from created_at (they are only read as last 10–20 per conversation, store.ts:261, and for 1 h / 24 h / 7 d counters — hardening.sql:76-88, dal.ts:274 — so anything older is dead data); bot_leads until groups.ends_on + 30 days (or 30 days after created_at for unlimited staff bots). Record the decision in docs/decisions.md and docs/course/770-open-questions.md.

2. Implement the sweep in Postgres, not in the request path: new migration adding `private.sweep_bot_data()` (security definer, `set search_path=''`) that deletes in batches of ≤5000 rows via `delete … where ctid in (select ctid … limit 5000)` for: bot_messages where created_at < now()-interval '30 days' and conversation_id not like 'owner:%'; bot_leads joined to bots→profiles→groups where ends_on + 30 days < current_date (Berlin). Schedule it with pg_cron (`select cron.schedule('sweep_bot_data','17 3 * * *', $$select private.sweep_bot_data()$$)`) — pg_cron is available on Supabase Free, so Vercel Hobby's lack of cron is not a blocker. Add a pgTAP test in supabase/tests/ asserting old rows go and fresh/preview rows stay. Only if pg_cron is refused in the dashboard, fall back to a lazy trigger: `after(() => sweepIfDue())` in app/api/b/[slug]/route.ts and app/api/tg/[botId]/route.ts gated by a single-row `private.sweep_state(last_run)` and `pg_try_advisory_xact_lock`, running at most once per 24 h — never inside insert_bot_message.

Side-effect check of the deletes: rate-limit windows (1 h, 24 h) and listGroupBots' 7-day count are unaffected; the dedupe indexes (dedupe_key, telegram_update_id) only need ~24 h of history because Telegram stops retrying after that; conversation history is per-conversation last-N, so a guest returning after 30 days simply starts fresh (cookie also expires at 30 days, route.ts:82 — align the two).

3. Do NOT delete leads at retireTelegram / «бот переехал»: export/page.tsx:64 promises they stay in «Заявки». Instead add an audited owner action `deleteLead(id)` / `deleteAllLeads()` in app/(platform)/app/bot/actions.ts (audit action e.g. `bot.leads_deleted`) and a teacher action in app/(platform)/teach to wipe a student's bot_messages+bot_leads (for a parent's erasure request) — currently that requires manual SQL. Update export/page.tsx text to state the retention date.

4. Add the platform section to content/privacy.ts (currently :69 says it «появится»): what guest chat data and leads are stored, by whom (bot owner + teacher), for how long (the numbers from step 1), and how to request deletion.

5. Drop the «truncate guest content to 1000» item from the fix: already enforced by BOT_LIMITS.messageMax=1000 (route.ts:26) and left(…,4000) in insert_bot_message.

Effort: medium (one migration + pgTAP test, two server actions, privacy text). No impact on Monday's lesson; can ship after the course.
- **Сложность:** medium

### DB-01 · medium · Group-page star stats read star_ledger without pagination: silently capped at 1000 rows (PostgREST Max Rows)
- **Файлы:** lib/server/dal/teacher.ts:369-384, app/(platform)/teach/groups/[id]/page.tsx:42,96-110, supabase/config.toml:13
- **Вердикт скептика:** Confirmed from code, with corrected line references (the finding's line numbers are stale: teacher.ts has 236 lines). /home/user/workshop/lib/server/dal/teacher.ts:164-190 `getGroupStarStats` runs `admin.from("star_ledger").select("delta, reason").in("user_id", students...)` with no `.limit()`/`.range()` and sums the returned rows in JS (lines 184-189). The admin client is service_role (lib/server/supabase/admin.ts:14-19); PostgREST's max-rows cap applies to every role, and this repo pins it to 1000 (supabase/config.toml:13 `max_rows = 1000`, which also matches the hosted default). supabase-js returns the truncated page without any truncation signal. The authors already know this: /home/user/workshop/lib/server/group-reports.ts:7-27 documents "PostgREST отдаёт не больше 1000 строк за запрос" and pages `star_ledger` via `fetchAll` with `.order("id").range(from,to)` (lines 78-87) — the group page query was simply missed. The result is rendered as «Роздано/Потрачено» in /home/user/workshop/app/(platform)/teach/groups/[id]/page.tsx:42,103-108.

Row rate check: text chat costs 1★ per message (init.sql:859, one debit row each), images 2-5★ (one debit row, plus a refund row on failure, init.sql:680-682), video 25-50★, bot replies one `bot_reply` row each (bot/brain.ts:209-212), plus one `welcome` row per student. 10 students: ~300 rows/lesson, cap crossed around lesson 3-4; afterwards «Потрачено» stops growing while «Роздано» still counts the (early) welcome rows, so the card silently reads as if the group stopped spending. 50 students: ~1500 rows after the first 75-minute workshop, so the card is already wrong at the end of lesson 1 (shows roughly 2/3 of real spend), and after 5 lessons shows ~1/7.

Severity stays medium: this is a display-only defect. Money is not at risk — spending limits are enforced inside SQL (`private.lock_for_spend`, security_hardening.sql:190-204) which aggregates correctly; the student report page (group-reports.ts) is correct; and the ledger itself is intact. But a teacher deciding whether to grant more stars from a group budget sees a misleading number, and the owner intends to sell the platform — a stats card that silently stops counting is a credibility problem. Confidence: high (direct code reading + repo's own config and comments).

The proposed SQL-aggregate fix is right in principle; two corrections: (1) the function must live in schema `public` (config.toml exposes only `public` to PostgREST; `private.*` cannot be called via `.rpc()`), following the existing `rate_limit_hit` pattern (security_hardening.sql:181-184: revoke from public/anon/authenticated, grant execute to service_role); (2) it must filter `p.role = 'student'` to match the current TS logic (teacher.ts:170-171), otherwise a teacher assigned to the group would be counted. The interim "reuse fetchAll from group-reports.ts" is risky as written: group-reports.ts imports `listStudents` from dal/teacher.ts (line 4), so importing fetchAll back into teacher.ts creates a circular import — move the helper to a shared module instead.
- **При 10:** 10 students × ~20 chat + 6 media + welcome ≈ 30 rows/lesson → 300 rows/lesson; the 1000-row cap is crossed in lesson 4: from then on «Потрачено» and «Роздано» on the group page under-count silently (the teacher sees budget numbers that stop growing).
- **При 50:** 50 students × 30 rows ≈ 1500 rows after the FIRST 75-minute workshop → the group page is already wrong at the end of lesson 1; a teacher deciding whether to grant more stars sees spending that is 30-60% too low. With 5 lessons ≈ 7500 rows, the card shows roughly 1/7 of real spending.
- **Как чиним (уточнённый план):** Preferred (small migration + 10-line TS change):

1. New migration, following the rate_limit_hit pattern in supabase/migrations/20261105100000_security_hardening.sql:181-184:
```sql
create or replace function public.group_star_stats(p_group uuid)
returns table (given bigint, spent bigint)
language sql stable security definer set search_path = '' as $$
  select
    coalesce(sum(case when l.reason in ('welcome','grant','adjust','quiz_bonus') then l.delta else 0 end), 0) as given,
    greatest(0, coalesce(sum(case when l.reason in ('debit','refund','bot_reply') then -l.delta else 0 end), 0)) as spent
  from public.star_ledger l
  join public.profiles p on p.id = l.user_id
  where p.group_id = p_group and p.role = 'student'
$$;
revoke all on function public.group_star_stats(uuid) from public, anon, authenticated;
grant execute on function public.group_star_stats(uuid) to service_role;
```
Must be in schema `public` (supabase/config.toml:11 exposes only public to PostgREST). Keep `p.role = 'student'` to match teacher.ts:170-171. Add the signature to lib/server/supabase/types.ts (Functions) and a pgTAP case in supabase/tests (authenticated cannot execute; result equals the sum for a seeded group with >1 student).

2. /home/user/workshop/lib/server/dal/teacher.ts:164-190 — replace the two queries with:
```ts
const { data, error } = await createAdminClient().rpc("group_star_stats", { p_group: groupId });
if (error) throw new Error(`teacher.getGroupStarStats failed (${error.code})`);
const row = data?.[0];
return { given: Number(row?.given ?? 0), spent: Number(row?.spent ?? 0) };
```
(bigint comes back as string/number depending on driver; coerce with Number.) Access control is unchanged: `assertStaff` + caller's `getGroup` already gate the groupId.

Interim/alternative if a migration is not wanted this week: move `fetchAll` (lib/server/group-reports.ts:13-27) into a shared helper, e.g. lib/server/supabase/page.ts, import it from both group-reports.ts and dal/teacher.ts (do NOT import teacher.ts <- group-reports.ts: group-reports.ts:4 already imports listStudents from teacher.ts, which would make a cycle), and page the ledger query with `.order("id").range(from, to)`. Note the index star_ledger_user_created_idx (user_id, created_at) serves the `in(user_id)` filter either way; at 50 students × 5 lessons this is ~8 sequential round-trips per page view, which is why the SQL aggregate is preferred.

Process guard: add one line to docs/platform/setup.md (near the pgTAP section) — "every list query on tables that grow per message/job (star_ledger, jobs, bot_messages, quiz_attempts, lesson_progress) must use .limit()/.range() or fetchAll, or aggregate in SQL" — and grep for `.from("star_ledger")` without `.limit`/`.range`/`head: true` as a review checklist item (today the only offender is teacher.ts:175-180; student.ts:16-20 uses limit(6), bot/store.ts:325-329 uses head:true).
- **Сложность:** small

### DB-02 · medium · Group report reads the whole ledger/progress of a group through RLS, evaluating 3 security-definer sub-queries per row; grows linearly with course length
- **Файлы:** lib/server/group-reports.ts:61-89, supabase/migrations/20261101200000_init.sql:314-327,391-392,406-410, app/(platform)/teach/groups/[id]/report/page.tsx:189-194
- **Вердикт скептика:** Confirmed from code, with two corrections that make it worse than stated and one that changes the likely failure mode.

What the code does (verified): lib/server/group-reports.ts:61 builds the teacher's RLS client (createClient) and lines 62-89 page through ALL lesson_progress, quiz_attempts and debit/refund/bot_reply star_ledger rows of the group, 1000 rows per request (PAGE=1000, line 13; the file's own comment at lines 9-10 says PostgREST caps at 1000). fetchAll (lines 18-27) is sequential per table; the three tables run in parallel (Promise.all). Policies ledger_read / quiz_attempts_read / lesson_progress_read (supabase/migrations/20261101200000_init.sql:391-392, 406-410) are `user_id = (select auth.uid()) or private.teaches_user(user_id)`. For a teacher the first branch is false on every row, so Postgres evaluates teaches_user(user_id) → teaches_group(group_id) → is_admin() + groups⋈profiles EXISTS (init.sql:306-327) per row: 3 nested SQL functions, ~4 PK index lookups. All three are `security definer ... set search_path = ''`, and both properties prevent SQL-function inlining, so the per-row call cannot be flattened or hoisted. No later migration (20261103*, 20261105*, 20261106*) touches these helpers or policies. Each chat message is a 'text' job and create_job writes one 'debit' row (lib/server/wallet.ts:78, init.sql:599), each bot reply writes a 'bot_reply' row (lib/server/bot/brain.ts:212), so the row-count assumptions are sound: ≈1.5k rows after 5 lessons for 10 students, 7-10k for 50.

Correction 1 — the finding understates per-page cost. `.order("id").range(from,to)` means every page request filters (RLS included) and sorts the whole candidate set before OFFSET/LIMIT is applied; Supabase's own RLS-performance doc says exactly this ("Postgres evaluates a policy expression against each candidate row ... including those using limit, offset, and ordering"). So at 50 students the ledger table is not ~3k policy evaluations per page but ~7-10k evaluations × 8-10 sequential pages ≈ 60-100k nested security-definer calls per report load, not ~30k total.

Correction 2 — the first hard limit hit is more likely Supabase's statement_timeout, not Vercel. Supabase docs (Timeouts guide) set the `authenticated` role's default statement_timeout to 8 s; the RLS client runs as `authenticated`. If a single 1000-row page over ~10k candidate rows exceeds 8 s on a Nano/Micro instance (plausible but not measured: ~0.1-0.2 ms per row of nested function calls gives 1-2 s per page on a warm Micro, several times that under the Monday load), fetchAll throws `groupReports.ledger failed (57014)` and the Suspense boundary in app/(platform)/teach/groups/[id]/report/page.tsx:16-18 renders an error — a deterministic failure regardless of Vercel settings. The Vercel page timeout is the second risk: the report page exports no maxDuration (report/page.tsx has only `metadata`, line 11; compare groups/[id]/page.tsx:19), so under non-Fluid Hobby it runs with the 10 s default while 8-10 sequential pages of 1-2 s each take 10-20 s wall time. Whether Fluid Compute is enabled is NOT determinable from the repo (no vercel.json, no mention in docs) — confidence on the 504 branch is medium; on the degradation and the statement_timeout exposure it is high.

Scenario at 10 students: ≈1.5k ledger + ~300 progress rows → 2 ledger pages, ~4.5k policy evaluations total, 1-3 s. Degraded, works. Scenario at 50 students: 7-10k ledger rows → 8-10 sequential pages each re-filtering the full set; 10-20 s total on Fluid (teacher waits, page looks hung), 504 on non-Fluid Hobby, or 57014 statement timeout from Supabase if any one page crosses 8 s. The teacher cannot show results to the center at course end. Students are unaffected, so I keep medium (degraded / one staff page fails), noting that at 50 students it is a hard failure of that page rather than a slowdown.

Correction 3 — the proposed fix's reference pattern is itself buggy. getGroupStarStats (lib/server/dal/teacher.ts:165-193) does use the admin client, but it reads all ledger rows of the group in ONE unpaged request; at 50 students it is silently truncated at PostgREST's 1000-row cap, so the group page's "spent" figure is already wrong at that scale. The fix must not copy that pattern; it should replace it. Also, PostgREST aggregates are disabled by default on Supabase, so `select('user_id, delta.sum()')` is not an option — an RPC is the right vehicle, and supabase-js can only call functions in an exposed schema (public), not private.
- **При 10:** After 5 lessons ≈ 1500 ledger rows + ~300 progress rows → 2 PostgREST pages, ~5-6k RLS sub-selects; page loads in 1-3 s. Degraded but works.
- **При 50:** After 5 lessons ≈ 7-10k ledger rows → 8-10 sequential 1000-row requests (fetchAll is sequential, group-reports.ts:20-25), each paying ~3k function calls plus JSON serialization of 1000 rows; on Nano/Micro shared CPU the report page takes 5-15 s and can hit the Vercel page timeout (10 s on non-Fluid Hobby per [research]) → teacher sees a 504 for the report at the exact moment the course ends and they want to show results to the center.
- **Как чиним (уточнённый план):** 1) New migration: `create function public.group_report_spending(p_group uuid) returns table (user_id uuid, spent_total integer, spent_today integer, generations integer, refunds integer, bot_replies integer) language sql stable set search_path = '' as $$ select l.user_id, coalesce(sum(-l.delta),0)::int, coalesce(sum(-l.delta) filter (where l.created_at >= (date_trunc('day', now() at time zone 'Europe/Berlin') at time zone 'Europe/Berlin')),0)::int, count(*) filter (where l.reason='debit')::int, count(*) filter (where l.reason='refund')::int, count(*) filter (where l.reason='bot_reply')::int from public.star_ledger l join public.profiles p on p.id = l.user_id where p.group_id = p_group and p.role = 'student' and l.reason in ('debit','refund','bot_reply') group by l.user_id $$;` — same Berlin-day expression as init.sql:491 so the report agrees with the daily-cap logic. Add `public.group_report_progress(p_group uuid) returns table (user_id uuid, lesson_id text, steps integer)` with `group by user_id, lesson_id`. Keep the function security-invoker (caller is service_role, RLS bypassed anyway) and follow the hardening pattern (20261105100000_security_hardening.sql:104-106): `revoke all on function ... from public, anon, authenticated; grant execute ... to service_role;`. Authorization stays in TypeScript exactly as for every other admin-client path: the report page already calls getGroup(user, id) (teacher.ts:108-117, RLS + teacher_id check) before getGroupReport, and listStudents is RLS-scoped; add an `assertTeachesGroup(user, groupId)` call at the top of getGroupReport for defence in depth since it will now read with admin rights.

2) In lib/server/group-reports.ts replace the three fetchAll loops with: `admin.rpc("group_report_spending", {p_group})`, `admin.rpc("group_report_progress", {p_group})`, and a plain admin select of quiz_attempts `(user_id, lesson_id, score, max_score, bonus) .in("user_id", ids)` (bounded by unique(user_id, lesson_id) at init.sql:185 → ≤ students × lessons rows, no paging needed). Delete fetchAll/PAGE/MAX_ROWS. This turns 8-10 sequential 1000-row RLS-filtered requests into 3 parallel requests returning ≤ 50 / ≤ 250 / ≤ 250 rows with zero per-row policy evaluation; the ledger aggregate uses star_ledger_user_created_idx (init.sql:171) via the profiles join and needs no new index at this scale.

3) Make getGroupStarStats (teacher.ts:165-193) consume the same RPC (sum spent_total; `given` from a second small aggregate `sum(delta) filter (where reason in ('welcome','grant','adjust','quiz_bonus'))` added to the function) — this also removes its silent 1000-row truncation bug.

4) `export const maxDuration = 60;` in app/(platform)/teach/groups/[id]/report/page.tsx as a safety net (no-op under Fluid, necessary under non-Fluid Hobby's 10 s default). Add a supabase/tests case that a teacher of another group gets `forbidden` from getGroupReport, since the data path no longer relies on RLS.

Optional, broader follow-up (not required for this finding, higher blast radius): rewrite the per-row `private.teaches_user(user_id)` branch in ledger_read / lesson_progress_read / quiz_attempts_read / wallets_read / bots_read as `user_id in (select private.taught_user_ids())` (a set-returning security-definer function evaluated once per statement, hash semi-join), per Supabase's RLS-performance guidance; it speeds up every teacher-side RLS read but must handle the admin case (all students) and be re-tested against supabase/tests.
- **Сложность:** medium

### DB-04 · medium · A text (chat) job that dies between create_job and complete_job stays `queued` with the debit kept, and is refunded only if the browser later polls that exact job
- **Файлы:** lib/server/generate.ts:313-330, lib/server/jobs.ts:277-285, lib/server/job-progress.ts:124-126, lib/server/lesson-progress.ts:153,171-189, supabase/migrations/20261101200000_init.sql:570-573
- **Вердикт скептика:** Verified against the code; the mechanism is real, two details of the evidence need correcting, and the proposed fix is partly ineffective as written.

Confirmed:
- lib/server/generate.ts:307-326 (runText): create_job debits first (generate.ts:269-280), then `await generateText()` (≤50 s budget), then `await completeJob()` at :322. `rpcBoolean` THROWS on any PostgREST error (lib/server/jobs.ts:16-23). The throw propagates to app/api/generate/route.ts:49-53 which returns `{error:"server_error"}` with NO jobId. Job stays `queued`, `gateway_task_id` null, debit kept.
- Second, unmentioned path to the same state: at generate.ts:319 the result of `refundJob()` is not checked; `refundJob` returns `{ok:false}` instead of throwing (lib/server/wallet.ts:98-103). A transient DB error there leaves the job queued+debited while the route returns 502 `gateway_error` (with jobId, but see below).
- Only refund path for a `queued` row is `advanceJob` on GET /api/jobs/[id] (lib/server/job-progress.ts:124-126, rule lib/server/job-rules.ts:14,37-39 = 3 min). There is no sweeper for text jobs (the only existing sweeper is montage-only: lib/server/studio.ts:298-310 `closeStaleUploads`).
- A text job is never shown in the gallery (lib/server/gallery.ts:27-32 MEDIA_ACTIONS excludes text), never in any teacher view (no teach/* page queries jobs by status), and in the lesson page only via getLessonState: `task_ref not null`, 80 newest rows, 6 newest per task (lib/server/lesson-progress.ts:17,36-54). Those 6 are rendered via GenerateJobCard→useLiveJob (components/platform/LessonTaskTools.tsx:98,191-203; GenerateJobCard.tsx:131; GenerateClient.ts:65-100), so a stuck job DOES self-heal IF the student re-renders the lesson page (navigation/refresh — a text success does not call onJob/refresh, GeneratePanel.tsx:185-191) while it is still among the 6 newest messages of that task. After 6 more chat messages it is invisible forever.
- The cap counts every `queued`/`submitted`/`storing` row regardless of age (supabase/migrations/20261101200000_init.sql:570-573); phantom rows therefore count forever toward `too_many_jobs`.

Corrections to the finding:
1. The 60 s kill is less likely than stated: `withRetries` caps total gateway time at the 50 s budget (lib/server/gateway.ts:201-218, per-attempt timeout = min(50 s, remaining)), leaving ~10 s for auth + 2-3 DB round trips. The more probable trigger is a transient PostgREST/DB error at completeJob (or refundJob) right after a paid generation — rare per request, non-zero over 1000 chat requests per 50-student workshop. No incident data in the repo; frequency is an estimate (confidence medium).
2. "Return jobId in the 500 response" alone does nothing: the client ignores `result.jobId` on any error (components/platform/GeneratePanel.tsx:180-184 → setError, return), and polling a text job cannot recover the text anyway — it only triggers the 3-min refund.

Scenario 10 students: ~200 chat requests per lesson; expected 0-1 incidents per lesson. A hit = one student silently loses the message price (1-3★) and carries a permanent invisible "active" job. Lesson does not break.
Scenario 50 students: ~1000 chat requests per workshop; a few incidents per workshop, more during a kie/Supabase degradation. Over several lessons a student can accumulate 5 phantoms → every generation returns 429 `too_many_jobs` with nothing visible to the student, the teacher or the gallery; the only remedy is an admin SQL fix. Severity medium is right: no lesson break, small star loss, but a permanent, invisible lockout on a product sold to other centers.
- **При 10:** Rare: one kie overload window with retries pushing a request past 60 s → 1-2 students lose 1-3★ each (not noticed) and carry a permanent phantom «active job»; after 5 such incidents over the course a student gets `too_many_jobs` (429) on every generation with nothing visible in progress.
- **При 50:** During a kie incident (owner observed ~10% Haiku success on 5 Nov) 50 students × several chat retries → dozens of functions near the 60 s ceiling; a few percent die → several students end the workshop with phantom queued jobs and lost stars; by the next workshop a handful hit `too_many_jobs` and the teacher cannot see why (jobs page shows nothing active).
- **Как чиним (уточнённый план):** Keep the finding; use this corrected fix (small effort):

1. SQL (new migration, also update supabase/tests/002_wallet.test.sql): in `public.create_job`, immediately before the `too_many_jobs` count (init.sql:570), sweep the caller's stale rows using the existing refund_job, then re-read the balance:
   ```sql
   -- задача, которую функция бросила между create_job и mark_job_submitted/complete_job (job-rules: 3 мин)
   perform public.refund_job(j.id, 'gateway_lost: stale queued')
     from public.jobs j
    where j.user_id = p_user and j.status = 'queued' and j.gateway_task_id is null
      and j.created_at < now() - interval '3 minutes';
   select w.balance into v_balance from public.wallets w where w.user_id = p_user;
   ```
   Safe: same transaction already holds the wallet row lock from lock_for_spend, refund_job's `for update` re-locks it without deadlock; refund_job is idempotent; the partial index jobs_open_idx (init.sql:148) makes the scan cheap; 3 min matches QUEUED_WITHOUT_TASK_LIMIT_MS and spec §3.1, and no legitimate job can still be pre-submit after 3 min (text budget 50 s, createTask budget 45 s, maxDuration 60). This fixes BOTH the lockout and the lost stars at exactly the moment the student next generates, with no extra RPC on read paths. Do NOT merely exclude stale rows from the count (leaves debits unrefunded). Skip the proposed `storing > 60 min` sweep: storing rows are media, visible in gallery/lesson results and already handled by advanceJob (job-progress.ts:135-143).

2. TypeScript, lib/server/generate.ts runText: wrap `completeJob` in try/catch; on failure attempt `refundJob(jobId, refundReason("gateway_error","complete failed"))` as best effort and return `{kind:"error", status:500, error:"server_error", jobId}`; also log when `refundJob` at :319 returns `ok:false`. These are belt-and-braces; the SQL sweep is the guarantee.

3. Optional (polish): in components/platform/GeneratePanel.tsx:180-184, when an error response carries `jobId` for a non-text action, call `onJob({...queued stub})` so the card polls and the 3-min refund fires without a reload. Not needed for text.

Add a pgTAP case: create a job, backdate created_at by 4 min with status queued, call create_job again → expect the old job `failed` with a `refund` ledger row and the new job created (count no longer blocked).
- **Сложность:** small

### DB-09 · medium · Signed URLs are re-created on every gallery/lesson render (no reuse), making each page view a Storage API burst and an uncached egress hit
- **Файлы:** lib/server/storage.ts:27-45, lib/server/gallery.ts:62,91,136, lib/server/lesson-progress.ts:192, lib/server/studio.ts:230-233
- **Вердикт скептика:** CONFIRMED (confidence: high on mechanism, medium on GB numbers). Code: lib/server/storage.ts:27-44 `signMediaPaths` calls `createSignedUrls` with no memoisation; lib/server/job-progress.ts:226 `toJobViews` signs every visible job's files on each call; callers lib/server/gallery.ts:62/91/136 (24 items/page), lib/server/lesson-progress.ts:56 (≤6 per task), lib/server/gallery.ts:238 `listReferenceOptions` (up to 60 jobs' files, called on EVERY lesson render — app/(platform)/app/lessons/[n]/page.tsx:54-56; the colleague missed this one), lib/server/studio.ts:230 (up to 300 paths), lib/server/bot/dal.ts:142 (24). Every Supabase token is a fresh JWT with a new `exp`, so every render yields a new URL. Supabase docs (guides/storage/cdn/smart-cdn, "Signed URLs and CDN caching") confirm verbatim: "Smart CDN treats each unique token as a separate cache key… If you generate a new signed URL on every request, the cache will never be warm and every request hits the origin." The same applies to the browser cache: upload sets `cacheControl: "3600"` (storage.ts:51) but a changed `?token=` is a cache miss, so the 1 h browser TTL is wasted. Amplifier the finding missed: components/platform/LessonTaskTools.tsx:100 `onSettled → router.refresh()` after EVERY job completes, re-running the whole `Lesson` server component (requireUser reads cookies → dynamic, no "use cache"), re-signing ~6×tasks + 60 reference files + motion library and swapping every `<img src>` to a new URL → all visible results re-download. Rendering: GenerateJobCard.tsx:22 `<img loading="lazy">` (only viewport images), video `preload="metadata"` (partial), so per-render egress is images in view plus video headers. Scenario 10 students / 75 min: ~2–6 refreshes from job completions + 3–6 navigations ≈ 10 lesson renders/student × (≈3 images × 1–2 MB + video metadata) ≈ 30–60 MB/student → 0.3–0.6 GB uncached egress per lesson, 20–30 `createSignedUrls` POSTs per student per lesson (2–3 per render; chunks ≤100 so one call per function) — the "Storage API burst" part is overstated, 10–30 POST/min total is nothing. Scenario 50: ≈1.5–3 GB per workshop. Plan evidence: docs/platform/setup.md:59 says the 50 MB bucket limit is "потолок бесплатного тарифа", i.e. production is assumed Free. Supabase egress docs: Free = 5 GB uncached + 5 GB cached per month; Smart CDN is Pro-only, so on Free ALL of this is uncached egress and the quota is gone within ~2 workshops of 50 (or ~8–10 lessons of 10). Exceeding Free quota → notification + grace period, then Fair Use restrictions (incl. 402 on ALL API requests, which would break the whole lesson, DB included). Not a same-day lesson breaker (grace period), and on Pro it is only cost (250 GB quota), so severity is medium rather than low: it directly threatens the "sold to other centers" scenario on Free and wastes money on Pro. Also note `useLiveJob` polling (/api/jobs/[id]) does NOT sign until status=done (job-progress.ts:212-214 returns [] otherwise), so polling is not an amplifier.
- **При 10:** ~10 Storage sign calls/min, egress ~0.5 GB per lesson. Fine on Pro, eats Free's 5 GB quota in ~10 lessons.
- **При 50:** Gallery/lesson re-renders by 50 students ≈ 50-100 sign calls/min plus full re-download of every thumbnail/video on each view; ≈2-3 GB egress per workshop ([research]) — fine on Pro, breaks Free within 2 workshops.
- **Как чиним (уточнённый план):** 1) Memoise signed URLs in lib/server/storage.ts: module-level `Map<path, { url: string; expiresAt: number }>` inside `signMediaPaths`; reuse an entry while `expiresAt - now > 15 min`, otherwise re-sign; cap the map (e.g. 5 000 entries, evict oldest) to bound memory per warm Vercel instance. Keep SIGNED_URL_TTL_SEC = 1 h. Do NOT use `unstable_cache` — in Next 16 with cacheComponents it is legacy, and the Next data cache would also serve stale URLs across deploys; a per-instance Map is enough because the goal is URL stability within a student's session (each warm instance gives the same URL for ≈45 min, so the browser cache with the existing `cacheControl: "3600"` finally hits, and on Pro the Smart CDN entry is reused). This is safe security-wise because every caller signs only after DAL/RLS filtering, and signed URLs are already bearer-token links by design. 2) Route the two bypasses through `signMediaPaths`: lib/server/studio.ts:230 and lib/server/bot/dal.ts:142 call `createSignedUrls` directly. For studio, `expiresAt` (studio.ts:224) must be derived from the cached entries' real expiry (min over returned entries), not `now + TTL`, or the studio will keep stale links past expiry. Optionally return `{url, expiresAt}` from signMediaPaths. 3) Reduce the lesson-page over-signing the finding missed: `listReferenceOptions` (gallery.ts:199-255) signs up to 60 files on every lesson render even though the reference picker shows them only when opened — either lazy-load it from a route/Server Action when the picker opens, or sign only the first N (e.g. 12) and lazily sign the rest. 4) The `router.refresh()` in components/platform/LessonTaskTools.tsx:100 is fine once URLs are stable (React keeps the same `src` → no re-download); alternatively skip the refresh when the completed job is already in `created` (the client already has the final JobView from the poll). 5) Doc note: record in docs/decisions.md that on the Free plan all Storage egress is uncached and 5 GB/month ≈ 8–10 lessons of 10 students; recommend Pro before a 50-student workshop regardless of this fix. Side effects: none functional; a cached URL may expire up to 15 min earlier than a freshly signed one would, which is still > any realistic page lifetime, and a hidden/deleted file is cut off by deletion (Supabase: deleting the object invalidates all tokens), not by token expiry, which is unchanged.
- **Сложность:** small

### GEN-02 · medium · Браузер опрашивает каждую карточку раз в 5 с без backoff, jitter и остановки на ошибках; один опрос = 2 вызова Vercel + 3 запроса PostgREST + 1 n8n + 1 kie recordInfo
- **Файлы:** components/platform/GenerateClient.ts:12, components/platform/GenerateClient.ts:50-59, components/platform/GenerateClient.ts:77-92, components/platform/GalleryGrid.tsx:51, components/platform/GenerateJobCard.tsx:130-132, lib/server/job-rules.ts:13, supabase/migrations/20261101200000_init.sql:617-626, proxy.ts:71, lib/server/dal/auth.ts:57-69
- **Вердикт скептика:** Код подтверждает все фактические утверждения находки. (1) components/platform/GenerateClient.ts:12,90 — фиксированный POLL_MS=5000, без backoff и jitter; первый тик для «queued» через 1,5 с (строка 92; GeneratePanel.tsx:203 ставит клиенту status "queued", так что это обычный путь). (2) GenerateClient.ts:53,56-58,82-90 — fetchJob возвращает null на любой не-OK ответ и на сетевую ошибку, а tick переназначает таймер в любом случае: опрос 401/404/5xx идёт бесконечно до размонтирования карточки (только скрытая вкладка пропускает тики). (3) Каждая карточка опрашивает сама: GalleryGrid.tsx:51, GenerateJobCard.tsx:131; страница галереи — до 24 карточек (lib/server/gallery.ts:25). (4) Сервер за один опрос: proxy.ts:71 matcher `/api/((?!tg/).*)` ловит /api/jobs → getClaims (с асимметричными ключами по setup.md:35 — локальная проверка, без похода в Auth, но это отдельный вызов функции); route.ts:18-23 → authorize() = getClaims + select profiles (auth.ts:57-69) + loadJob (job-progress.ts:62-71) + claim_job_poll RPC (job-progress.ts:130; init.sql:617-626) = 3 PostgREST (4 для ведущего: canViewJob job-progress.ts:79-84). (5) Клиентские 5 с + задержка сети ≥ серверные 5 с (job-rules.ts:13), поэтому claim проходит почти на каждом тике → почти каждый опрос = 1 выполнение n8n + 1 recordInfo kie (gateway.ts:252-255, таймаут 15 с). Единственное исключение — первый тик через 1,5 с после mark_job_submitted (last_polled_at = now(), init.sql:607-615) не проходит claim. Расчёты сценариев воспроизводятся: 10 учеников × 1 видео = 2 опроса/с (×2 вызова Vercel), 6 PostgREST/с, 2 n8n/с — терпимо, урок не ломается. 50 учеников — 10 опросов/с, 30 PostgREST/с, 10 n8n/с: Vercel Hobby (1 млн вызовов/мес) это переживает; главный риск — квота и параллелизм n8n Cloud и неизвестный лимит kie recordInfo (внешние цифры из находки — Starter 2 500 выполнений/мес, 5 параллельных — не проверяемы из репозитория, уверенность средняя). Замечание коллеги о синхронном старте 50 карточек через 1,5 с преувеличено: ученики жмут в разные секунды, синхронизация только внутри одной страницы. Коллега пропустил усилитель: общая галерея группы (listSharedGroupGallery при share_gallery и галерея ведущего listGroupGalleryForTeacher, gallery.ts) заставляет каждый открывший её браузер опрашивать все активные задачи группы на странице (до 24): при 50 учениках в общей галерее с 24 активными задачами = 1 200 карточек → 240 опросов/с и ~720 PostgREST/с к Nano — n8n при этом защищён claim_job_poll (≤ 24/5 с), но PostgREST/функции деградируют для всех. Это условный сценарий (по умолчанию открывается своя галерея), поэтому оставляю medium: урок при 10 не ломается, при 50 — деградация и риск сгорания квоты n8n, а не потеря денег/данных. Уверенность: по коду — высокая, по внешним лимитам — средняя.
- **При 10:** Пик: 10 видео одновременно → 120 опросов/мин = 2 запроса/с к Vercel (×2 с proxy = 4 вызова/с), 6 PostgREST/с, 2 n8n-запуска/с (~0,3 с каждый → <1 одновременного), 2 recordInfo/с. За урок (2 картинки ≈ 4 опроса, 2 видео ≈ 18 опросов, +queued/storing) ≈ 50 опросов на ученика → ≈ 1 000 вызовов Vercel и ≈ 450 n8n-запусков только на статусы. Терпимо.
- **При 50:** Пик: 50 видео → 600 опросов/мин = 10/с (20 вызовов Vercel/с), 30 PostgREST/с на Nano-инстансе Supabase (shared CPU [research]), 10 n8n-запусков/с ≈ 3 одновременных (Starter даёт 5 [research]), 10 recordInfo/с к kie (лимит для recordInfo не задокументирован [research]). Если ведущий сказал «все жмём» — 50 карточек запустят первый опрос через 1,5 с синхронно (GenerateClient.ts:92). За урок ≈ 2 500 статусов в n8n — Starter (2 500/мес) сгорает за один урок [research]. Ученик, у которого истекла сессия, продолжает слать 401 каждые 5 с до закрытия вкладки.
- **Как чиним (уточнённый план):** Порядок по соотношению польза/риск: (A) lib/server/job-rules.ts:13 — интервал claim по типу задачи: 5 с для image/voice/music, 10 с для video/motion/lipsync (SLOW_ACTIONS уже есть, job-rules.ts:19); передавать в claimJobPoll(job.id, intervalFor(job.action)) в job-progress.ts:130. Минус ~50% выполнений n8n и recordInfo для видео при задержке результата ≤ 10 с — безопасно, только сервер. (B) components/platform/GenerateClient.ts useLiveJob: интервал клиента согласовать с сервером и ввести backoff по возрасту задачи: 1,5 с первый тик (оставить), затем 5 с до 30 с жизни, 10 с до 2 мин, 15 с дальше, + jitter 0–1,5 с (Math.random()); для карточек video/motion/lipsync стартовать сразу с 10 с. Остановка: fetchJob должен различать ответы — на 401/403/404 остановить опрос после 2 подряд (это терминальные состояния: сессия/права/задача) и показать «обновите страницу»; на 5xx и сетевые ошибки НЕ останавливать (иначе кратковременный сбой шлюза «замораживает» карточку), а удваивать интервал до потолка 30 с и сбрасывать после первого успешного ответа. Это исправляет предложение коллеги «стоп после 3 не-OK», которое при 503/500 оставило бы ученика без результата до перезагрузки. (C) Пакетный опрос страницы: GET /api/jobs?ids=… (до 24 id, размер страницы галереи) с одним loadJob через .in("id", ids), одним authorize(), Promise.allSettled по advanceJob (один повисший getTaskStatus с таймаутом 15 с не должен задерживать остальные; maxDuration 60 хватает), ответ — частичный массив. На клиенте — провайдер/координатор на страницу (контекст вокруг GalleryGrid и LessonTaskTools), к которому карточки подписываются, вместо таймера в каждой useLiveJob. Это единственная мера, снимающая усилитель общей галереи (N учеников × M задач), и она же сокращает PostgREST с 3 на карточку до ~3 на страницу. Усилие среднее — делать после (A) и (B). (D) Исключение /api/jobs и /api/generate из matcher proxy.ts:71 допустимо и безопасно: маршруты сами вызывают authorize() (spec §8), а lib/server/supabase/server.ts:23-31 пишет обновлённые cookie в Route Handler (cookies().set там работает), так что обновление сессии при истечении токена в середине 75-минутного урока сохранится. Но выигрыш — только один вызов функции на опрос (главные затраты — n8n/PostgREST), поэтому ставить последним пунктом; при этом не трогать matcher для /app, /teach, /admin, /login. Проверка после внедрения: прогнать supabase/tests для claim_job_poll с новым интервалом и ручной тест «сессия истекает во время опроса» без proxy на /api/jobs.
- **Сложность:** medium

### GEN-03 · medium · Задачу, которую никто не опрашивает, никто не продвигает: закрытая вкладка/ушедший ученик = задача «submitted» навсегда, звёзды списаны, результат kie (14 дней) не скопирован, а зависшие задачи занимают лимит 5
- **Файлы:** lib/server/job-progress.ts:117-148, lib/server/gallery.ts:47-68, lib/server/gallery.ts:105-142, lib/server/lesson-progress.ts:35-43, lib/server/lesson-progress.ts:17, supabase/migrations/20261101200000_init.sql:571-573, supabase/migrations/20261101200000_init.sql:148, lib/server/job-rules.ts:14-16
- **Вердикт скептика:** Подтверждено кодом. advanceJob вызывается только из GET /api/jobs/[id] (app/api/jobs/[id]/route.ts:22; job-progress.ts:117), а запрос туда делает только useLiveJob в браузере (GenerateClient.ts:71-97; GalleryGrid.tsx:51), и только пока вкладка видима (GenerateClient.ts:79). Фонового обхода нет ни в одном маршруте/Server Action (grep по advanceJob/claim_job_poll). create_job считает все queued/submitted/storing без ограничения по возрасту (init.sql:571-573). Ссылки kie живут ~14 дней (docs/kie-gateway.md:20, spec.md:61). Галерея ведущего действительно опрашивает чужие задачи (canViewJob job-progress.ts:74-87), но только 24 карточки страницы (gallery.ts:25,126).

Уточнения к сценариям: (1) В сценарии 10 возврат происходит не «при первом же опросе»: после 14+ дней первый опрос даёт submitted→success→begin_job_storing→after(storeJobResult)→ kie 403/404 → `!response.ok` → download_failed (gateway.ts:346) → задача остаётся storing (job-progress.ts:175-177). Возврат (isStoringStale && isPastHardLimit, job-progress.ts:136-138) случится только на втором опросе ≥3 мин спустя; если ученик закрыл страницу раньше — снова «заморозка». (2) Потеря самого ролика требует, чтобы ни ученик (галерея — первые 24 медиа-задачи любого статуса, gallery.ts:56-58; урок — 6 на задание), ни ведущий не открыли нужную страницу 14 дней. При еженедельных уроках типичный исход — не потеря денег, а 25–140★ «заморожены» неделю и задача дозавершается при следующем визите. (3) Путь к too_many_jobs через медиа-задачи маловероятен (нужно 5 orphan-медиа не на текущем уроке и не в 24 новейших галереи); реальный путь — текстовые задачи (action 'text' не попадает в галерею, MEDIA_ACTIONS gallery.ts:32; в уроке 6 на задание), что пересекается с находкой о зависших queued-чатах (findings.md:148). Severity medium сохраняется: урок не ломается, деньги теряются редко, но платформа «лицо владельца» и ученик видит пропавшие звёзды.

Проблемы в предложенном фиксе: (B) как написано не работает: settings.key проверяется regex `^[a-z_]{2,40}$` (init.sql:108) — ключ 'sweep_'||uuid нарушит check; value — jsonb (init.sql:109), `value::timestamptz` без `->>` не скастуется. (A) after() в Server Component: у страниц /app, /app/gallery, уроков нет maxDuration (есть только у bot и teach/groups/[id]); на legacy non-Fluid Hobby дефолт 10 с (research.md:97) — скачивание видео в after() будет убито, задача снова в storing. Внешний пинг (C): proxy.ts пропускает /api/* без cookie (proxy.ts:34-36), маршрут достижим — ок; но GitHub Actions schedule автоматически отключается после 60 дней без активности в репозитории и может задерживаться — для курса с редкими коммитами ненадёжно. Колега пропустил самый дешёвый слой: Vercel Cron на Hobby доступен раз в сутки с CRON_SECRET (docs/research/platform-security.md:758) — этого достаточно, чтобы гарантированно закрыть потерю результата до истечения 14 дней. Новый индекс не нужен: активных строк единицы-десятки, частичный jobs_open_idx (init.sql:148) уже покрывает предикат по статусу. Эвристика «download_failed на задаче старше 14 дней» — гадание о TTL kie; правильнее различать 4xx (навсегда) и сеть/5xx (повтор) в downloadResult.
- **При 10:** Ученик запустил видео за 2 минуты до конца урока и закрыл ноутбук. Задача висит «submitted» до следующего открытия галереи/урока (неделя). Если он откроет её через 14+ дней — ссылка kie протухла → storeJobResult: download_failed → задача остаётся storing → через 3 мин перехват → снова download_failed… до 60 мин с момента отправки, потом refund result_unavailable — но «60 минут» давно прошли, так что при первом же опросе isPastHardLimit → возврат. Итог: ролик потерян, кредиты kie потрачены, 25★ вернулись через неделю.
- **При 50:** В конце 75-минутного воркшопа 50 человек уходят; 10–20 видео ещё генерируются → 10–20 orphan-задач (≈ 19 кредитов kie каждая, $1–2 потерь на урок) и 250–500★ «замороженных». На следующем уроке у кого-то 5 старых активных задач с разных уроков/страниц галереи → «Уже идёт несколько генераций. Дождитесь результата» (too_many_jobs), и пока он не откроет именно те страницы, где эти карточки опрашиваются (24 в галерее, 6 на задание), генерировать нельзя.
- **Как чиним (уточнённый план):** 1) Личный слой без нового серверного кода: на /app (кабинет), /app/gallery и странице урока в Server Component выбрать активные задачи пользователя (`select JOB_COLUMNS from jobs where user_id=$1 and status in ('queued','submitted','storing') and action<>'montage' order by created_at limit 5` — jobs_open_idx) и отрендерить клиентский блок «В работе с прошлого раза» на useLiveJob (GenerateClient.ts:71) — каждая карточка сама дергает GET /api/jobs/[id] (maxDuration 60, замки claim_job_poll/begin_job_storing уже есть). Ученик заодно видит, что ролик с прошлого урока готов. 2) Групповой слой: на /teach/groups/[id] (уже maxDuration 60) такой же блок «Генерации в работе» по группе (`group_id=$1 and status in (...) order by created_at limit 10`), mode="teacher" — canViewJob пропускает ведущего (job-progress.ts:74-87). Пока ведущий в классе с открытой вкладкой, он подметает все зависшие задачи группы; никакого throttle через settings не нужно. 3) Внешний слой: `POST /api/jobs/sweep` (вне proxy он доступен без cookie — proxy.ts:34-36), проверка `Authorization: Bearer $CRON_SECRET` через timingSafeEqual, maxDuration 60; берёт ≤5 самых старых активных задач с `coalesce(last_polled_at, created_at) < now() - interval '60 seconds'` и вызывает advanceJob по очереди (storeJobResult идёт в after(), 5 задач укладываются в минуту). Расписание: vercel.json crons раз в сутки (Hobby разрешает, CRON_SECRET присылает сам Vercel) — закрывает потерю результата до 14 дней; при желании добавить pg_cron + pg_net каждые 10 мин с секретом в Vault — закрывает «замороженные» звёзды. GitHub Actions не использовать (отключается через 60 дней без коммитов). 4) В downloadResult (gateway.ts:346) различать ответы: 4xx → `result_unavailable` (ссылка протухла/удалена — немедленный refund в storeJobResult), сеть/5xx → `download_failed` (повтор через 3 мин, как сейчас). 5) В create_job (init.sql:571) перед подсчётом вызывать `refund_job(id,'gateway_lost')` для queued без gateway_task_id старше 3 мин и `refund_job(id,'timeout')` для submitted/storing старше 60 мин этого пользователя (блокировки wallets/jobs реентерабельны в одной транзакции), затем считать активные — вместо отсечки «моложе 65 мин», которая прячет симптом. Новый индекс не нужен. Покрыть pgTAP-тестом в 002_wallet.test.sql: 5 старых queued не блокируют create_job и возвращают звёзды.
- **Сложность:** medium

### GEN-04 · medium · n8n-таймаут на KIE POST (40 с) возвращает платформе 500 «Error in workflow» — она считает это обычной ошибкой (не uncertain) и возвращает звёзды, хотя kie мог создать задачу; при платформенном таймауте (uncertain) результат теряется для владельца
- **Файлы:** lib/server/gateway.ts:410-418, lib/server/gateway.ts:428-433, lib/server/gateway.ts:437-440, lib/server/generate.ts:328-343, lib/server/job-rules.ts:14, lib/server/job-progress.ts:125-126
- **Вердикт скептика:** Mechanism CONFIRMED against the live n8n workflow v21nopocrCSYPBGd (read via MCP) and the code. n8n side: node «KIE POST» has options.timeout=40000 and response.neverError=true but no onError setting (default = stop workflow), the webhook «Receive Request» is responseMode=responseNode, and workflow settings have no executionTimeout. neverError only suppresses HTTP error statuses; a socket timeout (or a non-JSON body with responseFormat=json) throws, the run fails before «Send Response», and n8n answers the webhook itself with HTTP 500 and a JSON body of the form {"code":0,"message":"Error in workflow"} (no `ok` field). Platform side: lib/server/gateway.ts:420-434 parses that JSON fine, createTask (gateway.ts:246) sees body.ok!==true → failure(body,500) (gateway.ts:437-440) → {error:"gateway_bad_response" (body has `message`, not `error`), httpStatus:500} with NO uncertain flag; isTransientFailure returns true (status>=500, gateway.ts:195) but withRetries skips the retry only because 45_000-~40_300-1_500 < 10_000 (gateway.ts:212-213); submitTask then hits refundJob (generate.ts:341). So the documented guarantee in gateway.ts:167-168 and docs/kie-gateway.md:27 («платформа ждёт дольше, чтобы не вернуть звёзды за задачу, которую kie всё-таки создал») is defeated: the gateway's 40 s timeout arrives as a definite error, not an uncertain one, and stars are refunded while kie may have enqueued the task. By contrast kie's own fast failures come normalized as {ok:false,httpStatus:502,error,kieCode} («Normalize Response» node), so the two cases are distinguishable by the presence of `ok`.

Corrections to the finding: (1) Frequency is speculative, not evidenced: search of executions for this workflow with status error/crashed/canceled returns 0 since 2026-11-01 (253+ runs), the longest observed run is 22 s, and kie overload (5 Nov) is documented as fast 502/503 «Resources are tight», not 40 s hangs. So «1–2 из 10 видео» at 10 users and «15–30 orphan» at 50 have no data behind them; the realistic rate today is near zero, cost per incident ≈19 kie credits (~$0.10) of the owner's money, student unaffected. (2) The second half (platform-side uncertain at 45 s → taskId lost) is in practice NOT reached via a kie hang, because n8n's 40 s error returns first; it is reached when n8n itself is slow — e.g. execution queueing at the n8n Cloud concurrency limit at 50 simultaneous students (ties to GEN-02). In that case the run completes later, kie creates the task, and nobody learns the taskId → owner's credits lost; this makes the «lost taskId» half more plausible at 50 than at 10. (3) Proposed fix #2 as written is risky: an n8n-level 500 is also produced by a fast non-JSON kie response (HTML 502 page, parse error) and by network errors — cases that are safe and desirable to retry. Treating every 5xx-without-kieCode as uncertain would disable retries there and park students for QUEUED_WITHOUT_TASK_LIMIT_MS (3 min) before refund. (4) Fix #4 (90 s) is safe: mark_job_submitted is guarded by status='queued' (init.sql:606-614) and refund_job by status in (queued,submitted,storing), and app/api/generate/route.ts has maxDuration=60, so no submit can happen after created_at+60 s; 90 s leaves margin. Severity kept at medium (it breaks an explicit design guarantee and scales with students), with the caveat that the money at risk is small, owner-side, and unobserved so far. Confidence: high on mechanism, low on frequency.
- **При 10:** В окно перегрузки kie (как 5 ноября) 1–2 создания видео из 10 упираются в 40 с → ученик видит «перегружен», звёзды вернулись, но у kie реально рендерится ролик за 19 кредитов, который никто не заберёт. Деньги владельца, не ученика; ≈ $0,1–0,2 на инцидент.
- **При 50:** Тот же процент (10–20%) от 150 видео за воркшоп → 15–30 orphan-роликов ≈ 300–600 кредитов ($1,5–3) + 15–30 учеников ждут 45 с ради ошибки. Если когда-нибудь RETRY_BUDGET_MS.create поднимут (например, с maxDuration 300 на Fluid), гарантия «нет ретрая» исчезнет и начнутся двойные задачи у kie.
- **Как чиним (уточнённый план):** 1) n8n (primary, no platform deploy needed): on «KIE POST» set Settings → On Error = «Continue (using error output)»; wire the error output to a small Code node that emits, for route 'create' from «Доработка запроса», {ok:false, uncertain:true, httpStatus:504, error:'kie timeout: '+message} and for text/status/balance {ok:false, httpStatus:502, error:message}; connect it to «Send Response». Also set workflow Settings → Timeout Workflow ≈ 55 s so a hung run frees its concurrency slot. Optionally do the same for «KIE GET» (status) — not uncertain, just a normalized 502.
2) lib/server/gateway.ts (defensive, in createTask/post): mark uncertain when createsTask and (a) body.uncertain === true, or (b) the body has no `ok` field at all (an n8n-level error, not a «Normalize Response» body) AND the attempt took ≥ 30 s (kie POST timeout is 40 s; a fast n8n-level 500 from a non-JSON kie reply or network blip must stay transient and retryable). Do not key on the absence of kieCode alone — Normalize always sets `ok`, so `ok` presence is the reliable discriminator. Keep isTransientFailure returning false for uncertain (already true).
3) Recover lost taskIds instead of refunding blindly: platform passes jobId in the create body (cheap, now); in n8n, after a successful create, write {jobId, taskId, createdAt} to an n8n Data Table (one node, no extra execution) and add action:'lookup' {jobId} → {ok, taskId|null}. In advanceJob (job-progress.ts, case 'queued'), when the job is queued without task for > 60 s, gate with claimJobPoll and call lookup once: on hit → markJobSubmitted and continue polling; on miss → refund gateway_lost. Alternative with the same effect and synergy with GEN-02: pass kie callBackUrl = /api/jobs/callback?job=<id>&sig=<HMAC>; the callback handler attaches taskId to a still-queued job and stores the result, so orphans deliver even without lookup.
4) job-rules.ts:14 QUEUED_WITHOUT_TASK_LIMIT_MS: 3 min → 90 s (maxDuration 60 + margin; mark_job_submitted is status-guarded so no race). With fix 3, lookup at 60–90 s precedes the refund.
5) Add a supabase/tests or vitest case: createTask receiving HTTP 500 with body {"code":0,"message":"Error in workflow"} after 40 s → result.uncertain === true and no refund; same body after 1 s → transient, retried.
- **Сложность:** medium

### GEN-05 · medium · Сохранение результата в after() ограничено остатком maxDuration=60 маршрута опроса; fetch kie ждёт до 60 с, загрузка в Storage без таймаута, файл буферизуется целиком
- **Файлы:** app/api/jobs/[id]/route.ts:14, lib/server/job-progress.ts:158, lib/server/job-progress.ts:167-208, lib/server/gateway.ts:336, lib/server/gateway.ts:350-368, lib/server/storage.ts:48-57, lib/server/job-rules.ts:15, supabase/migrations/20261101200000_init.sql:629-637
- **Вердикт скептика:** Подтверждено по коду (confidence high на механику, medium на частоту срабатывания).

Что верно:
- app/api/jobs/[id]/route.ts:14 — maxDuration = 60; node_modules/next/dist/docs/01-app/03-api-reference/04-functions/after.md («Duration», строки 48-50 и 250) — after() живёт не дольше maxDuration маршрута, на Vercel через waitUntil. Значит storeJobResult (job-progress.ts:158, 167-208) обрывается вместе с функцией.
- Бюджет до старта after(): authorize + loadJob + canViewJob + claimJobPoll + getTaskStatus + beginJobStoring. getTaskStatus (gateway.ts:255) идёт через post() без withRetries с таймаутом 15 с (gateway.ts:169). Поправка к находке: «~40–45 с остатка» — это худший случай; в типичном случае статус отвечает за 1–3 с и остаётся ~55 с.
- downloadResult (gateway.ts:333-336): AbortSignal.timeout(60_000) на КАЖДЫЙ из до 3 хопов редиректа — любой из них длиннее остатка функции. Файл собирается целиком: chunks[] + вторая копия в bytes (gateway.ts:350-368) → пик ≈ 2× размера файла на одно скачивание, лимит 100 МБ (gateway.ts:288).
- uploadMedia (storage.ts:48-51): без таймаута; storage-js 2.118 upload() не принимает signal (только через кастомный fetch клиента).
- Несколько URL качаются последовательно (job-progress.ts:170).
- Обрыв → задача остаётся storing (нет refund, нет совершения). Перехват: begin_job_storing требует updated_at старше 3 минут (init.sql:629-637; updated_at ставит триггер touch_updated_at, init.sql:271-283) и чей-то опрос. Браузер опрашивает раз в 5 с, пока статус активен, но пропускает тики в фоновой вкладке (components/platform/GenerateClient.ts:12, 62-92); карточка всё это время показывает «Сохраняем в галерею…» (GenerateJobCard.tsx:92). Перехват = ещё один вызов статуса в n8n (job-progress.ts:141). Деньги не теряются: либо второй заход дотягивает, либо через 60 мин refund result_unavailable (job-progress.ts:137-138).

Сценарий 10: видео 5–15 МБ с CDN kie → Vercel → Supabase EU обычно 2–10 с, укладывается. Обрыв только при медленном/зависшем CDN kie; цена — один ученик 3 минуты смотрит на спиннер, ошибок не видит. Это «degraded», medium обоснован (платформа — лицо владельца), но не high.

Сценарий 50: оценка «750 МБ в одном инстансе» завышена: нужно, чтобы 50 скачиваний шли одновременно (окно 2–10 с), а при 1–3 видео на ученика за 75 минут финиши размазаны; Fluid масштабирует инстансы. Зато из-за двойной копии пик на файл 2×. Реальный риск при 50 — тот же per-job обрыв, доля растёт линейно; память — low.

Что в находке неточно/рискованно:
- Fix 4 (STORING_RECLAIM_AFTER_MS → 90 с) в формулировке меняет только TS-константу; SQL-интервал в begin_job_storing (init.sql:634) и bot/card.ts:181 тоже 3 минуты — менять надо синхронно миграцией, иначе isStoringStale и SQL разъедутся.
- Fix 2 (стриминг в Storage): storage-js 2.118 действительно принимает ReadableStream и сам ставит duplex "half" (dist/index.cjs:629), но: путь файла зависит от ext, который берётся из сниффинга первых байт (job-progress.ts:182, mediaPath) — нужно сначала прочитать первый чанк, потом пере-обернуть поток; лимит 100 МБ придётся считать в TransformStream и абортить; поведение Storage API с chunked-телом без Content-Length (проверка bucket file_size_limit) не проверено. Выигрыш — только при массовом наложении after(), которого при 10–50 учениках почти нет. Рекомендую отложить.
- Fix 3 (maxDuration 120–300) работает только при включённом Fluid Compute (на Hobby без Fluid максимум 60 с). В репо нет vercel.json/настроек — unresolved, проверять в Vercel → Settings → Functions.
- **При 10:** 10 видео Seedance Mini (~5–15 МБ) финишируют одновременно — 10 независимых after() в разных вызовах, по 3–10 с каждый: укладываются. Риск только при медленном CDN kie: функция убита на 60 с → карточка висит «Сохраняем в галерею…» 3 минуты, потом второй опрос дотягивает. Ученик теряет ~3 мин.
- **При 50:** 50 финишей в одну минуту: Fluid-инстанс обслуживает несколько after() параллельно, 50 × 15 МБ = 750 МБ в памяти одного инстанса в худшем случае (лимит 2 ГБ [research]) — впритык, но проходит. Доля обрывов на медленном CDN растёт пропорционально; каждая такая задача = +3 мин ожидания и +1 n8n-запуск при повторной проверке статуса (job-progress.ts:141).
- **Как чиним (уточнённый план):** 1) Бюджет времени (главное, малый риск). В GET /api/jobs/[id] зафиксировать deadline = Date.now() + 55_000 и пробросить в advanceJob → applyDecision → storeJobResult(job, urls, credits, deadline). В downloadResult добавить параметр signal/deadline: на каждый хоп `AbortSignal.timeout(Math.max(3_000, deadline - Date.now()))` вместо фиксированных 60_000 (gateway.ts:336); для bot/card.ts оставить дефолт. Для uploadMedia — отдельный storage-клиент с кастомным fetch: `createClient(url, key, { global: { fetch: (i, init) => fetch(i, { ...init, signal: AbortSignal.any([init?.signal, AbortSignal.timeout(left)].filter(Boolean)) }) } })`, где left = deadline - Date.now(). При аборте код уже корректно оставляет задачу в storing (download_failed → return; upload_failed → return).
2) Перехват быстрее: раз любая попытка гарантированно умирает ≤ 60 с после begin_job_storing, снизить интервал до 90 с СИНХРОННО: новая миграция `create or replace function public.begin_job_storing` с `interval '90 seconds'`, STORING_RECLAIM_AFTER_MS = 90_000 в job-rules.ts:15, комментарий в bot/card.ts:180 и строка в spec.md:76. Добавить SQL-тест на перехват через 90 с.
3) Проверить в Vercel, включён ли Fluid Compute; если да — поднять maxDuration у app/api/jobs/[id]/route.ts до 120 (только этот маршрут; остальные таймауты шлюза рассчитаны на 60 и не трогаются) и соответственно deadline = 110 с. Если Fluid выключен — включить (бесплатно на Hobby, даёт 300 с и переиспользование инстанса).
4) Память: не стримить сейчас. Дешёвый шаг — убрать вторую копию в downloadResult (возвращать Buffer.concat(chunks) или считать размер заранее по content-length) и ввести лимит по типу действия (image ≤ 20 МБ, audio ≤ 30 МБ, video ≤ 100 МБ) вместо единого 100 МБ. Стриминг (storage-js принимает ReadableStream, duplex выставляет сам) — отдельная задача после проверки поведения Storage API с chunked-телом и с учётом того, что ext/путь зависят от сниффинга первых байт.
5) Опционально (архитектурно): перенести копирование результата в n8n (шлюз уже знает URL результата и не ограничен 60 с) — загрузка в Storage по signed upload URL, а Vercel лишь переводит задачу в done. Снимает проблему целиком, но это изменение контракта шлюза (docs/kie-gateway.md).
- **Сложность:** medium

### GEN-06 · medium · При 5xx/504 от /api/generate клиент сбрасывает idempotencyKey — повторный клик создаёт вторую задачу и второе списание, пока первая висит queued до возврата через 3 мин
- **Файлы:** components/platform/GeneratePanel.tsx:109, components/platform/GeneratePanel.tsx:129, components/platform/GeneratePanel.tsx:178-185, components/platform/GenerateClient.ts:34-41, app/api/generate/route.ts:14, lib/server/gateway.ts:187, lib/server/generate.ts:307-326, lib/server/job-rules.ts:14
- **Вердикт скептика:** Confirmed from code, with one correction to the fix. (1) Client side: components/platform/GeneratePanel.tsx:182 keeps keyRef only when result.network; GenerateClient.ts:31-41 sets network:true only when fetch() throws. A Vercel timeout is an HTTP response with a non-JSON body → response.json() fails → data=null → {ok:false, error:'server_error'} without network → key is dropped → the next click sends a fresh idempotencyKey → create_job (supabase/migrations/20261101200000_init.sql:551-596) inserts a second job and debits again. (2) Server side: the debit happens at generate.ts step 4 (createJobWithDebit) before the gateway call (step 5), so a function killed during runText leaves a queued text job with no gateway_task_id. Text jobs never get a task id, so the only exit is advanceJob 'queued' branch (job-progress.ts:125-126) → isQueuedWithoutTaskTooLong after QUEUED_WITHOUT_TASK_LIMIT_MS = 3 min (job-rules.ts:14), and advanceJob runs only from GET /api/jobs/[id], i.e. only when a card for that job is mounted (LessonTaskTools.tsx:98 after reload via getLessonState lesson-progress.ts:35-43). So stars stay frozen until the student reloads the lesson and 3 minutes have passed. (3) Is a 60 s kill plausible? RETRY_BUDGET_MS.text = TIMEOUT_MS.text = 50 000 (gateway.ts:169,187); withRetries (gateway.ts:203-218) keeps retrying while ≥10 s of the budget remains, so in a kie overload window the gateway stage routinely consumes 45–50 s by design. That leaves ~10 s for authorize() (Supabase Auth), getPriceList (cached, TTL), create_job RPC, then refund/complete RPC + loadJob + toJobView. Normally 1–3 s, so the margin is ~7–9 s; exceeding it needs a Supabase/Auth stall of several seconds in the same request. Rare at 10 users; at 50 users in a kie overload window (all requests sitting at the 50 s ceiling while 50 browsers also poll /api/jobs every 5 s) it is credible but I could not quantify it — vercel.com is not reachable from this session, so I could not verify whether maxDuration counts cold start or the exact 504 body; the body is in any case not our {error, jobId} JSON. Severity stays medium: no permanent money loss (auto-refund), lesson continues, but affected students see an opaque error, a double debit and frozen stars until reload. Confidence medium. Fix correction: proposal 1 as written (“keep key on any 5xx”) is wrong. 502 gateway_error from generate.ts:319-320 and 340-342 is returned AFTER refundJob; create_job's replay (init.sql:551) returns the existing job regardless of status, so a retry with the kept key would hit replayOutcome (generate.ts:294-298) → 'accepted, reused' → the client would render a queued card that polls into 'failed' with no new generation and no debit: the retry becomes a no-op. Same problem for 503 not_configured and the generic 500 from route.ts:53. Keep the key only for the infra case (5xx with no JSON body) and make replay of a dead/failed job return an error.
- **При 10:** Редко (нужен обрыв на 60 с). Ученик видит «Что-то пошло не так. Попробуйте ещё раз», жмёт снова — второе списание 1★; первое вернётся через 3 мин после перезагрузки страницы. Запутанно, не критично.
- **При 50:** В окно перегрузки kie (ретраи до 50 с у всех) + очередь n8n вероятность 60-секундного обрыва растёт; десяток учеников получают server_error, у каждого по 2 списания и «замороженные» звёзды до следующего открытия урока. Ведущему придётся объяснять.
- **Как чиним (уточнённый план):** 1) GenerateClient.ts postGenerate: when !response.ok and data === null and response.status >= 500, return { ok:false, error:'server_error', infra:true } (no JSON = platform/Vercel layer answered, not our route). GeneratePanel.tsx:182: `if (!result.network && !result.infra) keyRef.current = null;`. Do NOT keep the key for 5xx that carry our JSON (502 gateway_error is already refunded; a replay would return the failed job). 2) generate.ts replayOutcome: if job.status === 'failed' → return { kind:'error', status:502, error:'gateway_error', jobId } so the client drops the key and a new click creates a fresh job; if job.action === 'text' && job.status === 'queued' && now - createdAt > ~65 s (the function that owned it is certainly dead) → await refundJob(jobId, refundReason('gateway_lost')) and return the same 502 gateway_error — this returns the stars immediately on the retry instead of after a reload + 3 min. 3) gateway.ts: lower RETRY_BUDGET_MS.text and TIMEOUT_MS.text to ~40 000–42 000 ms (n8n already cuts KIE POST at 40 s, docs/kie-gateway.md:27; text failures are never `uncertain` (gateway.ts:416, createsTask=false) so an earlier timeout only means an earlier refund, no double-charge risk). Leave `create` at 45 s (15 s margin). Update the comment at gateway.ts:186 and docs/kie-gateway.md:27. 4) Message for the infra case only: “Не удалось отправить. Если звёзды списались, они вернутся через несколько минут.” (map 'server_error'+infra to a dedicated code, e.g. 'request_lost', in lib/platform/generation-messages.ts). 5) Optional: in lesson-progress/GenerateJobCard also mount queued text jobs so the 3-min refund path is polled without the student having to retry.
- **Сложность:** small

### SEC-03 · medium · Правильные ответы тестов (correctIndex), план урока и заметки ведущего лежат в публичном GitHub-репозитории
- **Файлы:** docs/course/lesson-1.json, docs/course/lesson-2.json, docs/course/lesson-3.json, docs/course/lesson-4.json, docs/course/lesson-5.json, lib/server/course.ts:3-7, lib/server/course.ts:78, lib/server/course.ts:196-200, supabase/migrations/20261105100000_security_hardening.sql:17-21, supabase/migrations/20261101200000_init.sql:779-823
- **Вердикт скептика:** Confirmed, not refuted. `gh api repos/simonspivak/workshop` returns `{"private":false,"visibility":"public"}` and docs/platform/spec.md:10 / setup.md:5 state the repo is public by design. All five docs/course/lesson-N.json contain `quiz[].correctIndex` + `explanation` (10 questions each), plus `timeline[].teacherNotes` (10–11 blocks) and `materials` (8–9 items) that spec §4 (spec.md:89) marks «только ведущему». lib/server/course.ts:3-7 imports the JSON directly, lessonSchema (course.ts:78) requires correctIndex, getQuizKey (course.ts:196-200) feeds gradeQuiz → submitQuiz (app/(platform)/app/lessons/[n]/actions.ts:42-51). The hardening migration (20261105100000_security_hardening.sql:17-21) itself acknowledges «ответы лежат в публичном репозитории». Bonus is +20★ once per lesson (quiz.ts:9, record_quiz_attempt in init.sql:779-823: one row per user/lesson, bonus capped at 100). The colleague's finding is actually UNDER-stated on exposure: every docs/course/lesson-N.md also has a «## Ключ к тесту» table with the correct letters (lesson-1.md:88, lesson-2.md:99, lesson-3.md:72, lesson-4.md:72, lesson-5.md:97), so a student does not even need to parse JSON. Money math checks out: seedance 25★ = 19 kie credits ≈ $0.095 → 1★ ≈ $0.004; 100★/student ≈ $0.40, 50 students ≈ $20 — this is bounded by the one-attempt rule and the 100-cap, and lesson-1.md:86 says the bonus is «сверху, в расчёт не закладываем», so no lesson breaks and no uncontrolled spend. Severity medium is right: the quiz as an assessment (teacher report lib/server/group-reports.ts:71-116) is devalued, course IP the owner intends to sell is freely downloadable, but there is no data breach and bounded cost. The spec's own guarantee «correctIndex никогда не уходит в браузер» (spec.md:106) is technically kept yet meaningless while the key is one Google search away. Confidence: high (facts verified in repo and via GitHub API).
- **При 10:** Один ученик гуглит «ИИ-старт github», открывает lesson-1.json и сдаёт 10/10: +20★ × 5 уроков = 100★ ≈ 80 кредитов ≈ $0,4 на человека; отчёт учителя по тестам теряет смысл.
- **При 50:** Ссылка расходится по чату группы — 50 × 100★ = 5 000★ лишних (≈ $20 в кредитах) плюс полностью обесцененная проверка знаний; конкуренты-центры скачивают готовый курс, уроки и промпты бесплатно.
- **Как чиним (уточнённый план):** 1) Make github.com/simonspivak/workshop private — the only fix that also covers lesson-*.md «Ключ к тесту» tables, teacherNotes, materials and the course text itself. Vercel (Hobby included) and the Supabase GitHub integration (setup.md:76-92) both work through the GitHub App on private repos; nothing in code changes. Update spec.md:10 and setup.md:5 («Репозиторий публичный» → приватный; the «no secrets in git» rule stays).
