README.md:17/41 instructions (edit files/upload photo on GitHub) still work for the owner. 2) If the repo must stay public, the colleague's proposal is incomplete and has side effects: (a) also delete the «## Ключ к тесту» sections from all five docs/course/lesson-N.md and strip timeline/teacherNotes/materials; (b) move `correctIndex`+`explanation` to a source outside git — prefer a `private.quiz_keys(lesson_id text, keys jsonb)` table read by service_role in getQuizKey (one extra RPC per submitQuiz, negligible: ≤50 submits/lesson) over a Vercel env var (fine for size, but loses the build-time refine `correctIndex < options.length` unless re-validated at load, and makes local/Preview runs fail without the var — keep mock keys for mock mode); (c) scripts/check-lessons.mjs currently validates the quiz and the brief (770-brief.md:107) requires the answer not be guessable by option length — that check needs the key, so give the script an optional local key file (gitignored) instead of simply asserting «no correctIndex». 3) Independently of 1/2, keep the existing mitigations that bound the damage: one attempt per lesson and bonus ≤100 in record_quiz_attempt, self-signup disabled (770-status.md:14), teacher sees per-student quiz rows in the group report.
- **Сложность:** small

### SEC-04 · medium · Нет удаления данных: заявки гостей, переписка ботов, медиа и аккаунты живут вечно; раздел о платформе в политике конфиденциальности не написан
- **Файлы:** lib/server/bot/store.ts:299-306, lib/server/bot/store.ts:351-364, lib/server/bot/lifetime.ts:26-43, lib/server/bot/telegram-flow.ts:218-231, lib/server/studio.ts:297-323, content/privacy.ts:69, app/b/layout.tsx:22, docs/decisions.md:93-97, app/(platform)/teach/actions.ts:52-146
- **Вердикт скептика:** Суть подтверждена кодом, опровергнуть нельзя; часть ссылок и деталей исправления неверна.

Подтверждено:
- Единственные удаления в коде: lib/server/bot/store.ts:299-306 (deleteConversation — только превью владельца), lib/server/studio.ts:297-323 (брошенные mp4 монтажа), lib/server/lesson-progress.ts:94 (снятие галочки шага). grep по `.delete()` и `storage...remove` других мест не находит.
- bot_leads только вставляются/помечаются (store.ts:351-371: insertLead, markLeadNotified); чтение в lib/server/bot/dal.ts:111,170 — удаления нет. Поле «Контакт» у взрослых владельцев реально сохраняется (lib/server/bot/leads.ts:114-129, dal.ts:225-226), публичная страница /b/[slug] ведёт на /privacy (app/b/layout.tsx:22), а content/privacy.ts:69 — заглушка «когда заработает платформа…».
- Истечение срока бота не удаляет данные: lib/server/bot/lifetime.ts:26-43 только вычисляет retired; brain.ts:112 и dal.ts:211 показывают «бот переехал»; telegram-flow.ts:64,96-104 снимает вебхук и пишет audit. Цитата «telegram-flow.ts:218-231» неверна — файл 107 строк.
- В app/(platform)/teach/actions.ts есть только createGroup, updateGroup, setGroupPaused, createStudents, resetStudentPassword, updateStudentAge, grantStudentStars — ни «закрыть группу», ни отключения ученика. `profiles.disabled` (init.sql:52) проверяется при входе (login/actions.ts:57-58, dal/auth.ts:75, брейн бота brain.ts:181) и показывается бейджем (teach/groups/[id]/StudentList.tsx:45), но ни одно действие его не ставит. Админ-раздела в app/(platform) нет вовсе.
- decisions.md §7.3 «удалять через 30 дней после вечера» — строки 108-110 (не 93-97), статус «открытый вопрос», в коде не реализовано. pg_cron/pg_net/vercel cron в репозитории не упоминаются.

Что в находке неточно (смягчает, но не снимает):
1. База уже умеет каскад: profiles→auth.users on delete cascade (init.sql:44), jobs/wallets/star_ledger/bots cascade от profiles (init.sql:82,117,153,200), bot_messages/bot_leads cascade от bots (init.sql:228,246); триггер ledger_append_only допускает удаление каскадом (init.sql:287-289), это покрыто тестом supabase/tests/002_wallet.test.sql:212-214. Т.е. запрос «удалите мои данные» ученика выполняется одним удалением пользователя в Supabase Dashboard, а не «ручным SQL по таблицам». Но: (а) объекты Storage media/{user_id}/* при этом остаются сиротами — триггера на storage нет; (б) для гостя бота (заявка внутри чужого бота) удалять владельца-ученика нельзя, а точечного удаления заявки нет; (в) инструмента в продукте нет — только доступ к Dashboard у владельца.
2. `private.rate_limit_hits` уже чистится: security_hardening.sql:138-143 удаляет хиты старше окна и (с вероятностью 2 %) старше суток, :157 — сброс ключа. Этот пункт из исправления надо убрать.
3. «Пометить jobs как удалённые» невозможно без миграции: у jobs есть только `hidden` (init.sql:135), флага deleted нет. А физически удалять jobs нельзя: star_ledger.job_id ссылается на jobs без on delete (init.sql:158) → FK-ошибка.
4. Ссылка на «[research]: 10 ГБ/мес» верна (docs/platform/audit/2026-11-08-research.md:47-49) и там же уже рекомендовано правило retention — находка её дублирует, что нормально.

Сценарии: при 10 учениках в понедельник ничего не ломается — это не влияет на урок; риск юридический (принцип ограничения хранения, отсутствие раздела о платформе в политике при сборе ПД посторонних) и репутационный при продаже платформы. При 50 учениках/год — рост Storage на ~10 ГБ/мес без предела (на Pro это ~9-10 месяцев до 100 ГБ, на Free — read-only в первую неделю, см. research:38,48) и накопление контактов гостей без основания. Severity medium оставляю: не critical (деньги и урок не под угрозой), не high (ошибок ученики не увидят). Уверенность: high.
- **При 10:** После Хануки 10 ботов с заявками (имена, телефоны гостей) остаются в базе бессрочно; запрос «удалите мои данные» от гостя нечем выполнить, кроме ручного SQL. Для EU-проекта это нарушение принципа ограничения хранения.
- **При 50:** 50 групп в год × заявки × монтажи по 50 МБ → Storage и таблицы растут без предела (см. [research]: 10 ГБ/мес медиа), а личные данные гостей копятся у владельца без основания; при продаже платформы другим центрам ответственность владельца как оператора растёт пропорционально.
- **Как чиним (уточнённый план):** 1) Server Action учителя «Закрыть группу» (app/(platform)/teach/actions.ts, по образцу setGroupPaused с authorize(STAFF_ROLES)+assertTeachesGroup+audit):
   - profiles.disabled=true всем ученикам группы (поле уже проверяется при входе и в боте — UI-кнопка «Отключить/включить ученика» заодно закрывает дыру с бейджем без действия);
   - bots.active=false, при привязанном Telegram — deleteWebhook (как telegram-flow.ts:96);
   - delete bot_leads и bot_messages по bot_id ботов группы (один запрос admin-клиентом, RLS не мешает);
   - Storage: пройти jobs.storage_path учеников группы (точный список путей, без list по префиксу) и admin.storage.from('media').remove пачками ≤100, затем update jobs set storage_path=null, hidden=true. Строки jobs НЕ удалять (FK star_ledger.job_id, init.sql:158) и журнал звёзд не трогать.
   - Миграция: добавить `groups.closed_at timestamptz` и, при желании, `jobs.purged_at`; в тесты supabase/tests добавить проверку, что после закрытия у ботов группы нет заявок/сообщений.
2) Точечное удаление заявки гостя: кнопка «Удалить» в «Мой бот → Заявки» (lib/server/bot/dal.ts, рядом с чтением :111,170) — delete по id+bot_id владельца. Это закрывает запрос «удалите мои данные» от постороннего без удаления ученика.
3) Плановая чистка: pg_cron в Supabase (доступен и на Free) — ежедневно: delete bot_messages старше 30 дней (контекст берёт последние 10, лимиты считают 1 ч/24 ч — side effects нет); delete bot_leads, у которых bot→owner→groups.ends_on + 14 + 30 дней < today (при ends_on is null — не трогать или по created_at + 180 дней). rate_limit_hits НЕ добавлять — уже чистится (security_hardening.sql:138-143). Для Storage pg_cron не годится (delete из storage.objects оставляет файлы в S3): либо ленивый вызов очистки при открытии учителем страницы группы с истёкшим сроком (в духе closeStaleUploads, studio.ts:297), либо ночной pg_net → POST /api/admin/purge с секретом в заголовке и проверкой как у Telegram-вебхука. Ленивый вариант проще и не требует нового секрета.
4) content/privacy.ts:69 заменить заглушку разделом о платформе: какие данные (логин без e-mail, ник, возрастная группа, прогресс, работы и их медиа, переписка и заявки ботов — у взрослых владельцев с контактом гостя, хеш IP для лимитов), где (Supabase EU, Vercel), сроки (медиа и заявки — 30 дней после окончания группы + 14 дней работы бота; журнал звёзд — пока существует аккаунт; аккаунт удаляется по просьбе ведущего), как удалить (письмо владельцу; удаление ученика каскадом). Та же ссылка показывается гостям на /b/[slug], поэтому отдельно сказать про бота: «ответы пишет ИИ, заявку видит владелец бота и центр».
5) В отчёте учителя (teach/groups/[id]/report) и в «Мой бот» показывать дату автоудаления (lastDay из lifetime.ts + 30 дней).
Порядок: сначала п.4 (текст, нулевой риск, до понедельника), затем п.1-2 (одна миграция + действие), п.3 и 5 после запуска.
- **Сложность:** medium

### SEC-05 · medium · Возраст «взрослый» и «согласие родителей» ставятся одной галочкой пачкой, без записи кто, когда и на основании чего
- **Файлы:** app/(platform)/teach/actions.ts:174-179, app/(platform)/teach/actions.ts:258-267, app/(platform)/teach/actions.ts:318-347, lib/server/prices.ts:97-113, lib/server/bot/store.ts:146-148, supabase/migrations/20261101200000_init.sql:50-51, docs/course/770-status.md:21-23
- **Вердикт скептика:** Code facts confirmed, and the "fake adult" practice is stronger than the colleague stated — it is a written policy, not a guess. (1) app/(platform)/teach/actions.ts:174-179 takes one ageBand + one consent checkbox for the whole batch; :258-267 bulk-updates parental_consent_at = now() for every created id; audit meta at :273-279 stores only {count, failed, ageBand, consent, logins}. (2) updateStudentAge :318-347 writes any age_band; audit meta :340 is {ageBand, consent} with no previous value — and it cannot record one, because assertTeachesStudent (lib/server/dal/teacher.ts:215-229) selects only id, login, role, group_id, parental_consent_at, not age_band. (3) profiles (supabase/migrations/20261101200000_init.sql:43-56) has age_band + parental_consent_at only; no who/how/document. (4) ageBand==='adult' alone unlocks access 'adult' and 'google' (lib/server/prices.ts:102-105), Telegram and the contact field (lib/server/bot/store.ts:145-148). (5) docs/course/770-brief.md:9-11 lists real ages 13, 15, 17, 17 and says «ведущий ставит всем возраст „взрослый“»; docs/decisions.md:10 repeats it as a course decision; docs/course/770-status.md:22-23 confirms all 10 are 'adult' in a group of kind 'adults'. So the DB deliberately records false ages for four minors, and the only evidence of parental consent is a teacher checkbox with a timestamp. Scenario at 10 users (770): a parent or Google/Telegram asks on what basis a 13-year-old used Gemini/Nano Banana or a Telegram bot — the database can show only "teacher X set age_band=adult, consent=true on date Y"; the paper form is not linkable, and the age field itself contradicts reality. At 50 users / several teachers / other centres: the bulk checkbox makes one click cover 40 accounts (MAX_BATCH), no per-student record of which form, and later updateStudentAge overwrites without history, so reconstruction is impossible. This is not a runtime failure (no errors at 10 or 50 users, no stars lost), so under the rubric it is compliance/record-keeping exposure for a platform that processes children's data under GDPR and will be sold to other centres — medium is defensible; it is not high. Confidence: high on the code facts, medium on severity under this rubric. Side effects found in the colleague's fix: (a) a free-text consent_note on profiles would be readable by the student themselves — profiles_read RLS (init.sql:382-383) grants select on own row at row level, and Supabase has no column-level RLS, so a note like "form №12, signed by mother Anna" (a parent's personal data) leaks to the child; (b) consent_method 'none' alongside a non-null parental_consent_at is contradictory; (c) "require note when switching to adult in kind='teens'" would never fire for 770, whose group kind is 'adults' (770-status.md:23); (d) allow_google_minors already exists end to end (CreateGroupForm.tsx:67, GroupSettings.tsx:81-82, prices.ts:105), so fix item 2 is mostly a usage change, but it covers only access='google' — Telegram/talking head/contact (access 'adult', store.ts:146-148) would stay closed for real minors, which matches decisions.md:114 but contradicts what 770 currently does.
- **При 10:** Родитель спрашивает, на каком основании подросток пользовался моделью Google / Telegram-ботом: в базе есть только timestamp и действие учителя, нет ссылки на подписанную форму — доказать соблюдение нельзя.
- **При 50:** При 50 учениках и нескольких ведущих отследить, у кого разрешение есть на самом деле, невозможно; один щелчок «всем adult» распространяется на всех, а ответственность перед Google/Telegram и по GDPR (дети до 16) лежит на владельце платформы, которая продаётся другим центрам.
- **Как чиним (уточнённый план):** Small, no schema change on profiles: (1) lib/server/dal/teacher.ts assertTeachesStudent — also select age_band and return it; app/(platform)/teach/actions.ts updateStudentAge — write audit meta { from: { ageBand, consentAt }, to: { ageBand, consent }, note } so history is reconstructable (audit_log is admin-read only, init.sql:257-265, so it is the right place for sensitive notes). (2) Add an optional `note` (z.string().trim().max(200)) to both ageSchema and createStudentsSchema, and a text input in AgeForm (StudentList.tsx:111-139) and CreateStudents.tsx:85-92 labelled «Основание: номер/дата бумажной формы»; store it ONLY in audit meta, never on profiles (RLS at init.sql:382-383 lets the student read their own profile row). Make the field required when consent is checked. (3) If a durable per-student record is wanted, create a separate table public.consent_records(student_id, group_id, set_by, method check in ('paper','online'), note, created_at) with select/insert policy private.teaches_group(group_id) or is_admin, and show the latest record in the teacher report (lib/server/group-reports.ts) as «согласие: дата · кто · основание». Do not add 'none' as a method — absence of a row means no consent. (4) Stop faking age for 770: keep real age_band (u16 / 16_17) for the four minors and set allow_google_minors=true on the group (existing switch, GroupSettings.tsx:81) — this restores Google models for lessons 2–3 without lying in the data. Telegram export / talking head / contact field (access 'adult') then stay closed for minors, which is what docs/decisions.md:114 already prescribes; if the owner wants to override that, add an explicit group flag (e.g. allow_adult_features_minors) rather than age_band='adult', and record the decision in docs/course/770-open-questions.md (the Telegram-for-minors question at :31 is already open). (5) docs/platform/setup.md: add a short procedure — paper consent forms are kept by the centre, numbered; the number is entered in the «Основание» field; login↔name mapping stays offline (consistent with 770-status.md:27). Effort: small for (1)(2)(5); (3) and (4) are a short migration/UI change plus one owner decision.
- **Сложность:** small

### SEC-07 · medium · Любой может заблокировать вход конкретного ученика: 20 неудачных попыток на предсказуемый логин
- **Файлы:** lib/server/rate-limit.ts:17-22, app/(platform)/login/actions.ts:42-46, lib/server/accounts.ts:54-62
- **Вердикт скептика:** Подтверждено по коду (confidence: high для механизма, medium для вероятности злого умысла).

Механизм: lib/server/rate-limit.ts:19 — `account: limit 20, window 15 мин`, ключ = HMAC(email). app/(platform)/login/actions.ts:44 — попытка по логину засчитывается ДО signInWithPassword любому, кто прислал форму (не требуется ни сессия, ни тот же IP); сброс — только при успешном входе (actions.ts:55). SQL rate_limit_hit (supabase/migrations/20261105100000_security_hardening.sql:138-150): окно скользящее, отклонённые попытки не записываются, т.е. жертва своими попытками блок не продлевает, но и снять его не может — блок держится, пока 20 чужих записей не состарятся (до 15 мин после последней из них). Единственный публичный rate_limit_reset вызывается только из login после успеха; resetStudentPassword (app/(platform)/teach/actions.ts:297-316) меняет пароль через admin.updateUserById, но resetAttempts НЕ вызывает — новый пароль не помогает, пока окно не истечёт. Никакого другого «разблокировать» в учительском кабинете нет (grep по rate_limit/resetAttempts в app/(platform)/teach — пусто). Логины предсказуемы: `<код группы>-<транслит ника>` (lib/server/accounts.ts:55-64), печатаются на карточках и видны в списке учеников (teach/groups/[id]/StudentList.tsx:39). Supabase Auth per-account lockout не делает, лимитит только по IP Vercel — т.е. это единственный per-login замок, и он доступен любому анониму.

Дополнительно: (а) текст TOO_MANY «Подождите пару минут» (actions.ts:22) вводит в заблуждение — реальное ожидание до 15 мин; (б) тем же способом блокируется вход учителя/админа (ключ — его email); (в) если шутник делает это из класса (тот же Wi‑Fi), каждые 20 его попыток съедают 20 из 100 общего IP-бюджета (rate-limit.ts:21) — см. SEC-02; 5 таких «шуток» = заперт весь класс.

Сценарий 10: один подросток за 10–20 с с телефона (мобильный интернет или классный Wi‑Fi) вводит соседу 20 раз неверный пароль → сосед видит TOO_MANY до 15 мин; учитель бессилен (смена пароля не снимает счётчик). Урок не срывается, но один ученик теряет до 15 мин в начале занятия.
Сценарий 50: вероятность выше, несколько учеников не входят; при повторах из класса добавляется исчерпание общего IP-лимита (SEC-02) — уже весь класс. Severity medium оставляю: урок не ломается, деньги/данные не под угрозой, нужен умысел; в связке с SEC-02 становится high, но это уже учтено в SEC-02.

Оценка предложенного fix: п.1 (кнопка «Снять блокировку» + сброс в resetStudentPassword) — верный, дешёвый, без побочных эффектов (учитель — доверенная роль, писать в audit_log). п.2 (поднять до 30–40 + экспоненциальный backoff) — НЕ рекомендую в таком виде: поднятие лимита расширяет окно перебора (пароли 3 слова из ~230 + 2 цифры), а backoff требует менять контракт rate_limit_hit (boolean → retry-after), т.е. это не «small». п.3 (audit_log на каждую неудачную попытку) — рискован: 20 строк на шутку, анонимная запись в журнал сама становится вектором DoS по базе; писать надо одно событие на факт срабатывания лимита.
- **При 10:** Шутник в группе блокирует соседа на 15 минут; учитель не может помочь, кроме как выдать новый пароль (это не снимает счётчик).
- **При 50:** При 50 подростках вероятность «шалости» выше; несколько учеников не могут войти в начале урока, ведущий тратит время.
- **Как чиним (уточнённый план):** 1) app/(platform)/teach/actions.ts, resetStudentPassword: сразу после updateUserById добавить `if (student.login) await resetAttempts(LOGIN_LIMITS.account, studentEmail(student.login));` (импорт из lib/server/rate-limit и lib/server/accounts) — смена пароля всегда разблокирует. 2) Новое server action `unlockStudentLogin(studentId)` (authorize(STAFF_ROLES) + assertTeachesStudent) → resetAttempts для login-account ключа ученика + audit('student.login_unlocked'); кнопка «Снять блокировку входа» в StudentList.tsx рядом с «Новый пароль». 3) Не поднимать account-лимит; вместо этого в login/actions.ts разделить тексты: для IP-лимита оставить общий, для account-лимита — «Слишком много неверных попыток для этого логина. Подождите 15 минут или попросите ведущего снять блокировку» (ключ считается и для несуществующих логинов, утечки существования нет). 4) Писать в audit_log одно событие `login.locked` в момент, когда takeAttempt(account) впервые вернул false (не на каждую неудачу), с полем ipHash = HMAC(ipRateLimitKey(ip)) — чтобы учитель/админ увидел источник. 5) Опционально позже, отдельной задачей: учитывать неудачи по паре (login, ip-key) с меньшим порогом и по логину — с большим, или «доверенный браузер» по cookie после успешного входа; это не «small» и не нужно к понедельнику. 6) В docs/platform/setup.md: сценарий «ученик не может войти — TOO_MANY» → учитель жмёт «Снять блокировку» или ждёт 15 мин.
- **Сложность:** small

### TUX-01 · medium · Teacher cannot disable, remove or move a student — only pause the whole group
- **Файлы:** app/(platform)/teach/actions.ts:22-384, app/(platform)/teach/groups/[id]/StudentList.tsx:45, lib/server/dal/auth.ts:75, docs/course/770-status.md:116
- **Вердикт скептика:** Confirmed from code. app/(platform)/teach/actions.ts exports exactly createGroup, updateGroup, setGroupPaused, createStudents, resetStudentPassword, updateStudentAge, grantStudentStars (lines 68, 105, 150, 200, 297, 321, 364); a repo-wide grep finds no write to profiles.disabled or a profile's group_id anywhere in app/ or lib/ (the only `group_id:` write is in createUser app_metadata, actions.ts:240). The flag is read-only in the cabinet: teacher.ts listStudents (line ~137) selects it, page.tsx:128 passes it, StudentList.tsx:45 renders the «отключён» badge, and no form can set it. The flag IS honoured everywhere it matters — getUser (dal/auth.ts:75) returns null, login/actions.ts:58 signs the user out, lock_for_spend raises user_disabled (20261105100000_security_hardening.sql:182-184), bot brain refuses (lib/server/bot/brain.ts:181), RLS helpers exclude disabled staff (init.sql:309,321) — so the only missing piece is a write path + button, exactly as the finding says. The only per-student stop lever today is group-wide groups.paused (actions.ts:150-167). Status doc check also holds: 770-status.md:22 says the real group was created with 10 students, line 116 plans 9 — there is no cabinet action to get there. At 10 users: one no-show/misbehaving login can only be stopped by pausing all 10 or via Supabase SQL Editor mid-lesson. At 50: 1-2 problem accounts per workshop are plausible and pausing 49 people is unacceptable for a resold product. Severity stays medium, not high, because (a) a partial UI workaround exists that the finding missed: grantStudentStars accepts negative amounts down to -10000 (actions.ts:349-356; grant_stars in init.sql keeps balance >= 0), so a teacher can zero a wasteful student's balance and paid generations fail with insufficient_stars without pausing the group; (b) the owner is technical and can flip profiles.disabled in SQL before/after class; (c) no money or data is at risk and no student sees errors. Confidence: high (code-verified).
- **При 10:** Monday: one of the 10 printed logins turns out to be a no-show / a student misbehaves and burns stars on nonsense. The teacher's only lever is «Поставить на паузу», which stops all 9 other students; otherwise he must open Supabase SQL Editor during class.
- **При 50:** With 50 people one or two problem accounts per workshop are likely; pausing 49 people to stop one is not acceptable for a product sold to other centres. Removing the planned 10th student from group 770 also requires SQL.
- **Как чиним (уточнённый план):** Keep the proposed setStudentDisabled, with these corrections/additions:

1. lib/server/audit.ts:10-28 — extend the AuditAction union with "student.disable" | "student.enable" (otherwise the audit() call will not type-check; the DB check constraint `^[a-z_.]{2,60}$` already accepts it).

2. app/(platform)/teach/actions.ts — add, mirroring setGroupPaused (150-167):
   const disableSchema = z.object({ studentId: uuid, disabled: z.enum(["1","0"]) });
   export async function setStudentDisabled(_prev, formData): authorize(STAFF_ROLES) → safeParse → assertTeachesStudent(user, studentId) → createAdminClient().from("profiles").update({ disabled }).eq("id", student.id) → audit({ action: disabled ? "student.disable" : "student.enable", targetUser: student.id, targetGroup: group.id }) → refresh() → { ok: true }.
   Optional, best-effort only: createAdminClient().auth.admin.signOut(student.id, "global") to revoke refresh tokens. It is not required for safety: every page/Server Action/route passes through getUser (auth.ts:75) and every spend through lock_for_spend, both of which already reject disabled users on the very next request; the access token itself grants nothing useful. Wrap it in try/catch so a Supabase Auth hiccup does not fail the disable.

3. app/(platform)/teach/groups/[id]/StudentList.tsx — add a fourth form (DisableForm) to the details grid at line 53-57 (change lg:grid-cols-3 to 4 or stack it), using useActionState(setStudentDisabled); hidden studentId + hidden disabled="1"/"0" chosen from student.disabled; button «Отключить» (variant danger) when active, «Включить» when disabled, with a one-line hint «Ученик не сможет войти и тратить звёзды; звёзды и работы сохранятся». The existing badge at line 45 updates after refresh().

4. Do NOT implement removal via admin.auth.admin.deleteUser. profiles.id references auth.users ON DELETE CASCADE (init.sql:44) and wallets, star_ledger, jobs, quiz/progress, bots all cascade from profiles (init.sql:82,117,153,177,189,200), so deleting the 10th student would erase their ledger rows and retroactively change «Роздано/Потрачено» in getGroupStarStats (teacher.ts) and lose the money trail; audit_log.target_user has no FK and would keep dangling ids. Likewise do NOT implement removeStudentFromGroup as group_id = NULL: assertTeachesStudent (teacher.ts) throws forbidden when group_id is null and listStudents filters by group_id, so the profile becomes unreachable from any teacher UI while getUser still lets it log in. If a move is really needed, implement moveStudentToGroup(studentId, targetGroupId) with assertTeachesGroup on the target (same teacher or admin) and audit "student.move"; otherwise "remove" == disable.

5. For Monday: flip profiles.disabled = true for the 10th login once via SQL Editor (or the new button) before class; note that getGroup/listGroups count all role='student' profiles regardless of disabled (teacher.ts), so the header will still read «Учеников: 10» — either accept that or filter `.eq("disabled", false)` in those two counts (optional polish).
- **Сложность:** small

### TUX-02 · medium · «Звёзды группы» card (Роздано/Потрачено) reads star_ledger without paging — silently capped at 1000 rows
- **Файлы:** lib/server/dal/teacher.ts:263-279, app/(platform)/teach/groups/[id]/page.tsx:265-280, lib/server/group-reports.ts:206-220
- **Вердикт скептика:** Confirmed from code, but the cited line numbers are wrong. The actual locations: lib/server/dal/teacher.ts:164-190 (`getGroupStarStats`: `admin.from('star_ledger').select('delta, reason').in('user_id', ids)` with no .range()/.limit()/.order()); app/(platform)/teach/groups/[id]/page.tsx:42 (call) and :103-108 (the «Роздано»/«Потрачено» card); lib/server/group-reports.ts:13-24 (`fetchAll`, PAGE=1000) and :78-88 (the same star_ledger table read with paging). supabase/config.toml:13 sets `max_rows = 1000` for local dev and Supabase's default Max Rows for hosted projects is also 1000 (docs/platform/audit/2026-11-08-research.md:62-65), so PostgREST silently truncates the ledger read to 1000 rows. Because the query has no ORDER BY, which 1000 rows come back is unspecified, so both «Роздано» (welcome/grant/adjust/quiz_bonus rows) and «Потрачено» (debit/refund/bot_reply) can be under-counted, not only «Потрачено». The report page (group-reports.ts) pages correctly, so the two teacher views will disagree once a group exceeds 1000 ledger rows. Row-volume re-derivation: text chat is a job costing 1 star (init.sql:859-860) created via create_job (wallet.ts:78), so each chat message is one debit row; with ~15 chat + 1-3 images + 1-3 videos + occasional refunds + 1 welcome + quiz_bonus, a student produces ~20-30 rows per 75-minute lesson. At 10 students: 200-300 rows/lesson, the 1000 cap is crossed around lesson 4; before that the card is correct, so the Monday workshop itself is unaffected and the 770-status.md §6 check («бюджет, роздано и потрачено совпадают») passes on day one but will fail later for no visible reason. At 50 students: 50 x 20-30 = 1000-1500 rows in the first workshop, so by the end of the first 75 minutes the card under-reports (typically «Потрачено» by ~30-50%, since debits dominate), and a teacher deciding on top-ups or checking budget vs spend sees wrong totals. No money or data is at risk: wallet balances and daily caps are computed in SQL (security_hardening.sql:192-203), only the dashboard aggregate is wrong. Severity medium is correct (degraded/incorrect teacher reporting, silent). The original fix needs correction: (1) 'reuse fetchAll() from group-reports.ts' creates a circular import, since group-reports.ts:4 already imports listStudents from dal/teacher.ts; (2) a PostgREST `select reason, delta.sum()` aggregate is not safe to rely on because Supabase ships with PostgREST aggregates disabled by default (confidence: medium, from memory of Supabase defaults, not verified in this repo); (3) moving to the RLS client is viable: policy ledger_read (init.sql:391-392) allows `private.teaches_user(user_id)` and teaches_group includes is_admin (init.sql:314-320), so teacher and admin both read their students' ledger rows. Confidence: high on the bug and scenarios, medium on the aggregate-API caveat.
- **При 10:** ~15 chat debits + 5 media debits + refunds + bot replies ≈ 25–40 rows per student per lesson → 250–400 rows/lesson; after lesson 3 the group page shows «Потрачено» lower than the report page, and the «бюджет vs роздано» sanity check in 770-status.md §6 fails for no visible reason.
- **При 50:** 50 students × ~40 rows ≈ 2000 rows in the first 75 minutes → by the end of the first workshop the card under-reports spending by half; a teacher deciding whether to top up stars gets wrong numbers.
- **Как чиним (уточнённый план):** Aggregate in SQL instead of pulling rows into Node. Add a migration with a function the teacher's RLS client can call, e.g. `create function public.group_star_totals(p_group uuid) returns table(given bigint, spent bigint) language sql stable security invoker set search_path = '' as $$ select coalesce(sum(l.delta) filter (where l.reason not in ('debit','refund','bot_reply')),0), greatest(0, coalesce(sum(-l.delta) filter (where l.reason in ('debit','refund','bot_reply')),0)) from public.star_ledger l join public.profiles p on p.id = l.user_id where p.group_id = p_group and p.role = 'student' $$; grant execute on function public.group_star_totals(uuid) to authenticated;` (security invoker keeps RLS: ledger_read already lets a teacher/admin see exactly their students' rows, so a non-owning teacher simply gets 0/0; getGroup() before it already 404s for foreign groups). In lib/server/dal/teacher.ts:164-190 replace the two admin queries with `const supabase = await createClient(); const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc('group_star_totals', { p_group: groupId }).single();` and map to { given, spent } — one round trip, ~1 row, no Max Rows dependence, and it matches the RLS-client pattern of listGroups/getGroup/listStudents (teacher.ts:22-70). Add a pgTAP case in supabase/tests asserting the function equals a manual sum for a group with >1000 ledger rows (generate_series insert via the existing security-definer path or a direct insert as postgres in the test). If a pure-TS fix is preferred for now: do NOT import from group-reports.ts (circular); move fetchAll/PAGE into a small shared module such as lib/server/supabase/paging.ts, import it from both files, and add `.order('id').range(from, to)` to the star_ledger read in getGroupStarStats — but the SQL aggregate is cheaper (the TS paging would otherwise grow to 2-5 requests of 1000 rows per group-page render at 50 students). Side effects: none on wallets/ledger writes (function is read-only, security invoker); the group page loses one admin-client dependency; update docs/platform/spec.md §7 note that teacher aggregates are computed in SQL.
- **Сложность:** small

### TUX-03 · medium · No bulk grant: changing stars_per_student does nothing for existing students, so the planned 520→450 ★ correction is 10 (or 50) manual forms
- **Файлы:** components/platform/BudgetFields.tsx:415-418, supabase/migrations/20261103110000_group_budget.sql:57-78, app/(platform)/teach/groups/[id]/StudentList.tsx:229-270, docs/course/770-status.md:116
- **Вердикт скептика:** Confirmed in code, with two corrections to the evidence. (1) No bulk grant exists: grep over app/, lib/, components/, supabase/ finds no «всем»/bulk/grantAll action; the only grant path is the per-row GrantForm in app/(platform)/teach/groups/[id]/StudentList.tsx:67-109 (inside a <details>, StudentList.tsx:36-58), which calls grantStudentStars (app/(platform)/teach/actions.ts:364-384) → public.grant_stars for ONE student. (2) updateGroup (actions.ts:105-146) only rewrites groups.stars_per_student; the welcome grant is made once per student in private.apply_student_meta (supabase/migrations/20261103110000_group_budget.sql:57-78, guarded by `exists … reason='welcome'` plus unique index star_ledger_welcome_once, init.sql:170). BudgetFields.tsx:87-90 documents this («уже созданным — кнопкой «Начислить»»). (3) The trigger is real and already happened: docs/course/770-status.md:22-25 says group 770 was created 5 Nov with 10 students × 520 ★, while line 116 plans 9 students × 450 ★ (520 only if everyone gets the bonus) — i.e. 9–10 manual −70 grants. Corrections to the colleague's evidence: a typo like −700 is NOT applied — grant_stars raises `insufficient_stars` when balance+amount < 0 (init.sql:729), and any applied mistake is reversible by a counter-grant, so «irreversible» is overstated; the «За что» note is optional (grantSchema note: optional, actions.ts:357); and the page does refresh after the teacher's own grant (`refresh()` from next/cache, actions.ts:3,379) — only other people's changes (students spending) need a manual reload. Scenario at 10: ~10 opens + 10 forms ≈ 3–5 minutes before Monday, tolerable but error-prone; mid-lesson top-ups are one form each. At 50: 50 forms ≈ 10–15 minutes of clicking; a «raise everyone by N» or «bring everyone to N» is realistically skipped. Severity medium is right: nothing breaks and no money/data is at risk (degraded teacher UX), not high. Confidence: high (code read directly).
- **При 10:** Before Monday the teacher must open 10 rows and type -70 ten times; a typo (e.g. -700) is irreversible except by another manual grant. Mid-lesson, when 3 students run out of stars during video generation, each top-up is a separate form on a page that does not auto-refresh.
- **При 50:** 50 individual grants ≈ 10–15 minutes of clicking during a 75-minute workshop; realistically the teacher will not do it and students sit with 0 ★ (see TUX-09 for visibility).
- **Как чиним (уточнённый план):** Prefer a single SQL function over a server-action loop so the batch is atomic and one round trip: `public.grant_stars_group(p_actor uuid, p_group uuid, p_amount integer, p_mode text /* 'add' | 'top_up_to' */, p_note text, p_key text)` (security definer, search_path=''), authorised like grant_stars (admin, or teacher with groups.teacher_id = p_actor). It iterates `profiles where group_id = p_group and role='student' and not disabled`, locks each wallet `for update`, and for each student: mode 'add' → delta = p_amount, but clamp negative deltas to −balance (floor at 0) instead of raising insufficient_stars, otherwise one low-balance student aborts the whole group; mode 'top_up_to' → delta = greatest(0, p_amount − balance), skip when 0. Per-student idempotency key = `p_key || ':' || user_id` (star_ledger_idem is unique on (user_id, idempotency_key), init.sql:167, so a re-submit of the same form key is a no-op per student and a partially-applied batch can be safely retried). Write one star_ledger row per student (reason 'grant'/'adjust' like grant_stars) and ONE audit_log row `stars.grant_group` with {amount, mode, note, affected}. Expose it as a server action `grantGroupStars` next to grantStudentStars (same zod shape: amount −10000..10000, idempotencyKey from a client-side `crypto.randomUUID()` regenerated on success, like GrantForm) and a form «Всем ученикам группы: +N / до N» in the «Звёзды группы» card on app/(platform)/teach/groups/[id]/page.tsx (near the Бюджет/Роздано/Потрачено list) with a confirm step showing «N ★ × K учеников = M ★». If a server-action loop over listStudents is chosen instead, it fits maxDuration 60 (50 sequential RPCs ≈ a few seconds) but is not atomic — keep the derived per-student key so a retry is idempotent. For the «Роздано > Бюджет» warning: note that getGroupStarStats (lib/server/dal/teacher.ts:184-190) counts every non-debit delta as given, including quiz bonuses, so either compare budget against welcome+grant only or label the warning «включая бонусы» to avoid false alarms.
- **Сложность:** medium

### TUX-04 · medium · Task ids were renamed (l2-t1-script → l2-t1-hero-board, lesson-1 ids on 1e9c02d) without a data migration — orphaned lesson_progress rows and hidden job results
- **Файлы:** docs/course/lesson-2.json:105, lib/server/group-reports.ts:300-302, app/(platform)/app/lessons/[n]/page.tsx:63-64, lib/server/lesson-progress.ts:357,379-387, app/(platform)/app/lessons/[n]/actions.ts:102, supabase/migrations/20261101200000_init.sql:188-194
- **Вердикт скептика:** CONFIRMED by code and by read-only queries against the production DB (project njmstsfepkrtsixgtbcb); confidence high. Cited line numbers in group-reports.ts / lesson-progress.ts were wrong (files are 136 / 96 lines), but the mechanics are exact: lib/server/group-reports.ts:107-110 counts every lesson_progress row per lesson with no check against lessonProgressSteps(); app/(platform)/app/lessons/[n]/page.tsx:60-62 counts only current steps; lib/server/lesson-progress.ts:49-58 groups jobs by task_ref and page.tsx:189 renders only results[task.id], so jobs under a dead id disappear from the lesson page (gallery has no task_ref filter, so they remain there); actions.ts:102 (isLessonStep) refuses to toggle a non-current step, so a student cannot clear an orphan; components/platform/GeneratePanel.tsx:93 keys the localStorage chat thread as `iis-thread:${lessonId}:${taskId}:${option.key}`, so the browser chat history is also orphaned (note the option key for lesson-2 task 1 changed too, to «Длинный ответ», which alone would have reset that thread). Git: 1e9c02d (2026-11-05 00:37 UTC) renamed all 3 lesson-1 ids; ad4c5cc + e2bfe8b (2026-11-06 01:18 UTC) renamed lesson-2 ids; 4b8a0c3 / 0540753 restructured lessons 3/4 with different task counts (not 1:1, so no rename mapping exists there). Live data: orphans exist, all belonging to ONE md1 tester (user a642831d…): lesson_progress lesson-1 holds both old and new ids (hallucination-hunt, prompt-upgrade, my-character AND weak-strong-prompt, catch-mistake, homework-research) → 9 rows against 6 steps, so the teacher report renders «9/6»; lesson-2 holds l2-t1-script, l2-t2-sheet-frame, l2-t4-sound plus l2-t2-sheet, l2-t4-animate, quiz → report shows «6/8», student page shows 3/8 (exactly the colleague's example). jobs: 21 rows with dead task_ref (l1-t1-hallucination-hunt 4, l1-t2-prompt-upgrade 2, l1-t3-my-character 3, l2-t1-script 3, l2-t2-sheet-frame 2, l2-t3-animate 2, l2-t4-sound 3, l2-t5-edit 2), all this user's, all dated before the respective rename. The 770 group has zero rows under any id, so the Monday lesson (10 students) is NOT affected by existing data: at 10 users nothing breaks, only the md1 demo report looks absurd (9/6). At 50 users / other centres the risk is forward-looking and real: lesson JSON will be adapted per centre, and any id rename silently inflates report counts and hides results, since no code path or doc (spec §4 lists fields but says nothing about id stability) guards it. Severity stays medium: no money or data is lost (jobs/ledger intact, visible in gallery), no student sees an error on Monday, but the teacher report is already wrong for the test group and the defect recurs on every content edit. The proposed fix is wrong as written: `update lesson_progress set step=new where step=old` violates the primary key (user_id, lesson_id, step) today for lesson 1 because the tester already holds both rows, and a 1:1 rename is semantically wrong for lesson-1/3/4 where the tasks themselves changed, not just the ids.
- **При 10:** Teacher opens the report: md1 testers show inconsistent counts; a 770 student who tried lesson 2 on 5–6 Nov sees their «Герой и раскадровка» chat history gone and re-pays 3 ★ for it. Confusing but not blocking.
- **При 50:** Every future content edit that renames a task id (likely when selling the course to other centres and adapting lessons) silently corrupts progress for all existing groups; at 50 students the report becomes untrustworthy.
- **Как чиним (уточнённый план):** 1) Code, no migration needed and safe for Monday: in lib/server/group-reports.ts build `const valid = new Map(lessons.map(l => [l.id, new Set(lessonProgressSteps(l.id))]))` before the loop at lines 107-110 and count a row only if `valid.get(row.lesson_id)?.has(row.step)`; this makes the report agree with the student page (page.tsx:60-62) for any past or future rename. 2) One-off data cleanup for the only affected user (md1 tester) — conflict-safe, run in Supabase SQL editor as postgres: `insert into public.lesson_progress (user_id, lesson_id, step, completed_at) select user_id, lesson_id, 'l2-t1-hero-board', completed_at from public.lesson_progress where lesson_id='lesson-2' and step='l2-t1-script' on conflict (user_id, lesson_id, step) do nothing; update public.jobs set task_ref='l2-t1-hero-board' where lesson_id='lesson-2' and task_ref='l2-t1-script';` (this is the only semantically 1:1 rename: same slot, same chat task). For the remaining orphans (lesson-1 old ids, l2-t2-sheet-frame, l2-t3-animate, l2-t4-sound, l2-t5-edit) the tasks changed meaning, so do NOT remap; delete the dead progress rows: `delete from public.lesson_progress p where not exists (select 1 from (values ('lesson-1','theory'),('lesson-1','l1-t1-weak-strong-prompt'),…all current steps…) v(lesson_id, step) where v.lesson_id=p.lesson_id and v.step=p.step);` and leave the jobs as they are (they stay visible in the gallery; no money or files touched). Do not use a plain UPDATE on lesson_progress: the PK (user_id, lesson_id, step) already collides for lesson 1. 3) Process rule in docs/platform/spec.md §4 (tasks[].id): «id задания — стабильный ключ, хранится в lesson_progress.step, jobs.task_ref и в ключе чата localStorage; переименование = миграция данных (insert … on conflict do nothing + delete + update jobs) или новый id для нового задания». 4) Optional guard in scripts/check-lessons.mjs: keep a committed list of retired ids (docs/course/retired-task-ids.json) and fail the build if an id disappears from lesson-N.json without being added there; this is cheap and does not depend on git history at build time (Vercel shallow clones make a previous-commit diff unreliable). 5) Not needed: any change to actions.ts — once orphan rows are deleted and the report filters, the inability to toggle a dead step is harmless.
- **Сложность:** small

### TUX-05 · medium · Creating 50 students = two batches of 40, and the second batch erases the first batch's one-time passwords from the screen
- **Файлы:** app/(platform)/teach/actions.ts:22,210, app/(platform)/teach/groups/[id]/CreateStudents.tsx:42-44,69,100-157, lib/platform/messages.ts:72
- **Вердикт скептика:** Confirmed from code, confidence high. (1) app/(platform)/teach/actions.ts:22 `MAX_BATCH = 40`, :210 returns `too_many_students` → messages.ts:72 «За один раз — не больше 40 учеников». (2) app/(platform)/teach/groups/[id]/CreateStudents.tsx:42 `useActionState(createStudents, null)` — React replaces the state with each action's return value, and :44/:100–157 render table and print cards only from `state.created`; nothing accumulates. actions.ts:197 and :282–286 confirm passwords are returned once and never stored. (3) The component is mounted without a key (page.tsx:118) and `refresh()` at actions.ts:281 only refetches server components, so batch-1 state survives the refresh — it is lost only when batch 2 returns, exactly as the finding says. The React 19 form auto-reset after a successful server action makes the "paste 40 → paste the next 10 immediately" flow even more natural, since the textarea is already empty. (4) Hint at CreateStudents.tsx:69 says «До 40 за раз» but nothing says "print before the next batch"; the warning at :107–108 only mentions leaving the page. (5) No bulk reset exists: StudentList.tsx:144–162 has one `resetStudentPassword` form per student, so recovery for 40 students = 40 manual resets. Scenario at 10 users: not triggered (10 < 40); print path works (header/cards carry print:hidden). Scenario at 50 users: teacher pastes 50 → error → pastes 40, then 10 → 40 passwords vanish from the screen; realistic the night before a workshop. Severity medium is correct: teacher-side onboarding, recoverable, no money/data at risk, but costly in time and reputation ("platform is the owner's face"). The colleague's maxDuration claim is accurate: node_modules/next/dist/docs/.../maxDuration.md states page-level maxDuration applies to Server Actions on that page, and page.tsx:19 sets 60; 12 rounds of 5 parallel createUser calls (~0.3–0.7 s each round-trip Vercel→Supabase EU) is ~4–9 s, far under 60 s. Side-effect check of the proposed fix: raising MAX_BATCH alone is incomplete — the literal "40" is hardcoded in three more places (CreateStudents.tsx:69 hint, messages.ts:72, page.tsx:18 comment) and still breaks for groups larger than the new limit (planned_students allows up to 1000, actions.ts:57). Accumulating in client state is the robust fix and has no server-side side effects; logins are unique across batches (assignLogins against existing logins, actions.ts:218–222), so `key={s.login}` stays unique. Supabase admin createUser via service role has no documented hard rate limit at this volume (medium confidence), so a 60 limit is also safe.
- **При 10:** Not triggered (10 < 40). Printing works: header/footer/other cards carry print:hidden.
- **При 50:** Teacher pastes 50 names → error «не больше 40» → pastes 40, then immediately pastes the remaining 10 → the 40 passwords disappear; he has to reset 40 passwords one by one (TUX-06) or recreate. Realistic the night before the workshop.
- **Как чиним (уточнённый план):** Do both, with accumulation as the primary fix. (A) Accumulate across submits in CreateStudents.tsx using the same client-wrapper pattern already used in StudentList.tsx:70: `const [state, action] = useActionState<CreateStudentsResult | null, FormData>(async (prev, formData) => { const res = await createStudents(null, formData); if (res.ok && prev?.ok) { const seen = new Set(res.created.map(s => s.login)); return { ...res, created: [...prev.created.filter(s => !seen.has(s.login)), ...res.created] }; } if (!res.ok && prev?.ok) return { ...prev, lastError: res }; return res; }, null);` — i.e. on a later success append to the previous `created` (and show the latest `failed`); on a later validation error keep the previous `created` visible and render the error from `lastError` (FormError/FieldError would need to read `state.lastError ?? state`), so a typo in batch 2 never hides batch 1's passwords. Table and print view then show all students created in this page session; change the heading to «Создано за этот сеанс: N». Keep the existing «после ухода со страницы их не будет» notice — it stays true. (B) Raise MAX_BATCH to 60 in actions.ts:22 and update the three hardcoded strings: messages.ts:72 («не больше 60»), CreateStudents.tsx:69 («До 60 за раз»), page.tsx:18 comment; maxDuration=60 on the page already covers Server Actions and 12 rounds of 5 createUser calls take ~4–9 s. (C) Extend the hint at CreateStudents.tsx:69: «Если учеников больше, добавляйте по частям — карточки всех пачек останутся на экране до ухода со страницы.» Optional low-effort complement for TUX-06: a per-group «Сбросить пароли выбранным» bulk reset so recovery never means 40 clicks. Effort small; no migrations or server-side side effects.
- **Сложность:** small

### TUX-09 · medium · Nothing for running a live class: no auto-refresh, no «кто на нуле / у кого не идёт» view, no CSV export, no re-print of logins
- **Файлы:** app/(platform)/teach/groups/[id]/report/page.tsx:23-55, app/(platform)/teach/groups/[id]/page.tsx:206-304, app/(platform)/teach/groups/[id]/StudentList.tsx:182-223, lib/server/group-reports.ts:242-329
- **Вердикт скептика:** Every factual claim holds. (1) No auto-refresh: app/(platform)/teach/groups/[id]/page.tsx:37-42 renders Group as a request-time server component behind Suspense; grep for router.refresh/setInterval/visibilitychange/revalidate under app/(platform)/teach returns nothing, so balances change only on F5 (one wording fix: the pages are dynamic, not "static", renders — the point stands). (2) No sort/filter by balance: lib/server/dal/teacher.ts:135 orders by login; StudentList.tsx:20-31 renders rows in that order, balance is only a Stars badge at StudentList.tsx:46-48. (3) Failed generations invisible to the teacher: lib/server/gallery.ts:119 does `.neq("status","failed")` for the teacher gallery; the only trace is spent.refunds in lib/server/group-reports.ts:122 → «Возвратов» column at report/page.tsx:170, with no time or error text. (4) No last-activity field anywhere in StudentRow/ReportStudent types. (5) No CSV for the group report; the only CSV route is app/(platform)/app/bot/leads.csv/route.ts. (6) Passwords are shown once by design (spec §1), resetStudentPassword (teach/actions.ts:297-314) is per-student via ResetForm (StudentList.tsx:143-165); no bulk reset or re-print. Severity stays medium: this is a feature gap, not a defect against spec §7, and the lesson does not break. At 10 students the teacher can F5 and scan 10 rows (low-ish impact; the student also sees the failure on their own panel and asks). At 50 students the list has 50 collapsed <details> rows sorted by login, no way to find the 3 at 0 ★ or the ones with repeated refunds without opening /report and reading a 50-row table, and no per-student summary to hand the centre — genuinely degraded teacher operation, matching "medium". Confidence high on facts; the proposed fix is sound but needs three corrections (below).
- **При 10:** Teacher keeps F5-ing the group page to see who is out of stars; a student whose videos fail twice (refund) is invisible until he raises a hand.
- **При 50:** With 50 rows of <details> the teacher cannot find the 3 students at 0 ★ or the 5 whose jobs keep refunding; no export to hand the centre a per-student summary after the workshop.
- **Как чиним (уточнённый план):** Phase 1 (small, safe): (a) In page.tsx:122 sort students by balance asc then login before passing to StudentList, and in StudentList add client-side chips «все / 0 ★ / мало ★», where the «мало» threshold is NOT a hard-coded 50 but the cheapest enabled image price from price_list (pass it from the server; otherwise the chip is wrong whenever prices change). Highlight rows whose balance < that threshold. (b) Add a small client component <AutoRefresh intervalMs={30000}/> on the group page that calls router.refresh() on an interval only while document.visibilityState === 'visible' and pauses while any Server Action is pending (useTransition/isPending check) to avoid racing a grant submit. Load is negligible: 1 RSC refetch / 30 s per teacher tab = ~150 invocations per 75-min lesson, 4 Supabase queries each (listStudents ×2, getGroupStarStats ×2). Note getGroupStarStats reads star_ledger unpaged (TUX-02) — fix that first or the auto-refreshing card shows a stale cap at 1000 rows every 30 s. router.refresh() preserves useState/useActionState and uncontrolled input values because rows are keyed by student.id, and <details open> is uncontrolled DOM so stays open. (c) Add GET app/(platform)/teach/groups/[id]/report.csv/route.ts: requireStaff → getGroup (404 if not owner) → getGroupReport; columns nickname, login, balance, spent_today, spent_total, generations, refunds, bot_replies, then per-lesson steps/N and quiz score; reuse the formula-escaping from lib/server/bot/csv.ts:96-100 (extract a generic toCsv, leadsToCsv is leads-specific), prefix a UTF-8 BOM for Excel, cache-control: no-store, and write an audit row (action "group.report_export") exactly like leads.csv does — the file contains minors' logins. Phase 2: per-student last job status/time. Query via the teacher's RLS client (jobs_read already allows private.teaches_group(group_id), init.sql:397-404) using jobs_group_created_idx: select user_id,status,error,created_at where group_id=X order by created_at desc limit 500, reduce in JS to last job + failed-today count per student (PostgREST has no DISTINCT ON). Show «последняя генерация: 12:41, ошибка» and a «≥2 ошибок сегодня» badge in StudentList. Do NOT propose bulk password reset as the re-print fix during class: updateUserById({password}) logs the student out of all sessions (see AUTH finding, audit file line 46); offer «Сбросить пароли выбранным и распечатать карточки» only when the group is paused / before the lesson, with a warning in the UI.
- **Сложность:** medium

### AUTH-10 · low · Docs say the Supabase project is in Frankfurt; the live project is eu-west-1 (Ireland)
- **Файлы:** docs/platform/setup.md:18
- **Вердикт скептика:** Confirmed, not refuted. Supabase list_projects (run now, 2026-11-08) returns project `workshop` (ref njmstsfepkrtsixgtbcb) with region `eu-west-1` (AWS Ireland), status ACTIVE_HEALTHY. /home/user/workshop/docs/platform/setup.md:18 says «Проект `workshop`, регион ЕС (Frankfurt)». /home/user/workshop/docs/platform/spec.md:8 only says «регион ЕС» (correct, no change needed). The Frankfurt expectation originates in the research doc /home/user/workshop/docs/research/platform-security.md:61 and :1089 (recommendation «Central EU (Frankfurt)»), so setup.md copied the plan, not the deployed reality. No code, config or migration references a region (grep over *.ts/*.json/*.toml found nothing), so there is zero runtime impact at 10 or 50 users; both eu-west-1 and eu-central-1 are inside the EU, so the GDPR/data-residency claim in docs remains true. Purely a documentation inaccuracy that a buyer or auditor could catch. Severity low is correct; confidence high (verified against live project metadata).
- **При 10:** None.
- **При 50:** None; a buyer reading the docs gets a wrong fact.
- **Как чиним (уточнённый план):** Change /home/user/workshop/docs/platform/setup.md:18 to: «Проект `workshop`, регион ЕС (AWS eu-west-1, Ирландия).» Optionally add one sentence so the deviation from the research recommendation is explicit and nobody "fixes" it back: «В исследовании (docs/research/platform-security.md §1.3) рекомендовался Frankfurt; фактически проект создан в Ирландии — тоже ЕС, переносить не нужно (смена региона в Supabase = новый проект и миграция данных).» Leave spec.md:8 («регион ЕС») and the research docs as they are (they are historical recommendations, not state). No side effects: nothing in code depends on the region string.
- **Сложность:** small

### AUTH-5 · low · Session cookies are neither httpOnly nor Secure (ssr defaults) although the app has no browser Supabase client and allows 'unsafe-inline' scripts
- **Файлы:** lib/server/supabase/server.ts:18-33, proxy.ts:38-52, node_modules/@supabase/ssr/dist/main/utils/constants.js:4-10, next.config.ts:39
- **Вердикт скептика:** Facts confirmed against the code (confidence high):
- lib/server/supabase/server.ts:18-33 and proxy.ts:41-54 (the finding cites :38-52; createServerClient actually starts at line 41) call createServerClient without `cookieOptions`.
- node_modules/@supabase/ssr@0.12.7 dist/main/utils/constants.js:4-11: DEFAULT_COOKIE_OPTIONS = { path:'/', sameSite:'lax', httpOnly:false, maxAge: 400d }. No `secure` key appears anywhere in @supabase/ssr dist (grep), so the session cookies are emitted without HttpOnly and without Secure.
- The server-client path applies cookieOptions in applyServerStorage, cookies.js:463-471 (`{...DEFAULT_COOKIE_OPTIONS, ...cookieOptions, maxAge}`), called from createServerClient.js:65-68 and cookies.js:378-389. The colleague's reference cookies.js:223-230 is the browser-storage branch (`!isServerClient`), which this app never uses; the mechanism is the same but the citation should be corrected.
- No browser Supabase client exists: grep for createBrowserClient / @supabase/supabase-js / document.cookie outside lib/server returns nothing; the only importers are lib/server/supabase/{server,admin}.ts. Login/refresh/logout are all server-side (app/(platform)/login/actions.ts:50-66, proxy.ts:57). So httpOnly:true cannot break anything the client does.
- next.config.ts:39 confirms `script-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline'` on the platform CSP.
- Inconsistency that supports the finding: the project already sets `httpOnly: true, sameSite: 'lax', secure: !isDevelopment()` on its own bot-session cookie (app/api/b/[slug]/route.ts:78-80), but not on the far more valuable Supabase session cookie.

Why I lower severity from medium to low:
- Rubric: medium = degraded experience, low = polish/hardening. At 10 and at 50 users nothing degrades: no request fails, no star is lost, no job stalls. The risk is purely latent and requires a prerequisite XSS that the code does not currently expose: there is no dangerouslySetInnerHTML, no react-markdown/rehype-raw/marked in package.json, model output is rendered as React text. 'unsafe-inline' only matters once an injection point exists.
- Secure flag: HSTS max-age 2y (next.config.ts:62), `upgrade-insecure-requests` in prod CSP (next.config.ts:50) and Vercel-only HTTPS make the missing Secure attribute belt-and-braces rather than an exploitable gap.
- The colleague's "refresh token never expires" point is Supabase default behaviour (refresh tokens have no expiry, only rotation/reuse detection) and is consistent with maxAge 400d; it raises the value of a stolen cookie but does not change the exploitability.
So: real, cheap, worth doing before the platform is shown to a buyer's security review, but not a lesson or money risk. scenario10/scenario50 as stated by the colleague are accurate ("no functional impact").
- **При 10:** No functional impact; latent risk that a single XSS gives an attacker a permanent session of a student (or the teacher/admin, whose session can create students and move stars).
- **При 50:** Same latent risk, larger blast radius (50 students' long-lived refresh tokens). Not a lesson-breaker, but the owner wants to sell the platform; this is a cheap item that a buyer's security review will flag.
- **Как чиним (уточнённый план):** Keep the colleague's fix but centralise it so both call sites cannot drift, and correct the citations.

1. Add one shared helper, e.g. in lib/server/supabase/cookie-options.ts (or next to isDevelopment in lib/server/env.ts):
   export function sessionCookieOptions() {
     return { httpOnly: true, secure: !isDevelopment(), sameSite: "lax" as const, path: "/" };
   }
   Do NOT set `name` or `domain` (ssr deletes `name` before passing to Next, and `domain` changes chunk-removal scope at cookies.js:476-481).

2. Pass `cookieOptions: sessionCookieOptions()` as a third-arg property in both:
   - lib/server/supabase/server.ts:18 createServerClient<Database>(url, publishableKey, { cookieOptions: ..., cookies: {...} })
   - proxy.ts:41 createServerClient(url, publishableKey, { cookieOptions: ..., cookies: {...} })
   proxy.ts already imports from "@/lib/server/env", so importing isDevelopment there adds no new dependency; env() is zod-parsed and cached, negligible cost per request.

3. Side effects checked:
   - httpOnly: safe, no browser code reads sb-* cookies (grep above). proxy.ts:31 reads them server-side only.
   - secure: must stay false in local `next dev` over http (otherwise login works but the cookie is dropped and the user is bounced to /login). `!isDevelopment()` handles this; it also works for Vercel preview deployments (NODE_ENV=production, https).
   - Existing students' cookies: a Set-Cookie with the same name+path replaces the old cookie and its attributes on the next refresh/login, so the rollout needs no forced logout.
   - Chunked cookies (sb-*-auth-token.0/.1) inherit the same options via applyServerStorage (cookies.js:463-471), both for set and for the maxAge:0 removals on signOut.

4. Verification: in prod, log in and inspect DevTools > Application > Cookies: every sb-* cookie shows HttpOnly ✓ Secure ✓ SameSite=Lax; `document.cookie` in the console no longer lists sb-*; refresh after >1h still works (proxy.ts getClaims path); logout clears all chunks. Locally in dev, confirm login still sticks over http.

5. Optional complementary hardening that attacks the real precondition rather than the symptom: keep model output rendered as text (never add dangerouslySetInnerHTML / rehype-raw for chat/bot replies), and note in docs/platform/spec.md §8 that the CSP is 'unsafe-inline' by necessity (cacheComponents) so output-encoding is the primary XSS control.

Corrected citations for the report: proxy.ts:41-54 (not 38-52); @supabase/ssr cookies.js:463-471 applyServerStorage (not 223-230, which is the unused browser-client branch); add app/api/b/[slug]/route.ts:78-80 as the in-repo precedent.
- **Сложность:** small

### AUTH-6 · low · Logout signs out every device of the login (global scope) — a student using phone + laptop loses both
- **Файлы:** app/(platform)/login/actions.ts:66-72, node_modules/@supabase/auth-js/dist/main/GoTrueClient.js:3417, components/platform/PlatformHeader.tsx:61-70
- **Вердикт скептика:** Confirmed at the code level. `logout()` in /home/user/workshop/app/(platform)/login/actions.ts:66-72 calls `supabase.auth.signOut()` with no options; in the installed auth-js (/home/user/workshop/node_modules/@supabase/auth-js/dist/main/GoTrueClient.js:3417, `async signOut(options = { scope: 'global' })`) the default is `global`, and the JSDoc right above (lines 3384-3388) explicitly warns that this "signs the user out of every device they are currently signed in on". The form in /home/user/workshop/components/platform/PlatformHeader.tsx:61-70 wires the "Выйти" button straight to that action. The other device's behaviour is as described: both proxy.ts:55 and lib/server/dal/auth.ts:58 use `getClaims()`, which accepts the still-valid access JWT until it needs a refresh; supabase/config.toml:40 sets `jwt_expiry = 3600` and `enable_refresh_token_rotation = true` (line 41), so after at most 1 h the refresh fails (token revoked server-side), `getClaims` returns no `sub`, and proxy.ts:58-62 redirects to /login. The cross-device side effect is therefore real, not speculative. I could not independently re-run the auth.sessions query the colleague cites (read-only DB check), but the code path alone is sufficient; the scenario holds regardless of how many sessions users currently have. Impact assessment: the Monday scenario (10 students, 75 min, one device each in a room) is unaffected unless a student logs out on one device while using another; at 50 students the chance of a student with phone + classroom laptop rises, but the consequence is only an unexpected redirect to /login and re-entering the card — no lost data, no money, no broken lesson. Severity "low" is correct. The proposed fix is sound and has no harmful side effects: with `scope: 'local'`, `_signOut` (GoTrueClient.js:3427 ff.) still calls the server logout endpoint for the current session (revoking that device's refresh token) and clears the cookies via @supabase/ssr's setAll (Server Actions can write cookies, per lib/server/supabase/server.ts), so a shared classroom laptop is still fully signed out. Keeping the implicit global scope on the disabled-account branch (actions.ts:59) is right: when a profile is disabled you do want every session of that user revoked. Confidence: high.
- **При 10:** A student logs out on the classroom laptop at the end; their phone, which they use to watch the result later, silently stops working within an hour and asks for the card again.
- **При 50:** Same, more often; also two students sharing one card on two devices (not intended, but happens) log each other out.
- **Как чиним (уточнённый план):** In /home/user/workshop/app/(platform)/login/actions.ts, change `logout()` (line 69) to `await supabase.auth.signOut({ scope: "local" });` and add a one-line comment explaining that 'Выйти' should sign out only this device (auth-js default is 'global'). Leave the disabled-account branch at line 59 as `signOut()` (global) — better still, make the intent explicit with `signOut({ scope: "global" })` and a comment, so a future reader does not "fix" it to local. No change to PlatformHeader.tsx is needed. Document in docs/platform/setup.md (teacher section) that a student card may be used on several devices at once and that 'Выйти' affects only the current device; if the owner instead wants "one card = one active device" as a product rule, that is a separate decision (e.g. `scope: 'others'` at login time) and should be recorded in docs/decisions.md rather than left to the library default. Optional follow-up for the teacher's "disable account" flow: verify it revokes sessions (e.g. via admin API) rather than relying on the student logging in again, since the only global sign-out currently happens on the student's own next login attempt.
- **Сложность:** small

### AUTH-7 · low · Leaked-password protection: status log says ON, live project says OFF — settings and documentation disagree
- **Файлы:** docs/course/770-status.md:13-16, docs/platform/setup.md:20-33, app/(platform)/teach/actions.ts:233-241, app/(platform)/teach/actions.ts:307-308
- **Вердикт скептика:** Confirmed, with one important correction to the fix. (1) Live state: Supabase security advisor for project `workshop` (njmstsfepkrtsixgtbcb), fetched 2026-11-08 during this check, reports `auth_leaked_password_protection` WARN "Leaked password protection is currently disabled". (2) Documentation claims the opposite: /home/user/workshop/docs/course/770-status.md:16 ticks "защита от утёкших паролей включена" under a [x] item, and /home/user/workshop/docs/platform/audit/2026-11-08-findings.md:654 repeats "защита от утёкших паролей включена (770-status.md:13-16)" as evidence. /home/user/workshop/docs/platform/setup.md:20-33 never mentions the setting at all, so the status log is the only source of the claim. (3) The finding's fix ("either enable it … or untick") is half-wrong: enabling is not available on this project. Official Supabase docs (password-security guide, fetched via search_docs): "Leaked password protection is available on the Pro Plan and above." The project's own docs state it runs on Free: setup.md:59 "лимит файла 50 МБ — это потолок бесплатного тарифа", and docs/research/platform-security.md:186 and :1001 already record "Проверка по базе утёкших паролей (HaveIBeenPwned) есть только на Pro" / "Утёкшие пароли (HIBP) | Нет | Да". So the status note is simply false and cannot be made true without upgrading; the correct action is to untick it. (4) Impact re-derived: zero lesson impact at 10 or 50 students — login is password-based with teacher-issued passwords, and the setting only gates password set/update. The password generator (lib/server/accounts.ts:85-88: three dictionary words of 4-9 Latin letters + 2 digits, ≥17 chars, ~200^3*90 ≈ 7e8 combinations of transliterated Russian words) would very rarely hit HIBP, and createStudents already tolerates per-student failures (actions.ts:247-255 → `failed` list); resetStudentPassword (actions.ts:307-309) would surface as `server_error` via fail() at actions.ts:34-38 — only relevant if the owner later moves to Pro and enables it. The colleague's claim that GoTrue's checkPasswordStrength runs in admin createUser/updateUserById (admin.go:239, :453) was not verified here (no GoTrue source in repo); treat as plausible but unconfirmed. Severity low is correct: documentation/configuration drift only, no runtime or money risk. Confidence: high for the disagreement and plan constraint; medium for the GoTrue admin-path detail.
- **При 10:** No lesson impact either way; the risk is only that the owner believes a protection is active when it is not.
- **При 50:** Same. If the owner enables it later, expect rare single-student failures in batch creation (handled) and in password reset (surfaces as 'Что-то пошло не так' — teacher just clicks again).
- **Как чиним (уточнённый план):** 1. In /home/user/workshop/docs/course/770-status.md:16 replace "защита от утёкших паролей включена" with something like "защита от утёкших паролей — недоступна на Free (только Pro), не включена; при переходе на Pro — включить в Authentication → Sign In / Providers → Passwords". Do not leave it under a [x] item. 2. Fix the derived claim in /home/user/workshop/docs/platform/audit/2026-11-08-findings.md:654 so the audit summary no longer cites it as an active control. 3. Add the setting to /home/user/workshop/docs/platform/setup.md §1.1 (around lines 28-31, the Passwords block) as an explicit "Pro only — skip on Free" note so the owner does not look for a toggle that is not there, and so a future center on Pro knows to turn it on. 4. Optional, only if the platform ever runs on Pro with the feature enabled: in resetStudentPassword (actions.ts:307-309) and in the createStudents batch (actions.ts:233-255), on a 422 weak_password/pwned response from Auth, regenerate the password once and retry before recording failure. Not worth doing now on Free — it adds code for a path that cannot trigger. No side effects from steps 1-3 (docs only); step 4 is low-risk but currently dead code.
- **Сложность:** small

### AUTH-8 · low · Login failures and 429s are not logged anywhere — no evidence to debug a bad workshop start
- **Файлы:** app/(platform)/login/actions.ts:49-54, lib/server/audit.ts:30-48
- **Вердикт скептика:** Partly confirmed, partly refuted; severity stays low.

CONFIRMED (code): /home/user/workshop/app/(platform)/login/actions.ts has no console.* at all. The app's own limiter rejection at actions.ts:44-46 returns TOO_MANY *before* Supabase is called, and lib/server/rate-limit.ts:31-46 logs only RPC *failures* (lines 38, 43), never "limit exhausted" (data === false → return false silently). So a student blocked by OUR ip/account limiter leaves no trace anywhere: not in Vercel logs, not in Supabase Auth logs (no request was made), and private.rate_limit_hits (migration 20261105100000:111-149) only stores HMAC keys of accepted hits, not rejections.

REFUTED (evidence): the claim "Unified logs show only pgbouncer/postgres sources, so Vercel function logs are the sole place where auth failures could appear" is wrong. I queried the workshop project (njmstsfepkrtsixgtbcb) unified logs on 2026-11-08: sources present = edge_logs 558, pgbouncer_logs 317, storage_logs 155, postgres_logs 23, auth_logs 10, auth_audit_logs 3, workflow_run_logs 8, realtime_logs 1. auth_logs records every POST /token?grant_type=password with status, auth_event.action, actor_username (synthetic e-mail), remote_addr and request_id (sample: md1-matvey login, status 200, 11:02:47Z). A Supabase 429 (actions.ts:52) and a wrong password (Supabase returns 400 invalid_credentials) both appear there as server-side entries regardless of what the app logs, with 1-day retention on the free plan. So the "measurement is lost" scenario50 for Supabase 429s is false; the only lost measurement is the count of rejections by our own limiter (LOGIN_LIMITS.ip 100/15 min, account 20/15 min).

Also a flaw in the proposed fix: lib/server/audit.ts:7 itself notes Vercel logs live one hour (Hobby runtime-log retention; log drains are Pro-only). A console.error in actions.ts is therefore only useful if the owner is watching Vercel logs *during* the lesson; for post-hoc analysis it is gone by the evening. Writing login outcomes to audit_log from an unauthenticated endpoint is a bad idea: it adds a DB write on the hot path and lets anyone fill the admin-only table by hammering /login.

Scenario at 10 users (same NAT IP, logins within ~2 min): ~10-30 attempts, far below ip 100/15 min and account 20/15 min; our limiter will not fire, so the gap is practically unobservable; Supabase-side outcomes are in auth_logs. Scenario at 50: 50-150 attempts in 2 min from one classroom IP approaches the ip limit of 100/15 min only with heavy retrying; if it fires, nothing records it — that is the one real evidence gap. Confidence: high on code and on the existence/content of auth_logs (verified by query); medium on exact Supabase free-plan log retention (1 day per docs as I recall; not re-verified).
- **При 10:** If a student cannot log in, the owner cannot tell afterwards whether it was a typo, our limiter or Supabase's bucket.
- **При 50:** The first big workshop is the only chance to measure how many server-side sign-ins hit Supabase 429 (unresolved research item); without a log line the measurement is lost.
- **Как чиним (уточнённый план):** 1) In lib/server/rate-limit.ts takeAttempt (line ~41), when `data === false` emit `console.warn("rateLimit: exhausted", { purpose: rule.purpose, limit: rule.limit })` — no login/IP/hash values. This covers the only outcome that is logged nowhere else (our limiter), and purpose already tells 'login-ip' vs 'login-account'. 2) In actions.ts:52, for the Supabase 429 branch add `console.warn("login: supabase 429", { code: error.code })` so the two 429 sources are distinguishable live in Vercel logs; do not log e-mail or password; do not log plain wrong-password (Supabase auth_logs already has it with the attempted username and status 400). 3) Do NOT write login outcomes to audit_log (unauthenticated hot path, table fill risk). 4) Document the runbook in docs/platform/setup.md: after a workshop, open Supabase → Logs → Auth (source auth_logs, filter path /token, status 400/429) within 24 h; Vercel runtime logs on Hobby keep only ~1 h, so watch them during the lesson, not after. Optionally count accepted attempts per window from private.rate_limit_hits (key prefix 'login-ip:'/'login-account:' is plaintext, hashes are not reversible) via SQL editor.
- **Сложность:** small

### AUTH-9 · low · Polish: dead /admin prefix, logged-in user not redirected away from /login, TOO_MANY wording
- **Файлы:** proxy.ts:14, app/(platform)/login/actions.ts:27, app/(platform)/login/page.tsx:10-27, next.config.ts:83, app/(platform)/login/actions.ts:22
- **Вердикт скептика:** Verified in code (confidence high): (1) proxy.ts:14 PROTECTED and :71 matcher include "/admin", next.config.ts:83 platformRoutes includes "/admin" and "/admin/:path*", yet `find` shows no admin route under app/ (only app/(platform)/{app,login,teach}); safeNext (login/actions.ts:27) accepts only /app|/teach, so a /login?next=/admin round-trip ends at homePath(). Effect is a 404 (with session) or login redirect (without) — no student ever hits this path; docs/research/platform-security.md:166 and the audit findings doc both plan an /admin area, so the prefix is forward-compat, not a bug. (2) login/page.tsx:10-27 renders LoginForm unconditionally; no getUser(). However the finding understates what the user sees: PlatformLayout (app/(platform)/layout.tsx) renders PlatformHeader whose UserNav (PlatformHeader.tsx:29-70) shows nickname, balance and a logout button on /login too, so a signed-in student is not misled into thinking they are logged out. A second signInWithPassword simply writes a new sb- cookie via createClient().setAll (server.ts:22-31); the old refresh token row stays in auth.sessions until expiry — harmless, nobody holds that token anymore; no security or money impact. (3) TOO_MANY (actions.ts:22) says "пару минут" while both LOGIN_LIMITS windows are 15*60 s (rate-limit.ts:19,21) and rate_limit_hit is a sliding window (security_hardening.sql:138-149): after a block the real wait is until the oldest hit ages out, 0–15 min. Correction to scenario50: rate_limit_hit returns false BEFORE inserting (sql:146-148), so retries made while already blocked do NOT extend the lockout or consume the IP counter; only retries made before the IP cap is reached count (and note the IP hit is consumed even on successful logins and never reset — resetAttempts is called only for the account key, actions.ts:55). So the wrong wording causes frustration, not a longer lockout. 10 users: no functional impact. 50 users: same; the IP-cap exhaustion itself is a separate finding (AUTH-1). Severity low is correct; the fix as proposed has side effects (see correctedFix).
- **При 10:** Minor confusion only: a student who bookmarked /login re-enters the password unnecessarily.
- **При 50:** Same; the misleading wait time causes repeated retries that consume the IP counter (see AUTH-1).
- **Как чиним (уточнённый план):** (a) /admin: do NOT remove it from proxy.ts PROTECTED/matcher or next.config.ts:83 — it is harmless forward-compat for the planned admin page and removing it means re-adding later; the only consistency fix worth making is adding `admin` to the safeNext regex (actions.ts:27 → /^\/(app|teach|admin)(\/|\?|$)/) when the /admin route lands, so ?next=/admin survives login. (b) /login for a signed-in user: with cacheComponents: true (next.config.ts:86) getUser() awaits cookies()/connection(), so it must run inside a Suspense boundary, not at the top of LoginPage. Add an async child `<Suspense fallback={null}><SignedInGate/></Suspense>` in page.tsx that calls getUser() and, if non-null, either redirect(homePath(user)) or (preferred for a classroom where kids may need to switch accounts on a shared device) renders a Notice "Вы уже вошли как {nickname} — <Link href={homePath(user)}>в кабинет</Link> или выйдите" above the form. No redirect loop is possible: /app→/login only when getUser() is null, /login→/app only when non-null; a disabled profile yields null in both, so the form is shown. (c) TOO_MANY: state the real window — "Слишком много попыток. Подождите до 15 минут и попробуйте снова." (the 429 branch at actions.ts:52 comes from Supabase Auth's own per-IP bucket, so keep the same wording there). Also check lib/platform/messages.ts:45 (`rate_limited`: same "пару минут" text) against the window of the limiter that emits it before copy-pasting.
- **Сложность:** small

### BP-10 · low · Telegram reply path: the owner is charged before the message is sent; if after() is cut by maxDuration, the guest gets nothing but the 1★ stays
- **Файлы:** app/api/tg/[botId]/route.ts:19, app/api/tg/[botId]/route.ts:63-64, lib/server/bot/telegram-flow.ts:194-207, lib/server/bot/brain.ts:202-227, lib/server/gateway.ts:169, lib/server/gateway.ts:187
- **Вердикт скептика:** Partly confirmed, but the trigger is misattributed. CONFIRMED from code: (a) ordering — brain.ts:208-215 spendStars → brain.ts:278-285 insertMessage(assistant) → telegram-flow.ts:82 sendMessage; (b) no refund path for spend_stars — supabase/migrations/20261101200000_init.sql:745-780 only debits (idempotent on key); refund_job is job-only (wallet.ts:98); (c) after() is bounded by maxDuration=60 (route.ts:19; node_modules/next/dist/docs/.../after.md:50,250 via waitUntil). REFUTED as the main mechanism: the maxDuration cut. The charge happens only when generateText returned ok (brain.ts:203-206 returns before spendStars on failure), so the 50 s RETRY_BUDGET_MS (gateway.ts:187) can only be fully consumed on the charging path if the model succeeds at the very edge; worst realistic timeline is ~1-2 s pre-200 (loadBotById, insertMessage) + sendTyping <1 s + ≤50 s model + ~1 s DB + <1 s sendMessage ≈ 54 s.
