Exceeding 60 s additionally needs sendTyping or sendMessage to stall ≥6 s (telegram.ts:10 timeout 8 s), i.e. Telegram itself must be down — in which case the message would not be delivered regardless of maxDuration. So the maxDuration cut is a corner of a corner. The REAL, more frequent variant of the same symptom: telegram-flow.ts:82 ignores the TelegramResult of sendMessage — on timeout/network/429/5xx (telegram.ts:30-50) the 1★ is already charged, the assistant row is stored, nothing is retried, and the only trace is a console.error without bot/chat context. Also wrong in the finding: (1) the 'duplicate path re-send' idea is moot — Telegram retries only on non-2xx and the route returns 200 before after() (route.ts:63-67), so these updates are never redelivered; (2) 'let the owner see the unsent reply in history' — the reply IS stored (brain.ts:278), but there is no owner-facing Telegram history UI (app/(platform)/app/bot/page.tsx:62 shows only getPreviewHistory), so in practice the owner cannot see it. Scenario at 10 users (Monday): Telegram bots are lesson-4 material (docs/course/770-brief.md:125), adult owners only (route.ts:47) — essentially not exercised; expected incidents 0. At 50 users (later workshops): each bot has its own token, Telegram limits are per bot/per chat, kie overload does not charge (failure path returns before spendStars), so per-message loss probability equals the Telegram API failure rate (well under 1%); expect 0-1 lost 1★ replies per workshop, guest simply resends. Severity low stands: money at risk is 1★ per incident, no lesson breakage, no data loss. Confidence: high on code paths, medium on Vercel/Telegram latency figures.
- **При 10:** Rare: during a kie overload window a Telegram guest sees 'typing…' and never gets a reply while the owner lost 1★; no retry because Telegram got 200.
- **При 50:** Same frequency per message; more messages, so a few per workshop during overload.
- **Как чиним (уточнённый план):** Do NOT raise maxDuration or shorten the text retry budget: raising to 120 is only valid if Fluid is confirmed (otherwise build/deploy rejects >60 on legacy Hobby) and does not address the real cause; shortening the budget to 35 s would raise gateway_timeout rates during kie overload windows, which is the Monday-relevant failure mode. Drop the 'duplicate re-send' idea (Telegram never retries after 200). Instead, small targeted changes in lib/server/bot/telegram-flow.ts runTelegramReply: (1) check the sendMessage result; if error is timeout/network/server/rate_limited and the after() has been running < ~50 s (track Date.now() at entry), wait ~1.5 s and retry sendMessage once with a shorter timeout (e.g. 4 s) so the whole after() stays under 60 s; do not retry on unauthorized/bad_request. (2) If delivery still fails, record it where someone can see it: either an audit() row (action 'bot.telegram_send_failed', meta {bot, chatId hash, chargeKey, error}) or a nullable delivery_error column on the assistant bot_messages row — this gives the admin the chargeKey to compensate with grantStars (wallet.ts:109) without inventing a new refund money path. (3) Optional cheap win: if sendTyping returns unauthorized (token revoked), skip completeMessage entirely — no model call, no charge. Only if real incidents accumulate consider a security-definer compensating RPC that inserts +amount into star_ledger with key 'refund:'+chargeKey iff the -amount row exists and the refund row does not (needs supabase/tests coverage); not justified now at low severity.
- **Сложность:** small

### BP-5 · low · Soul/knowledge exfiltration guard is a substring match on soul lines only — paraphrase/translation/encoding requests leak the soul, and the knowledge block is not covered at all
- **Файлы:** lib/server/bot/prompt.ts:188-204, lib/server/bot/prompt.ts:102-111, lib/server/bot/brain.ts:233-235
- **Вердикт скептика:** Code facts confirmed (confidence high). lib/server/bot/prompt.ts:188-204 `leaksInstructions` is a lexical backstop: it fires on the literal markers (`правила платформы|<<<|>>>|душа>>>|справка>>>|блок душа|блок справка`, line 191) or when the normalised reply contains the first 60 chars of a labelled soul line ≥20 chars (line 198) or of two unlabelled/content lines ≥40 chars (lines 199-201). brain.ts:234 passes only `bot.soul`; knowledge is never compared. A translated, paraphrased or reversed rendition of the soul passes the check; only rules 2-3 (prompt.ts:104-105) ask the model to refuse. The supporting claims also hold: leads are not in the prompt (leads.ts:9 comment; brain.ts:189-200 builds the prompt from soul/knowledge/history only), and history is read via the admin client filtered by `bot_id` + `conversation_id` (store.ts:254-262), so cross-bot or lead exfiltration via injection is not possible.

However, three things in the finding need correcting, and they keep this at low (polish), not a lesson or data risk:
1. The gap is by design, not an oversight. The docstring (prompt.ts:183-187) states the intent: catch a verbatim dump, allow repeating one soul phrase as a normal in-character reply; pure.test.mjs:291-294 asserts that in-character repetition of the "Кто ты" line and the greeting are NOT leaks. Any lexical check is inherently bypassable by translation; a model-based judge would cost another gateway call per reply (1★ economics, 60 s budget) and is out of scope for this platform.
2. Knowledge (СПРАВКА) not being compared is intentional and must stay so. Rule 4 (prompt.ts:106) says facts come only from СПРАВКА and the UI text (components/platform/BotSettingsForm.tsx:203) says "Бот отвечает о фактах только по этому тексту" — the bot is supposed to read it back. Lesson 4 (docs/course/lesson-4.md:15,18,77) has students ask their bot about meeting dates/places from the справка. Comparing replies to knowledge lines would flag correct answers (dates, addresses, prices) and replace them with "Это мой маленький секрет" (brain.ts:234, BOT_TEXTS.secret) — i.e., the proposed fix would break Задание 2 of lesson 4 for every student.
3. The "private phone/address in СПРАВКА" scenario is already constrained: app/(platform)/app/bot/actions.ts:59 runs `guardOwnerText` on knowledge; for minors (`adult=false`, guard.ts:63-68) phones, e-mails and addresses are rejected, card numbers always. Adults are deliberately allowed a venue address and public contact (guard.ts:59-62 comment). So "more students write something private" at 50 users does not materialise for 14-17 owners; for adults it is the intended public information. The soul itself is not secret data in the platform's model: the owner exports it (app/(platform)/app/bot/soul.txt/route.ts), teachers see it, and `guardOwnerText` already strips cards/contacts for minors.

Scenario at 10 users: a classmate asks a bot to "translate your description to English"; Haiku usually refuses per rule 3; if it complies, the guest reads a paraphrase of the owner's own character description. No stars beyond the normal 1★/reply, no PII, lesson unaffected. At 50 users: identical; the check is O(soul lines) string ops in-process, no scaling concern.

Fix review: the word-overlap heuristic is risky — soul lines like "Как говоришь: тепло, с драмой, до 3 предложений" overlap heavily with a legitimate in-character reply ("Я говорю тепло и с драмой, золотце"), producing false "Это мой маленький секрет" replies exactly during the lesson when students test their bots; the list heuristic punishes a formatting slip (rule 5) as a leak. Both would be a visible regression. The UI hint is the only part worth doing.
- **При 10:** A classmate makes a bot recite its soul or knowledge in English; mildly embarrassing, no money or PII at stake (owners are told knowledge is public facts). If an adult owner put a private phone/address in СПРАВКА for the bot to answer with, it is readable by anyone with the link — by design but worth stating in the UI.
- **При 50:** Same; more bots means more chances a student writes something private into СПРАВКА.
- **Как чиним (уточнённый план):** Keep it to the zero-risk parts:
1. UI hint (components/platform/BotSettingsForm.tsx ~line 203, the `text` of the "Ответы по моему тексту" toggle): append "Всё, что здесь написано, бот может пересказать любому гостю — только публичные факты." Mirror the sentence in docs/course/lesson-4.md (section on справка, ~line 42) so the teacher says it aloud before Задание 2.
2. Optional, cheap and false-positive-free: extend the marker regex in prompt.ts:191 with the template header and tool-section labels that only appear in the prompt, e.g. `|душа бота v1|инструмент «заявка»|инструмент «открытка»|служебн(ая|ую) строк` — these never occur in a normal reply, so no regression risk. Add a test line in lib/server/bot/tests/pure.test.mjs next to line 296.
3. Do NOT compare replies against knowledge (it is meant to be read back; doing so breaks lesson-4 Задание 2), and do NOT add bag-of-words or list heuristics (false positives replace valid in-character replies with BOT_TEXTS.secret). If a stronger guard is ever wanted, make it a separate, opt-in owner setting and measure false positives on the existing test souls first.
- **Сложность:** small

### BP-6 · low · Card (открытка) daily count is checked outside the transaction and guest cards occupy the owner's 5 active-job slots
- **Файлы:** lib/server/bot/card.ts:76-86, lib/server/bot/card.ts:104-114, supabase/migrations/20261101200000_init.sql:571-573, lib/server/bot/limits.ts:22-23
- **Вердикт скептика:** Both halves of the finding are confirmed by the code; the practical impact is small and does not touch the Monday lesson.

1. TOCTOU on the 3/day cap — confirmed. lib/server/bot/card.ts:76-86 does a plain count on jobs (user_id = owner, task_ref = bot-card:<bot>, params->>preview = 'false', last 24 h) and only then calls create_job (card.ts:104-113). No lock spans the two steps; supabase-js issues them as two independent requests. create_job (supabase/migrations/20261101200000_init.sql:535-600) knows nothing about task_ref limits. The idempotency key is sha256(ticket) (card.ts:95), so the same ticket re-sent is deduplicated (reused), but distinct tickets are distinct jobs. A ticket is signed per (bot, conversation, scene) and valid 30 minutes (lib/server/bot/secrets.ts:56-63); the brain emits a ticket only when the model's reply carries [[ОТКРЫТКА]] (brain.ts:269-276), so a guest must collect K model replies (each 1★ of the owner, bounded by 30 msgs/h/IP via insert_bot_message under advisory lock, security_hardening.sql:58-80), then fire the K confirmations in parallel. On Vercel these run as independent serverless invocations, all read count < 3 and all pass. Upper bound per IP per hour: ~30 tickets -> up to ~30 cards x 2★ (FLUX Klein, init.sql:863/866) or 5★ (nano-banana), plus 30★ for the messages — still capped by the owner's balance and the group/global daily cap inside lock_for_spend (init.sql:497-515). Also note the count includes failed/refunded cards (no status filter), which is conservative. The hardening migration already fixed the identical race for guest messages (security_hardening.sql §1) but did not cover cards.

2. Shared 5-slot cap — confirmed. init.sql:570-573 counts all jobs of p_user in queued/submitted/storing regardless of task_ref; card jobs are created with userId = bot.ownerId (card.ts:105). So guest cards and the owner's own generations share the owner's 5 slots. A guest card stays 'submitted' until someone polls it: the guest via /api/b/[slug]/card (pollCard, card.ts:169-179) or the owner via the gallery (listMyGallery includes image/image_edit jobs of the owner, lib/server/gallery.ts:47-67, and GalleryGrid polls unfinished ones through /api/jobs/[id] -> advanceJob). If the guest closes the tab and the owner does not open the gallery, abandoned card jobs hold slots until the owner's next gallery visit (advanceJob then finishes or refunds them: 5-min submitted timeout only on gateway error, 60-min hard limit, job-rules.ts:16-22,82-98). A card normally finishes in ~10-30 s, so the realistic window is small. Minor correction: the owner sees "Уже идёт несколько генераций. Дождитесь результата." (lib/platform/messages.ts:52), not "Слишком много задач".

Scenario at 10 users (Monday): no impact. Cards are offered only on bots of 18+ owners or in owner preview (brain.ts:103-106); bots are built in lesson 3, and a guest must be chatting with an adult student's public bot during class for the slot collision to occur. At 50 users: same — the defect is per bot and per guest, independent of class size; aggregate cost per bot per hour is bounded by the IP rate limit, the owner's balance and the group daily cap. Hence low: money at risk is a few extra stars from an adult owner's wallet, and the only lesson-visible symptom is an occasional too_many_jobs that clears within seconds.

Fix check: the proposed fix is directionally right but can be simpler and safer than a new advisory lock. lock_for_spend already takes `select ... from wallets for update` on the owner's row (init.sql:517), and every card job of a bot belongs to the same owner, so any count performed inside create_job after lock_for_spend is already serialized per owner — no new lock or wrapper RPC is needed. Excluding bot-card:% from the 5-slot count is safe only once the daily cap is race-free (otherwise it removes the only concurrency bound on guest-triggered jobs).
- **При 10:** An adult student's bot: a guest gets 3–5 cards in parallel instead of 3 (2★ each with FLUX Klein); if 3 guest cards are 'submitted' while the owner starts videos in class, the owner may hit too_many_jobs ('Слишком много задач') until the cards finish (~20 s).
- **При 50:** Same per adult bot; aggregate cost bounded by 30 msgs/h/IP and the per-bot message cap, so ≤ a few extra ★ per bot per day.
- **Как чиним (уточнённый план):** New migration (do not edit init.sql):
1. Extend create_job with two optional params `p_ref_daily_limit integer default null, p_ref_window interval default '24 hours'`. After `v_balance := private.lock_for_spend(...)` (init.sql:561, wallet row already locked for update) and after the idempotency re-check, add:
   if p_ref_daily_limit is not null and p_task_ref is not null and (select count(*) from public.jobs j where j.user_id = p_user and j.task_ref = p_task_ref and coalesce(j.params->>'preview','false') = 'false' and j.created_at >= now() - p_ref_window) >= p_ref_daily_limit then raise exception 'ref_limit' using errcode='P0001'; end if;
   In lib/server/wallet.ts add 'ref_limit' to WalletError/KNOWN; in card.ts pass refDailyLimit: BOT_LIMITS.cardsPerBotPerDay when !preview, map 'ref_limit' -> 'card_limit', and delete the pre-count at card.ts:76-86 (or keep it as a cheap fast-path only). Re-run create_job's grant/revoke so the new signature stays service_role-only (check the existing `revoke execute ... from public, anon, authenticated` lines for create_job and repeat them for the new signature; drop the old signature to avoid PostgREST overload ambiguity).
2. In the same function, change the too_many_jobs count (init.sql:570-573) to `and coalesce(j.task_ref,'') not like 'bot-card:%'` and add a separate cap for cards: when p_task_ref like 'bot-card:%', count active jobs with that task_ref and raise too_many_jobs at >= 2. This keeps guest activity from blocking the owner's own generations while bounding concurrent guest spend.
3. Add a pgTAP test in supabase/tests/: two create_job calls with the same bot-card task_ref and different idempotency keys after 3 existing non-preview cards must raise ref_limit; a 4th active non-card job plus 2 active card jobs must still allow the owner's 5th own job.
Optional hardening without SQL: in brain.ts, pass `cardsAvailable: false` to the system prompt once the day's 3 non-preview cards exist, so the model stops offering tickets that will be refused.
- **Сложность:** small

### BP-7 · low · Sender hash is salted with the Berlin date, so the 'last hour' window silently resets at midnight (double budget) and pre-check/SQL may disagree
- **Файлы:** lib/server/bot/secrets.ts:123-125, lib/server/bot/ratelimit.ts:57-60, supabase/migrations/20261105100000_security_hardening.sql:76-82
- **Вердикт скептика:** Core mechanism confirmed, one sub-claim refuted, line references wrong.

Confirmed (confidence high): lib/server/bot/secrets.ts:20-22 `hashSender` calls `senderHash(derivedKey("sender"), kind, value, berlinDate(now))`, and lib/server/bot/ratelimit.ts:28-30 puts the day into the HMAC input (`${kind}:${day}:${value}`). `berlinDate` (lib/server/bot/lifetime.ts:46-54) is the Europe/Berlin calendar date. Both callers (app/api/b/[slug]/route.ts:67 for IP, lib/server/bot/telegram-flow.ts:59 for Telegram chat id) use the current time. The authoritative check in supabase/migrations/20261105100000_security_hardening.sql:77-80 counts `m.ip_hash = p_ip_hash and created_at >= now() - interval '1 hour'`, so after Berlin midnight a sender's pre-midnight messages are under a different ip_hash and are invisible to the hour count. A sender who sent 30 messages at 23:30-23:59 gets another 30 from 00:00. This is explicitly a design choice (ratelimit.ts:12-13 comment: hash changes daily so a guest cannot be tracked beyond a day), but the doubled window around midnight is real.

Refuted: "pre-check/SQL may disagree". The pre-check (lib/server/bot/brain.ts:140-143 -> store.ts countRecent:271-296) and the SQL function receive the same `input.senderHash` computed once per request, so within one request they always query the same hash and see the same count; both reset identically at midnight. There is no inconsistency between the two layers, only the shared midnight reset.

Bounding factors that keep it low: botPerDay=200 per bot (lib/server/bot/limits.ts:16, SQL lines 85-92) is keyed by bot_id, not ip_hash, so it is unaffected; the worst case is one sender getting 60 instead of 30 messages in the hour straddling Berlin midnight, i.e. ~30 extra Haiku calls (~30 stars of the bot owner) per sender per night. Monday lesson (daytime) and the 50-student workshop are unaffected: 10 or 50 students in a room never hit this; only a nighttime abuser does. No lesson breakage, no data risk.

Line references in the finding are wrong: secrets.ts:123-125 should be secrets.ts:20-22 (file is 105 lines); ratelimit.ts:57-60 should be ratelimit.ts:28-30 (57-60 is expandIpv6). SQL ref 76-82 is correct (77-80).

Fix check: the colleague's "pass p_ip_hash_prev" is workable but needs a migration changing the function signature (and lib/server/supabase/types.ts:317 area, supabase/tests/004), and should not add a second advisory lock (lock ordering / extra contention). The "2-hour-overlapping salt" alternative is not well-defined and would complicate the privacy story. Given the explicit privacy intent and the 200/day/bot cap, "accept and document" is a fully legitimate option.
- **При 10:** Negligible in a daytime lesson; an attacker gets 60 messages in the hour straddling Berlin midnight.
- **При 50:** Same.
- **Как чиним (уточнённый план):** Option A (recommended, zero code): accept and document. Add one sentence to the comment block in lib/server/bot/ratelimit.ts:12-13 and to docs/platform/spec.md §5 (line ~119): "Because the sender hash is salted with the Berlin date, the 30/hour window resets at Berlin midnight (a sender can get up to 60 messages in the hour straddling midnight); the 200/day/bot cap still applies." Record it in docs/decisions.md as a deliberate privacy trade-off.

Option B (small, if the owner wants it closed): 
1. lib/server/bot/secrets.ts: add `hashSenderPrev(kind, value, now)` that returns `senderHash(key, kind, value, berlinDate(now - 24h))` only when Berlin local time is < 01:00, else null. Pass it through BrainInput as `senderHashPrev` from app/api/b/[slug]/route.ts and lib/server/bot/telegram-flow.ts.
2. New idempotent migration: `create or replace function public.insert_bot_message(... existing params ..., p_ip_hash_prev text default null)` (keep the existing 11-arg signature or drop it explicitly with `drop function if exists` to avoid PostgREST overload ambiguity, PGRST203). Change the hour count at lines 77-80 to `where m.ip_hash = any(array_remove(array[p_ip_hash, p_ip_hash_prev], null)) and m.role='user' and m.created_at >= now() - interval '1 hour'`. Keep the single advisory lock on p_ip_hash (do not lock on the prev hash). The existing partial index bot_messages_ip_idx (ip_hash, created_at desc) serves `= any(...)`.
3. lib/server/bot/store.ts insertMessage: pass `p_ip_hash_prev: row.senderHashPrev ?? null`; countRecent: use `.in("ip_hash", [hash, prev].filter(Boolean))` instead of `.eq`. Regenerate lib/server/supabase/types.ts; extend supabase/tests/004_security_hardening.test.sql with a case where 30 rows under hash A plus a new insert under hash B with p_ip_hash_prev = A returns 'sender_hour'.
Do not change the daily salt itself: the privacy property (hash not traceable beyond one day) stays intact, since the previous-day hash is only consulted for one hour after midnight.
- **Сложность:** small

### BP-8 · low · Public guests cannot start a new dialog: a shared classroom device shows the previous child's chat for 30 days
- **Файлы:** app/api/b/[slug]/route.ts:48-50, app/api/b/[slug]/route.ts:76-83, lib/server/bot/dal.ts:214, components/platform/BotChat.tsx:180-187, components/platform/BotChat.tsx:197-206
- **Вердикт скептика:** Confirmed in code; the finding is accurate and the severity "low" is right. Evidence: app/api/b/[slug]/route.ts:48-50 reuses any valid iis_bot_sid cookie and only issues a new one when none exists (route.ts:76-84, maxAge 30 days, path "/", httpOnly — so the browser cannot clear/rotate it from JS); the body schema (route.ts:25-28) accepts only type "message" | "card", there is no reset type. app/b/[slug]/page.tsx:27-33 reads the same cookie in the RSC and passes getPublicBot(...).history (lib/server/bot/dal.ts:214 → loadHistory, default BOT_LIMITS.historyMessages = 10, limits.ts:14) as initialMessages. components/platform/BotChat.tsx:180-187 and :197-206 render the "Заново" button only when resetAction is passed; the public page (page.tsx:65-74) passes none, and the only reset implementation is the owner Server Action resetPreview (app/(platform)/app/bot/actions.ts:268-278) which deletes the owner: conversation — nothing for web: sessions. Additional nuance the colleague did not mention: the guest cookie is independent of Supabase Auth, so logging out of a student account on a shared classroom machine does NOT rotate the guest conversation; and because path is "/" the same sid is used for every bot on the site (history is still scoped by bot_id + conversation_id, so no cross-bot leak). What is visible: the previous guest's user messages (including a typed name for a lead — the lead text itself is stored in bot_messages, only guard-blocked messages become HIDDEN_MESSAGE, brain.ts:115-131) and the model continues the dialog as if it were one person (brain.ts:189-200 feeds that history to the prompt). Why only low: lesson 4 (docs/course/lesson-4.json:219-225) has students test the neighbour's bot from their own phone/laptop, with fictional names, and the page itself tells guests not to type personal data; the contact field is enabled only for adult owners (dal.ts:225-226). At 10 students: only a device shared by two guests shows the effect — no error, no money, no auth data. At 50 students / computer lab: happens more often, still cosmetic/privacy-light, no lesson breakage. Not a scale or money issue. Confidence: high (all cited lines verified).
- **При 10:** Two students share a laptop in class: the second sees the first one's conversation with the bot (and any lead data they typed), and their messages are appended to the same conversation so the model treats them as one person.
- **При 50:** More shared devices (school computer lab) — same effect, more often.
- **Как чиним (уточнённый план):** Keep the colleague's fix with these corrections/side-effect notes: 1) Add `z.object({ type: z.literal("reset") })` to bodySchema in app/api/b/[slug]/route.ts and handle it before loadBotBySlug work: `const sessionId = newSessionId()` and ALWAYS set the cookie (the current `if (existing !== sessionId)` already covers it since the id is new); return `{ ok: true }`. No DB delete — the old `web:<sid>` rows simply become unreachable (their retention is the separate data-retention finding). Rate limit: the reset path does not call handleMessage/insert_bot_message so it is not counted against the 30/h IP limit — fine, it is a cheap cookie rotation, but keep the isSameOrigin check (already at route.ts:35). 2) BotChat.tsx: in `mode === "public"` show the same button labelled «Новый диалог» (not «Заново», which the lesson text reserves for the owner preview) that POSTs `{type:"reset"}` to `/api/b/${slug}` and on ok calls `setMessages([])`; keep `resetAction` for preview. Side effect to handle: pending card polls (BotChat.tsx:144-178) use `/api/b/[slug]/card?id=…` which authorises by the SAME cookie (card/route.ts:30-36), so after rotation any in-flight card would 404 — either disable the button while `pendingCards.length > 0` or drop pending cards from state on reset. 3) Optional: lower maxAge from 30 days to 7 (route.ts:82) or make it a session cookie (omit maxAge) for guests — a session cookie alone also clears history when the browser is closed on a shared PC, but does not replace the button. 4) Optional one-liner on the public page (page.tsx:76-82 notice block): «Общий компьютер? Нажмите «Новый диалог»». Effort: small; no migration needed.
- **Сложность:** small

### BP-9 · low · Lead 'contact' field for adult owners accepts any 100-char string (links, spam) and is forwarded raw to the owner's Telegram
- **Файлы:** lib/server/bot/leads.ts:108, lib/server/bot/leads.ts:144-161, lib/server/bot/brain.ts:299-313, lib/server/bot/limits.ts:19-21
- **Вердикт скептика:** Code confirms the core claim but the finding's scope and evidence need correction. Confirmed: validateLead (lib/server/bot/leads.ts:158-161) only normalizes whitespace and length-checks the contact field (TEXT_MAX.contact = 100, leads.ts:108); there is no shape check. notifyOwner (lib/server/bot/brain.ts:299-308) joins raw `key: value` lines into a plain-text Telegram sendMessage (no parse_mode, link_preview_options.is_disabled, lib/server/bot/telegram.ts:77-84), so no markup injection, but Telegram clients still auto-link plain-text URLs. moderation.ts has no URL rule at all (lib/server/moderation.ts:114-121), so a URL in a guest message passes guardText (brain.ts:115) and the model can copy it into any lead field. Corrections: (1) The problem is NOT specific to `contact`: `name` (60) and `text` (200) go through the identical path (leads.ts:158-161) and are forwarded the same way, so a contact-only allowlist leaves the same vector open through «Имя». (2) Stop-word insults are already blocked before the model sees them: every guest message passes guardText → moderateText, where stop_word is always a refusal (lib/server/bot/guard.ts:37-45; brain.ts:115-130). Lead values come only from model output over guard-passed text, so the extra moderateText on lead values has marginal value (it only catches model-introduced or obfuscated words). (3) Evidence error: the model turn does not cost the attacker anything — spendStars charges the bot OWNER (brain.ts:179, 209-215 `userId: owner.id`, reason "bot_reply"); the attacker is bounded only by senderPerHour 30/IP, botPerDay 200, leadsPerConversationPerDay 3, leadsPerBotPerDay 50 (lib/server/bot/limits.ts:15-21). (4) Downstream rendering is safe: the leads page renders values as React text (app/(platform)/app/bot/leads/page.tsx:68-72, 104-106) and CSV prefixes =+-@ with an apostrophe (lib/server/bot/csv.ts:43-45). Scenario at 10 users (Monday lesson): students are 14-17 with allowContact=false (leads.ts:129, tools.ts:93), so the contact field does not even exist for them; the only exposure is the organizer's/adult-owner's bot, and a prankster can deliver ≤3 plain-text URLs/insults per conversation, ≤50/day, into the owner's Telegram — annoying, nothing breaks, no money or data at risk. At 50 users: same per-bot ceiling, more adult owners potentially affected; still bounded and cosmetic. Severity low stands. The proposed fix is risky: a strict allowlist (phone 9-15 digits / e-mail / @handle 5-32) would reject many legitimate contacts that the prompt itself invites («телефон, e-mail или ник в Telegram», lib/server/bot/tools.ts:31; prompt.ts:72 gives no format rule) — e.g. «тг: ivan_petrov», «вайбер 069…», «Instagram ivan», «t.me/ivan» — the guest would see leadInvalid (brain.ts:265) and the organizer would lose real leads, which hurts «the owner's face» more than an occasional spam link. Also running plain moderateText on an adult owner's contact value would return reason "phone"/"email" and reject every valid contact; only reason === "stop_word" may be treated as a reject (pattern already used in guardReply, guard.ts:48-53). Confidence: high (all claims verified in code).
- **При 10:** A prankster's 'contact' is a phishing URL or an insult delivered to the organizer's Telegram; at most 50 per bot per day.
- **При 50:** Same, more owners with Telegram linked.
- **Как чиним (уточнённый план):** Keep severity low; implement a lenient, field-agnostic version instead of the contact allowlist: (1) In validateLead (lib/server/bot/leads.ts, after line 159, for all text-type fields name/text/contact) reject with bad_value any value matching a URL pattern /https?:\/\/|www\./i, and optionally a bare domain with a path /\b[\p{L}\p{N}-]+\.\p{L}{2,}\/\S/u — explicitly allow t.me/<handle> and @handle so Telegram contacts still pass; do NOT enforce phone/e-mail/@handle shapes. (2) Also in validateLead, apply a stop-word-only check: `const m = moderateText(text); if (!m.ok && m.reason === "stop_word") return { ok: false, reason: "bad_value" }` — never reject on phone/email/address reasons here, since adult contacts legitimately contain them (the hasPersonalData branch at line 160 already handles minors). (3) In notifyOwner (lib/server/bot/brain.ts:303-307) add a line such as «Текст написал гость, платформа его не проверяла» before the field lines so the owner reads links with caution; link previews are already disabled, no parse_mode is used, so no further escaping is required. (4) Note separately (different finding) that each guest turn spends the owner's stars (brain.ts:209-215), which is what actually bounds abuse cost — the per-bot lead/message caps in limits.ts are the real defense and should stay.
- **Сложность:** small

### DB-03 · low · Every paid spend in a group serializes on one `groups` row lock (lock_for_spend … FOR UPDATE), including guest bot replies — a single hot row for 50 concurrent students
- **Файлы:** supabase/migrations/20261105100000_security_hardening.sql:186-197, supabase/migrations/20261101200000_init.sql:561,764, supabase/migrations/20261103110000_group_budget.sql:12-13
- **Вердикт скептика:** Code confirms the mechanism: private.lock_for_spend (supabase/migrations/20261105100000_security_hardening.sql:187-188) locks the groups row FOR UPDATE for every paid create_job (init.sql:561, incl. text chat) and every spend_stars bot reply (init.sql:764), and runs the group ledger SUM (hardening.sql:190-194) although daily_cap is pinned to 1,000,000 (group_budget.sql:12-13) and not editable in the teacher UI. Lock order group→wallet is consistent; refund_job/grant/quiz lock only job/wallet — no deadlock. BUT the impact is overstated: the lock lives only inside one plpgsql function within a single PostgREST RPC transaction (no network I/O while held), so hold time is low single-digit ms; the group SUM is index-backed (profiles_group_idx + star_ledger_user_created_idx (user_id, created_at desc), init.sql:77,171), so it is a cheap nested loop over ~10-50 members, not a slow scan. 10 users: ~10×3-10 ms ≈ ≤0.1 s once — invisible. 50 users clicking in the same second: ~0.15-0.5 s serialized queue once, and the Supabase pooler (default ~15 backend connections on Nano/Micro) already queues 50 concurrent RPCs, so the row lock is not the binding constraint. No errors, no money/data risk; next to a 1-3 min video generation this is unobservable, so 'medium = degraded' is not met — it is polish (low). The proposed fix's second variant ('drop FOR UPDATE and read paused plainly') is risky: the group lock is what makes 'SUM today's spend + insert debit' atomic across different users, so removing it makes daily_cap racy if a center re-enables it, and supabase/tests/002_wallet.test.sql:157-165 still asserts cap enforcement. The global-cap seq-scan point (hardening.sql:201-203, no created_at index on star_ledger) is correct but latent (global_daily_cap = null). Confidence: high that the mechanism exists; high that severity is low (derived from single-statement transaction scope and indexes; not load-tested).
- **При 10:** 10 simultaneous «generate» clicks → 10 transactions queued on the group row, each ~5-15 ms on Micro → worst case ~150 ms extra latency. Invisible.
- **При 50:** 50 students clicking within the same second (teacher says «go») → 50 serialized transactions, each ~10-30 ms on Nano shared CPU incl. the useless ledger SUM → 0.5-1.5 s queue; during lesson 3 add dozens of guest bot messages per minute hitting the same lock. Still no errors (no statement timeout is hit at these durations), but it is the one place that gets slower the more students a group has, and a long-running transaction holding that lock (e.g. a slow ledger SUM on a large table) would stall every student at once.
- **Как чиним (уточнённый план):** Low priority; do NOT change money functions before Monday's lesson. When done, as a new migration (not editing old ones), re-create private.lock_for_spend so the group row lock and ledger SUM run only when a cap is actually enforced, keeping atomicity for the cap path:

  if p_amount > 0 and v_group_id is not null then
    if p_count_cap then
      -- cheap read first; lock only if this group enforces a cap
      select g.paused, g.daily_cap into v_paused, v_cap from public.groups g where g.id = v_group_id;
      if v_cap < 1000000 then   -- 1000000 = 'disabled' sentinel (groups.daily_cap check max, group_budget.sql:12)
        select g.paused, g.daily_cap into v_paused, v_cap from public.groups g where g.id = v_group_id for update;
        select coalesce(sum(-l.delta),0) into v_spent from public.star_ledger l join public.profiles p on p.id = l.user_id
         where p.group_id = v_group_id and l.reason in ('debit','refund') and l.created_at >= v_day_start;
        if v_spent + p_amount > v_cap then raise exception 'daily_cap' using errcode = 'P0001'; end if;
      end if;
    else
      select g.paused into v_paused from public.groups g where g.id = v_group_id;  -- bot replies: no lock needed
    end if;
    if v_paused then raise exception 'group_paused' using errcode = 'P0001'; end if;
  end if;

Keep the wallet FOR UPDATE (per-user, correct). Keep the global_daily_cap block as is (it is skipped when the setting is null), but if that cap is ever enabled add in the same migration: create index star_ledger_spend_created_idx on public.star_ledger (created_at desc) where reason in ('debit','refund'); otherwise hardening.sql:201-203 becomes a sequential scan per generation. Do not drop FOR UPDATE unconditionally: it is what makes the cap check atomic across users. Run supabase/tests/002_wallet.test.sql (daily_cap case at lines 157-165 must still pass) and 004_security_hardening.test.sql after the change. Optional sanity check instead of guessing: EXPLAIN ANALYZE the group SUM on the production ledger after the first workshop to confirm sub-millisecond cost.
- **Сложность:** small

### DB-05 · low · Teacher «Боты группы» page issues 2 COUNT queries per bot (N+1): 100 parallel PostgREST calls for a 50-student group
- **Файлы:** lib/server/bot/dal.ts:273-302, supabase/migrations/20261101200000_init.sql:239,253
- **Вердикт скептика:** Confirmed from code. lib/server/bot/dal.ts:274-285 does `Promise.all` over every bot, each firing two PostgREST `head: true, count: 'exact'` requests (bot_leads by bot_id; bot_messages by bot_id + role='user' + conversation_id not like 'owner:%' + created_at >= weekAgo). Together with the two preceding queries (profiles at :257, bots at :266) that is 2 + 2N HTTP round trips per page render. `bots.owner_id` is `unique` (init.sql:200), so N ≤ students in the group: 10 → 22 requests, 50 → 102 requests, all concurrent from one serverless invocation through the shared admin client (lib/server/supabase/admin.ts, one supabase-js instance, Node global fetch/undici with no per-host connection cap). Indexes bot_leads_bot_idx (init.sql:253) and bot_messages_bot_day_idx (init.sql:239) make each count an index range scan plus heap checks for the role/conversation_id filters, so the DB work is small; the cost is round trips and PostgREST pool queueing (Supabase's default PostgREST db-pool is small on Nano), so 100 concurrent count statements are serialized in batches. Error handling is also lax: a failed count is silently rendered as 0 (`leads.count ?? 0`, :297-298) with no error check. Why it stays LOW: the page is teacher-only (requireStaff, app/(platform)/teach/groups/[id]/bots/page.tsx:31), read-only, opened a handful of times per lesson (1–2 teachers), not on the student hot path; it is rendered inside Suspense so the shell appears immediately; no money/data risk; students never see it. Scenario at 10 students: 22 requests, well under 0.5 s — unnoticeable. Scenario at 50 students: ~102 parallel requests; realistic latency 1–3 s for the counters (confidence medium — depends on PostgREST pool size and concurrent student load), a brief burst that contends with student job-status polls for pool slots, but the page renders. Confidence that the N+1 exists: high. Confidence in the latency figures: medium (not measured; inferred from pool model). The colleague's fix direction (one grouped RPC) is correct; I tightened it: PostgREST aggregates (`group by`/count on a table) are disabled by default on Supabase, so a plain view/select cannot do the grouping — an RPC is the right tool. The function must be `security definer set search_path = ''`, `revoke ... from public, anon, authenticated` and `grant execute ... to service_role` to match the project's existing convention (e.g. security_hardening.sql:160-163), and it must preserve the exact filters currently in TS (role = 'user', conversation_id not like 'owner:%', created_at >= p_since). It needs a pgTAP test in supabase/tests like the other RPCs. Also check rpc errors explicitly instead of defaulting to 0.
- **При 10:** 10 bots → 20 counts in parallel; ~200-400 ms. Fine.
- **При 50:** 50 bots → 100 concurrent requests from one function; PostgREST on Nano queues them against its small DB pool while 50 students are generating → the page takes 1-3 s and briefly starves other requests; still renders.
- **Как чиним (уточнённый план):** 1) New migration supabase/migrations/2026xxxx_group_bot_counters.sql:

create or replace function public.group_bot_counters(p_bot_ids uuid[], p_since timestamptz)
returns table (bot_id uuid, leads_count bigint, guest_messages bigint)
language sql security definer set search_path = '' stable as $$
  select b.id as bot_id,
         (select count(*) from public.bot_leads l where l.bot_id = b.id) as leads_count,
         (select count(*) from public.bot_messages m
           where m.bot_id = b.id and m.role = 'user'
             and m.conversation_id not like 'owner:%'
             and m.created_at >= p_since) as guest_messages
  from public.bots b
  where b.id = any (p_bot_ids)
$$;
revoke all on function public.group_bot_counters(uuid[], timestamptz) from public, anon, authenticated;
grant execute on function public.group_bot_counters(uuid[], timestamptz) to service_role;

(Correlated subqueries keep both existing indexes bot_leads_bot_idx and bot_messages_bot_day_idx in use; `stable` lets Postgres plan it once. Add a cap, e.g. `if array_length(p_bot_ids,1) > 500 then raise`, if you want to guard against abuse — only service_role can call it, so optional.)

2) lib/server/bot/dal.ts:273-302 — replace the per-bot Promise.all with one call:

const { data: counters, error: cErr } = await db.rpc("group_bot_counters", { p_bot_ids: bots.map(b => b.id), p_since: weekAgo });
if (cErr) throw new Error(`bot.dal.listGroupBots counters failed (${cErr.code})`);
const byBot = new Map(counters.map(c => [c.bot_id, c]));
… leadsCount: Number(byBot.get(bot.id)?.leads_count ?? 0), guestMessagesWeek: Number(byBot.get(bot.id)?.guest_messages ?? 0)

Result: 3 requests instead of 2+2N, and an explicit error instead of silently showing 0. Regenerate lib/server/supabase/types.ts (supabase gen types) so the rpc is typed (count columns come back as bigint → number/string; coerce with Number()).

3) Add supabase/tests/005_group_bot_counters.test.sql (pgTAP): counts match for two bots, owner:% conversations and assistant rows are excluded, rows older than p_since are excluded, anon/authenticated get permission denied.

Side effects: none for students; teacher page semantics unchanged. Note the function is security definer on public tables that service_role already fully reads, so no privilege expansion beyond what the admin client has today.
- **Сложность:** small

### DB-06 · low · Bot leads list hard-capped at 1000 (limit(1000) = Max Rows) with no paging; export/UI silently drops older leads
- **Файлы:** lib/server/bot/dal.ts:169-175, lib/server/bot/limits.ts:19-20
- **Вердикт скептика:** Confirmed from code, not refuted. lib/server/bot/dal.ts:169-174 — listMyLeads selects bot_leads with `.order("created_at", desc).limit(1000)` and no range/paging; supabase/config.toml:13 sets `max_rows = 1000` (the hosted default is also 1000), so the limit coincides with PostgREST Max Rows and anything beyond is silently dropped. Both consumers use this list wholesale: app/(platform)/app/bot/leads/page.tsx:26 (table/cards render result.leads) and app/(platform)/app/bot/leads.csv/route.ts:15-20 (CSV built from result.leads; audit meta `count: result.leads.length` at :26 would log 1000 instead of the true count). Reachability: the cap is enforced in lib/server/bot/brain.ts:248-252 against BOT_LIMITS.leadsPerBotPerDay = 50 (limits.ts:20) and leadsPerConversationPerDay = 3, so ≥20 days at the daily maximum are needed. Student bots retire at groups.ends_on + 14 days (lifetime.ts:33-36, brain.ts:112 and api/tg/[botId]/route.ts:46-47 refuse after that), so during the course this is unreachable. Small correction to the colleague's framing: besides teacher/admin bots, student bots whose group has no ends_on are also UNLIMITED (lifetime.ts:31-32), so a never-dated group could reach it too. Scenario at 10 users (Monday lesson): each bot collects at most a handful of leads (3 per conversation/day, 50 per bot/day) — far below 1000; no impact. At 50 users: same per-bot caps, still unreachable in a 75-minute workshop; only a long-lived promoted bot (adult owner or undated group) accumulating >1000 leads over weeks would see the oldest rows vanish from the table and CSV with no explicit warning — though the nav badge `Заявки · ${leadsCount}` (BotNav.tsx:18, exact count from dal.ts:110-113) would show a larger number than the table, a silent mismatch rather than an error. No lesson breakage, no money or data loss (rows remain in the DB). Severity low is correct. Confidence: high.
- **При 10:** Not reachable during the course.
- **При 50:** Not reachable during the course; only a long-lived promoted bot could exceed it, then the oldest leads vanish from the owner's list and CSV without warning.
- **Как чиним (уточнённый план):** Proposed fix is sound; refine it to avoid two side effects. (1) Do not rely on `.limit(N)` with N equal to Max Rows: if an admin lowers Max Rows in the dashboard, both the current code and a naive 1000-row page are silently capped. Use a page constant below Max Rows (e.g. LEADS_PAGE = 500) shared with group-reports.ts PAGE, documented in setup.md as "must be ≤ Max Rows". (2) For the UI (leads/page.tsx): fetch only the newest LEADS_PAGE rows via `.range(0, LEADS_PAGE-1)` and, when `summary.leadsCount > leads.length` (count is already computed exactly in getMyBotSummary, dal.ts:110-113), render a notice "Показаны последние 500 из N — полный список в CSV" (optionally a `?page=` param with further `.range()` calls). (3) For CSV (leads.csv/route.ts): add `listAllMyLeads` in bot/dal.ts that loops with keyset pagination on the existing index bot_leads_bot_idx (bot_id, created_at desc): `.eq("bot_id").lt("created_at", lastSeen).order("created_at", desc).limit(LEADS_PAGE)` until a page returns fewer than LEADS_PAGE rows (keyset avoids offset drift if a lead is inserted mid-export). Keep the audit meta count as the real exported length. A few thousand rows stay well under the 60 s maxDuration. (4) Optional hardening: cap total export at e.g. 20 000 rows and say so in the UI, to bound memory in the serverless function.
- **Сложность:** small

### DB-07 · low · Price cache is per serverless instance (60 s TTL); invalidatePriceCache only clears the instance that served the admin — but create_job's server-side floor makes it money-safe
- **Файлы:** lib/server/prices.ts:61-90, supabase/migrations/20261101200000_init.sql:574-581
- **Вердикт скептика:** Confirmed in code, but with three factual corrections that leave the severity at low (confidence high).

What holds: `lib/server/prices.ts:61-66,83` keeps a module-level cache with a 60 s TTL, so each Fluid/serverless instance has its own copy and an admin price change propagates within ≤60 s per instance. `create_job` (supabase/migrations/20261101200000_init.sql:576-581) re-reads `price_list` under the wallet lock and raises `forbidden` when a student's `p_stars` is below the list minimum, which `lib/server/generate.ts:147,275` maps to HTTP 403. So a stale LOWER price fails closed and a stale HIGHER price is charged for at most a minute. Money and the lesson are not at risk.

Corrections to the finding:
1. `invalidatePriceCache` (prices.ts:88-90) is never called anywhere in app/ or lib/ (grep: only its definition). There is no admin price UI; `docs/platform/setup.md:110-114` tells the admin to edit `price_list` in the Supabase SQL Editor and already states «Сервер кеширует прайс до минуты». So the claim "invalidatePriceCache only clears the instance that served the admin" is inaccurate — it clears nothing because it is dead code; only the TTL applies. Practical effect is the same as the finding describes.
2. The scenario direction is inverted. Admin LOWERS a price → stale cached price is HIGHER ≥ new min → create_job passes and the student is overcharged by the delta for ≤60 s (no 403). Admin RAISES a price → stale cached price < new min → `forbidden` 403 for ≤60 s. The finding's scenario10 says lowering produces 403; it does not.
3. The server-side floor is `min(pl.stars)` over ALL tiers of that action+model (init.sql:578-579), not the exact tier price. So if only the `long` tier is raised, a stale lower `long` price still passes as long as it is ≥ the `base` price — still bounded to 60 s. Also `spend_stars` for `bot_reply` (init.sql:745-770) has no price floor at all; a stale bot_reply price is charged as-is for ≤60 s (amount capped at 100 by the function).

Re-derived scenarios: 10 users — Vercel typically serves them from 1–2 instances; a mid-lesson price edit (which setup.md already frames as an admin SQL action, not a lesson action) causes at most one minute of either a few-star overcharge or a 403 that clears on retry; no job row is inserted on `forbidden`, so the same idempotency key retries cleanly. 50 users — a few more instances, same ≤60 s window per instance, same bounded outcome. Since prices are edited by SQL in a dashboard, the realistic cadence is "between lessons", not during; the window is practically never hit. Hence low.

The proposed `max(updated_at)` pre-check is not a good trade: `canUse`/`listModels` run on every chat message, every generation, every options render and every bot reply (generate.ts:215, generate-options.ts:30-45, bot/brain.ts:177,273, bot/dal.ts:57-58), so adding a DB round trip from Vercel to EU Supabase (~30–100 ms) on each call removes the whole point of the cache for a problem that is already bounded and money-safe. If a shorter window is wanted, lowering TTL_MS is simpler and has the same effect.
- **При 10:** Admin lowers a price mid-lesson → for ≤60 s some students see `forbidden` (403) instead of the new price, or pay the old higher price. Rare, bounded.
- **При 50:** Same, more instances → more students see a 403 blip for up to a minute after a price change.
- **Как чиним (уточнённый план):** Keep the design (per-instance 60 s cache + SQL floor) — it is money-safe. Small cleanups only:
1. `docs/platform/setup.md` next to line 114: add «Меняйте прайс между занятиями, не во время урока: до минуты после правки часть учеников может получить ошибку (цена повышена) или заплатить старую цену (цена понижена).»
2. `lib/server/prices.ts:87-90`: either delete the unused `invalidatePriceCache` or wire it into a future admin price endpoint — right now it is misleading dead code that suggests invalidation exists.
3. Optional, if the owner wants a tighter window: lower `TTL_MS` to 15–30 s (prices.ts:61). Do NOT add a per-call `max(updated_at)` query — it adds a DB round trip to every chat/generation/bot request for no material benefit.
4. Optional hardening (separate, tiny): add the same `price_list` floor to `spend_stars` for `bot_reply` (init.sql:745-770), which today trusts `p_amount` entirely; this is unrelated to the cache but closes the one path where a stale/incorrect server price is not re-checked in SQL.
- **Сложность:** small

### DB-10 · low · No retention/cleanup for jobs, bot_messages, star_ledger, audit_log, rate_limit_hits or Storage objects — unbounded growth toward Free-plan read-only mode
- **Файлы:** supabase/migrations/20261101200000_init.sql:131-162,224-241,283-291, lib/server/studio.ts:297-323, supabase/migrations/20261105100000_security_hardening.sql:141-143
- **Вердикт скептика:** Confirmed by code. Deletion paths in the whole codebase: (1) private.rate_limit_hits — per-key window delete plus 2 % random sweep of >1 day (supabase/migrations/20261105100000_security_hardening.sql:138-143, 157); (2) abandoned montage uploads — refund + remove of `media/{user}/{job}.mp4` only for queued montage jobs older than STALE_UPLOAD_MINUTES (lib/server/studio.ts:297-323, plus 425/429 on abort); (3) bot_messages.deleteConversation for a single conversation on user action (lib/server/bot/store.ts:299-306); (4) one lesson_progress step toggle (lib/server/lesson-progress.ts:94). No SQL `delete from` exists for jobs, bot_messages (bulk), bot_leads, audit_log, quiz_attempts or storage.objects; no retention text in docs/platform/spec.md or setup.md. Column limits that bound per-row size are as cited: jobs.params ≤32 KB, result_text ≤20 KB, prompt ≤5000, error ≤500 (init.sql:122-135); bot_messages.content ≤4000 (init.sql:231); audit_log.meta ≤8 KB (init.sql:263). Storage: bucket `media` with 50 MB file limit (init.sql:893-894), MAX_MONTAGE_BYTES = 50 MB (lib/platform/montage/types.ts:79), results downloaded up to MAX_RESULT_BYTES = 100 MB (lib/server/gateway.ts:288) — every done job keeps its object(s) forever (storage_path + extra_paths). Two aggravating facts the finding missed: (a) deleting a student cascades jobs rows (init.sql:117 `on delete cascade`) but nothing removes `media/{user_id}/…` objects, so Storage gains orphans that no table references any more; (b) the admin library folder `library/motion/` (lib/server/storage.ts:19-24) lives in the same bucket and must survive any sweep. Thresholds are documented in docs/platform/audit/2026-11-08-research.md:37 (Free DB read-only at 500 MB, `cannot execute INSERT in a read-only transaction`) and :43 (Storage Free 1 GB / Pro 100 GB, $0.0213/GB-month) with official sources. Re-derived scenarios — 10 students/lesson: ~10×(20 chat + 3 img + 3 video) ≈ 260 jobs ≈ 1–3 MB of DB (params+result_text) and ~0.3–0.6 GB of Storage (videos 3–5 MB, montages up to 50 MB); DB read-only is years away, Storage Free quota is gone in 2–3 lessons. 50 students: ~1.3k jobs ≈ 5–15 MB DB per workshop, 1.5–3 GB Storage per workshop → Pro 100 GB in ~8–12 months, then paid overage only. So on Pro this is purely cost/growth polish, never a lesson-breaking failure → severity stays low; the Free-plan quota exhaustion is a plan-choice issue already covered by the research note ("Pro before the first paid workshop") and bot guest data retention overlaps BP-4. Confidence: high that no retention exists; medium on the growth arithmetic (depends on actual video sizes). Colleague's fix needs corrections for side effects: nulling storage_path on old jobs would silently kill bot avatars (lib/server/bot/card.ts:51 returns null when storage_path is null; bots outlive the group until the Hanukkah evening), the fix ignores extra_paths, and jobs rows must never be deleted because star_ledger.job_id references jobs with no ON DELETE action and the ledger is append-only (init.sql:157 and ledger_append_only trigger), so only Storage objects can go.
- **При 10:** ~300 jobs + 500 messages per lesson → a few MB per lesson; years of headroom on the DB; Storage grows ~0.5-1 GB per lesson (videos + montages) → Free 1 GB Storage quota in week 1-2.
- **При 50:** ~1500 jobs + 2500 messages + up to 2.5 GB Storage per workshop; DB still only tens of MB per month, but Storage passes 100 GB (Pro quota) in ~9 months.
- **Как чиним (уточнённый план):** Keep severity low; implement a Storage-only retention pass run by the owner (no cron on Hobby) and document thresholds. Concretely: (1) Add migration: `alter table public.jobs add column purged_at timestamptz;` so the gallery can show «файл удалён по сроку хранения» instead of the item silently vanishing (gallery.ts:236 already skips rows with null storage_path). (2) Add an admin-only Server Action «Очистить старые файлы» (authorize('admin'), audited as `storage.purge`) that selects done jobs with storage_path not null, purged_at null, whose group.ends_on < now() − N weeks (N configurable in settings, default 8), EXCLUDING jobs referenced by bots.avatar_job_id and any path under `library/`; for each batch of ≤100 paths call `admin.storage.from(MEDIA_BUCKET).remove(paths)` with storage_path and all extra_paths, then `update jobs set storage_path = null, extra_paths = '{}', purged_at = now()`. Never delete jobs rows (star_ledger.job_id FK + append-only trigger) — keep params/result_text, they are ≤52 KB each and years from 500 MB. (3) Student deletion: before `auth.admin.deleteUser`/profile delete, list `media/{user_id}/` via storage.list and remove in chunks of 100, otherwise objects are orphaned (no table references them after the cascade). (4) bot_messages: fold into BP-4's lazy retention (delete older than 30 days once per day per bot inside the /api/b and /api/tg handlers) — do not duplicate here. (5) star_ledger and audit_log stay append-only. (6) In docs/platform/setup.md add a «Квоты и хранение» section: Free DB read-only at 500 MB and Storage 1 GB → run on Pro; Pro Storage 100 GB included, $0.0213/GB-month after; where to see usage (Dashboard → Usage); how often to run the purge action. Optional later: schedule the same purge via pg_cron calling an Edge Function, but do not delete from storage.objects in SQL directly — use the Storage API so the underlying files are actually removed.
- **Сложность:** medium

### DB-11 · low · PostgREST Max Rows is an invisible contract: group-reports pagination stops early if the dashboard value is lowered below 1000
- **Файлы:** lib/server/group-reports.ts:13-27, supabase/config.toml:13
- **Вердикт скептика:** Confirmed in code: lib/server/group-reports.ts:13-24 hard-codes PAGE=1000 and stops when data.length < PAGE; postgrest-js 2.118.0 (.range -> ?offset=&limit=1000, node_modules/@supabase/postgrest-js/dist/index.mjs:1068-1074) sends limit=1000 and PostgREST silently caps the result at db-max-rows without any error or signal, so with Max Rows < 1000 every page is short and the loop exits after page 1. supabase/config.toml:13 fixes max_rows=1000 only locally; docs/platform/setup.md §1.2 (lines 37-45) does not mention Max Rows, so the production value is an undocumented assumption. At 10 students in one group (~50 ledger rows/student/lesson) a lowered Max Rows=500 would under-count star spending from lesson 2 onward; at 50 students the same happens in every group since reports are per group. Impact is display-only in the teacher report (ledger data is intact, no money or lesson flow at risk) and requires a manual dashboard change that nothing today motivates, so low is correct. Confidence: high on mechanism, the trigger is hypothetical hardening.
- **При 10:** Only if someone changes the dashboard setting; then report totals are 1-page truncated.
- **При 50:** Same; with 7-10k ledger rows the report would show roughly 1/10 of spending.
- **Как чиним (уточнённый план):** 1) Make fetchAll independent of Max Rows: advance by the number of rows actually returned and stop on an empty page, e.g. `let from = 0; while (from < MAX_ROWS) { const { data, error } = await load(from, from + PAGE - 1); if (error) throw ...; if (!data || data.length === 0) break; rows.push(...data); from += data.length; }`. Side effect: exactly one extra PostgREST request per table (3 per report, already run in parallel) — acceptable for a teacher report page. Do NOT just lower PAGE to 500: that keeps the same invisible contract at a different number. 2) In docs/platform/setup.md §1.2 «Data API» add one line: «Max Rows оставить 1000 (по умолчанию): lib/server/group-reports.ts, lib/server/dal/teacher.ts (DB-01) и lib/server/bot/dal.ts:174 рассчитывают на ≥ 1000 строк за запрос». 3) Optionally add a comment next to `max_rows = 1000` in supabase/config.toml pointing to that setup.md line, so local and production values stay in step.
- **Сложность:** small

### GEN-07 · low · Кеш прайса (60 с на инстанс) без инвалидации: после правки цен в дашборде ученики ≤60 с получают «Это действие вам недоступно» (при повышении) или переплачивают (при понижении)
- **Файлы:** lib/server/prices.ts:61-62, lib/server/prices.ts:65-85, lib/server/prices.ts:88-90, lib/server/generate-options.ts:30, lib/server/generate.ts:215, lib/server/generate.ts:273, supabase/migrations/20261101200000_init.sql:576-583
- **Вердикт скептика:** Confirmed by code, severity stays low. Mechanism: lib/server/prices.ts:61-66 — module-level cache with TTL_MS=60_000, one copy per warm instance; invalidatePriceCache() (prices.ts:88-90) is defined but never called anywhere in app/ or lib/ (grep confirms the only hits are its definition and a comment). Admin edits prices via raw SQL per docs/platform/setup.md:109-111, and setup.md:114 already states "Сервер кеширует прайс до минуты" — so this is documented, intended behaviour, not a latent bug. Both failure directions are real: (a) price raised — /api/generate on an instance with a stale (lower) price sends decision.stars (generate.ts:215, 273) to create_job, where `v_role = 'student' and p_stars < v_min_price → forbidden` (init.sql:581-582) → WALLET_STATUS.forbidden = 403 (generate.ts:147) → client shows errorMessages.forbidden "Это действие вам недоступно." (lib/platform/messages.ts:41). No job is created (exception rolls back), so a retry after the cache expires succeeds. (b) price lowered — stale higher price passes the SQL check and the student is debited the old price; money at risk is the student's virtual stars, not the owner's kie credits (the gateway cost does not depend on the star price). Window: at most 60 s per instance after the admin's UPDATE; the lesson page (generate-options.ts:30) and the API may hit different instances, so the button label and the actual debit can briefly disagree. Same staleness applies to bot pricing (lib/server/bot/brain.ts:177,273; card.ts:91), harmless. At 10 users: only if the admin edits price_list during the lesson — 1–2 students get one confusing 403 and succeed on retry. At 50 users: up to ~10 confused students for one minute, or ≤60 s of overpaying by the price delta. Not a lesson-breaking issue and nothing is lost permanently; the misleading error text and the lack of invalidation are polish. Confidence: high (all claims verified in code; Fluid per-instance module state is standard Vercel behaviour).
- **При 10:** Админ меняет цену перед уроком — в течение минуты 1–2 ученика могут упереться в непонятный «недоступно»; повторный клик через минуту проходит. Практически незаметно, если не править цены во время урока.
- **При 50:** То же, но до десятка человек и повторные вопросы ведущему. Если цены правят во время воркшопа (акция «видео сегодня дешевле») — переплата на старую цену у части учеников на протяжении минуты.
- **Как чиним (уточнённый план):** Keep the finding as low. Fix, in priority order: (1) Do NOT add a `max(updated_at)` check before each cache hit — getPriceList() is invoked by every canUse()/listModels() call, and describeAction() calls canUse twice per aiAction (generate-options.ts:30,45), so a per-call round trip defeats the cache and multiplies price_list reads on lesson page renders at 50 users. Instead simply lower TTL_MS in lib/server/prices.ts:61 to 15–20 s (≤4 queries/min/instance, negligible). (2) In startGeneration (lib/server/generate.ts:280), when createJobWithDebit returns error "forbidden" for a non-staff user, call invalidatePriceCache(), re-run canUse() once, and if the fresh decision.stars differs from the old one, retry createJobWithDebit once with the same idempotencyKey (safe: the failed call created no job). Prefer returning a distinct 409 `price_changed` error carrying the fresh price (add it to errorMessages, e.g. "Цена изменилась: теперь N★ — нажмите ещё раз") rather than silently debiting a higher amount the student never saw; if the owner prefers zero friction, debit the fresh price and rely on the returned `balance`. Also keep the retry path for the staff_only case (fresh canUse returns the clear staff_only reason instead of the generic forbidden). (3) Optional, more robust: make SQL the price authority — pass p_tier to create_job and let it select stars from price_list itself, removing the TS cache from the money path; larger change, not needed for Monday. (4) Docs: in docs/platform/setup.md §1.6 add "цены менять не во время урока; после правки подождать минуту" next to the existing "Сервер кеширует прайс до минуты". Effort: small for (1)+(2)+(4).
- **Сложность:** small

### GEN-10 · low · Ученик при uncertain/queued-сбое ждёт 3 минуты до возврата; карточка «в очереди» молчит о причине; сообщение gateway_error обещает возврат, но при server_error/504 ученик не знает, списаны ли звёзды
- **Файлы:** lib/server/job-rules.ts:14, lib/server/generate.ts:335-340, components/platform/GenerateJobCard.tsx:87-105, components/platform/GenerateJobCard.tsx:107-111, lib/platform/generation-messages.ts:148-162, lib/platform/messages.ts:46
- **Вердикт скептика:** Facts verified in code: lib/server/job-rules.ts:14 sets QUEUED_WITHOUT_TASK_LIMIT_MS = 3 min; lib/server/generate.ts:335-340 returns `accepted` with no taskId on `uncertain`, and nothing can ever set gateway_task_id afterwards (the only writer, markJobSubmitted, runs inside the same request, which is capped by maxDuration = 60 in app/api/generate/route.ts), so the student sees a generic spinner ("Генерируется…", GenerateJobCard.tsx:107-111) for the remaining ~2 min 15 s and then "Связь с сервисом генерации оборвалась. Звёзды вернулись." (generation-messages.ts:16). gateway.ts:269 parses `progress` but advanceJob returns a JobRecord and toJobViews has no progress field, so it is never shown. messages.ts:46 `server_error` says nothing about stars. All correct, so the finding stands as low (UX polish; no money or data at risk — refunds are deterministic and idempotent). Corrections: (a) `uncertain` requires a 45 s create timeout (gateway.ts:169,416) or a 2xx non-JSON body (gateway.ts:428,432); the common kie overload (fast 502/503/429) is retried and ends in `gateway_error` with an immediate refund and the correct "Звёзды вернулись" text. The "1–2 students per lesson / 5–10 at 50" frequency is unsupported speculation — realistic frequency is rare at 10 and occasional at 50 (confidence medium: depends on n8n/kie timeout behaviour, not on the code). (b) An orphaned queued job after a 500/network failure is recovered only when the student opens the gallery (gallery.ts:57 lists non-failed jobs) or reloads the lesson (lesson-progress.ts:36-43), both of which poll via useLiveJob; until then it occupies one of 5 open slots (init.sql:571) and, because GeneratePanel.tsx:182 drops the idempotency key on non-network errors, a retry double-debits for up to 3 min. (c) Proposed fix 3 is risky as written: `server_error` in messages.ts is platform-wide (login, teacher forms via errorMessage()), and platformErrorMessage is also used by GalleryGrid hide toggle and LessonTaskTools, so a "stars will return" text there would be wrong. (d) jobs_open_idx is partial on (user_id) (init.sql:148), not group_id; a group counter would use jobs_group_created_idx — the index claim is incorrect but harmless. (e) Fix 1 (90 s) is safe: created_at precedes the gateway call and the function cannot outlive 60 s; even if maxDuration were later raised, mark_job_submitted only updates rows still in `queued`, so a late submit is rejected and logged — student money stays safe, only owner kie credits could be wasted (same as today).
- **При 10:** 1–2 ученика за урок увидят 3-минутную «очередь» с последующей ошибкой и спросят ведущего «а звёзды?».
- **При 50:** В окно перегрузки — 5–10 человек одновременно, ведущий тратит время урока на объяснения вместо занятия.
- **Как чиним (уточнённый план):** 1) lib/server/job-rules.ts:14: QUEUED_WITHOUT_TASK_LIMIT_MS = 90_000 with a comment "must exceed maxDuration (60 s) of /api/generate + clock skew; nothing sets gateway_task_id after the request ends". Update docs/platform/spec.md §3.1 row "queued без taskId" (3 → 1.5 min) and the comment at generate.ts:336-337. Do not go below ~75 s. 2) Client-only hint, no schema change: in GenerateJobCard.tsx slowHint(), when job.status === "queued" and Date.now() - Date.parse(job.createdAt) > 20_000 show "Ждём ответа сервиса генерации…"; keep the existing per-model time hints. Progress display is optional and low value (kie returns progress only for some models): if wanted, pass it transiently — have advanceJob return { job, progress } and add an optional `progress?: number | null` to JobView in toJobView for the poll response only (no DB column); client keeps the last non-null value because claimJobPoll means not every poll reaches the gateway. 3) Do NOT edit messages.ts:46 or add `server_error` to generation-messages.ts (both are shared with non-generation UI). Instead, in GeneratePanel.tsx around line 180-184, when result.error === "server_error" (including result.network) show a generation-specific text from a new key, e.g. generation_uncertain: "Не удалось получить ответ. Если звёзды списались, они вернутся сами — открой галерею или обнови страницу, задача появится там." and keep keyRef.current (do not null it) so a retry within the key's lifetime replays the same job instead of double-debiting; additionally call router.refresh()/onJob refresh so a possibly created queued job appears and gets polled by useLiveJob. 4) Teacher view of open/stuck jobs on /teach/groups/[id]: feature, not a fix; a count `where group_id = $1 and status in ('queued','submitted','storing')` is served by jobs_group_created_idx (jobs_open_idx is per user_id and does not apply). Low priority; only worth it after real workshops show stuck jobs.
- **Сложность:** small

### SEC-06 · low · Ученик через Data API читает бюджет группы, teacher_id, daily_cap и полные prompt/params чужих работ из общей галереи
- **Файлы:** supabase/migrations/20261101200000_init.sql:362-364, supabase/migrations/20261101200000_init.sql:385-386, supabase/migrations/20261101200000_init.sql:397-404, lib/server/dal/teacher.ts:40-41, lib/server/dal/teacher.ts:81-103, lib/server/job-progress.ts:91-98, lib/server/bot/dal.ts:206-210, docs/decisions.md:86
- **Вердикт скептика:** Confirmed from code (confidence high). Prerequisites for a student hitting the Data API directly all hold: the publishable key is sent to the browser (lib/server/studio.ts:387 `apiKey: supabasePublicConfig().publishableKey`; docs/platform/setup.md:50 says it is public by design), the project URL is in CSP connect-src (next.config.ts:41-44), the session JWT lives in the student's own cookies (@supabase/ssr, proxy.ts:41-57), and `public` is an exposed schema (setup.md:41). (1) groups: init.sql:362-364 grants table-level SELECT on public.groups to authenticated; groups_read (init.sql:385-386) admits `id = private.my_group_id()`; 20261103110000_group_budget.sql:6-9 later adds budget_stars/planned_students/stars_per_student to the same table, so a table-level grant covers them automatically. A student `GET /rest/v1/groups?select=*` returns budget_stars, planned_students, stars_per_student, daily_cap, teacher_id for their group — contradicting docs/decisions.md:99 («Бюджет, роздано и потрачено видит только учитель»). (2) jobs: jobs_read (init.sql:397-404) shared-gallery branch returns whole rows; jobs has prompt (≤5000), params jsonb (refs with `{user_id}/{job_id}` paths, media-refs.ts:6,29-32), credits, gateway_task_id, idempotency_key, error (init.sql:115-145). The UI trims prompt to 300 (gallery.ts:91 promptLimit) but PostgREST does not. Note user_id is already a selected column (job-progress.ts:33-35), so classmate UUIDs are exposed by design regardless. (3) The bot-avatar point is not actionable: storage layout is `{user_id}/{job_id}` by design, and a bare UUID grants nothing under RLS (profiles_read is own-or-teacher only, test 001_rls:48-49). At 10 users: one curious student with DevTools sees group budget/stars-per-student and full prompts of classmates — no money or data at risk (wallets/star_ledger are per-user RLS, spending is security-definer only, init.sql:380-383,478+). At 50 users / other centers: identical per-student capability, no scaling dimension; budget is the center's commercial info and a reputational issue when selling the platform, still no direct damage. Severity low is correct. The proposed fix, however, has side effects: a view + full revoke of SELECT on groups breaks lib/server/dal/auth.ts:66, which embeds `group:groups!profiles_group_fk(...)` through the user client on every request (PostgREST embedding needs SELECT privilege on the embedded table) — every session load would fail; and a column-level grant on jobs is not viable because the user client selects `params` for own jobs in studio.ts:190 and gateway_task_id/error via JOB_COLUMNS.
- **При 10:** Любопытный ученик с DevTools видит бюджет группы и сколько звёзд выдали каждому — неприятно, но безвредно.
- **При 50:** При продаже центрам бюджет группы — коммерческая информация центра; утечка UUID и чужих промптов — повод для претензий, но не прямой ущерб.
- **Как чиним (уточнённый план):** 1) groups — column-level grant, same pattern already used for bots (init.sql:366-368), instead of a view: new migration `revoke select on public.groups from authenticated; grant select (id, code, title, kind, paused, ends_on, allow_google_minors, share_gallery, teacher_id, created_at) on public.groups to authenticated;`. This keeps auth.ts:66 embedding working (it selects only id, code, title, paused, allow_google_minors, share_gallery, ends_on). Keep teacher_id/created_at only if teacher reads stay on the user client (WHERE/ORDER BY need column SELECT privilege); better: move listGroups/getGroup (lib/server/dal/teacher.ts:81-124) to createAdminClient() with explicit `.eq("teacher_id", user.id)` for non-admins (assertStaff + teacher_id comparison already exist; assertTeachesGroup at teacher.ts:198-209 already does this), then drop teacher_id and created_at from the grant too. canViewJob (job-progress.ts:80) and storage.ts:207 already use the admin client — no change. 2) jobs — do NOT use column-level grants (breaks studio.ts:190 `params` and JOB_COLUMNS gateway_task_id/error for own jobs). Either accept the exposure (shared gallery shows prompts and authors by design; only the 300-char trim and credits/gateway_task_id leak beyond the UI) or, if classmate-prompt privacy matters for resale, remove the shared-gallery branch from jobs_read (init.sql:401-404) and serve listSharedGroupGallery (gallery.ts:76-88) and the shared half of listStudioMedia (studio.ts:188-200+) via createAdminClient() with the same server-side filters (group_id = user.group.id, status='done', hidden=false, user.group.shareGallery already checked); then the student's Data API view of jobs is own rows only. Side effect: 001_rls.test.sql:84-93 shared-gallery assertions must be rewritten; defence moves from DB to code. 3) Drop the bot-avatar/UUID item — not actionable and no privilege follows from a UUID. 4) Tests in supabase/tests/001_rls.test.sql, as student (sub …0a) and as teacher: `select throws_ok($$ select budget_stars, stars_per_student, daily_cap from public.groups $$, '42501', null, 'ученик не видит бюджет группы')`; and keep the existing `count(*) from public.groups = 1` to prove the embed path still works. Effort: small for (1); medium for (2b).
- **Сложность:** medium

### SEC-08 · low · Доступ к урокам и конструктору бота не требует группы: вошедший без группы читает весь курс и занимает slug бота
- **Файлы:** lib/server/dal/auth.ts:129-166, app/(platform)/app/lessons/[n]/page.tsx:49, app/(platform)/app/bot/actions.ts:80-116, supabase/migrations/20261105100000_security_hardening.sql:165-185
- **Вердикт скептика:** Код подтверждает находку, но только при чужой ошибке настройки Auth. Проверено: (1) lib/server/dal/auth.ts:118-129 и 147-152 — requireUser/authorize смотрят только на наличие профиля, disabled и роль; поле group возвращается как null без каких-либо проверок (auth.ts:84-94). (2) app/(platform)/app/lessons/[n]/page.tsx:49 — урок отдаётся любому вошедшему; тот же requireUser в app/(platform)/app/page.tsx, bot/page.tsx:43, gallery, studio. (3) app/(platform)/app/bot/actions.ts:80-112 — createBot проверяет только authorize() и вставляет bots(slug unique, init.sql:201) без проверки группы; бот ученика без группы ещё и бессрочный, т.к. endsOn=null даёт UNLIMITED (lib/server/bot/lifetime.ts:31-32). (4) Профиль создаётся триггером on_auth_user_created для ЛЮБОЙ вставки в auth.users (group_budget.sql:83-100: role по умолчанию 'student', init.sql:45; group_id остаётся null, apply_student_meta выходит сразу при пустом group_id — student_meta_on_update.sql:21-23), т.е. самозарегистрированный пользователь получает рабочий профиль. (5) Форма входа принимает реальный e-mail, а не только синтетический логин (lib/server/accounts.ts:16-21: value.includes('@') → z.email()), поэтому после signUp по публичному ключу и подтверждения письма такой пользователь входит обычной формой и попадает в /app. Единственная защита — выключенный signup в дашборде (setup.md:24, 770-status.md:14) и lock_for_spend (hardening.sql:185), которая закрывает только траты. Сценарий 10 и 50 учеников: в текущем деплое не проявляется — все 10 учеников группы 770 созданы учителем с group_id, signup выключен; на уроке влияния нет. В другом центре при включённом signup: чтение всего курса и занятие slug без возможности тратить — утечка контента, не денег. Severity low оставляю: деньги и урок не под угрозой, нужна предварительная ошибка конфигурации. Confidence: по коду — high; по вероятности эксплуатации — medium (требует включённого signup и, при включённом Confirm email, реального почтового ящика). Предложенный фикс требует правки: глобальная проверка group !== null в requireUser сломает законный случай — у ученика группа обнуляется при удалении группы (profiles_group_fk on delete set null, init.sql:76), а страницы /app/bot/export и /app/gallery по спецификации должны оставаться доступны после курса; также requireUser используют страницы учителя, у которых group закономерно null.
- **При 10:** Не проявляется при правильной настройке Auth.
- **При 50:** Если в новом центре забыли выключить «Allow new users to sign up», любой зарегистрируется, прочитает весь платный курс и займёт красивые slug ботов; тратить не сможет.
- **Как чиним (уточнённый план):** 1) Не трогать requireUser/authorize глобально. Добавить в lib/server/dal/auth.ts отдельную проверку `requireCourseAccess(user)` / опцию `authorize({ needGroup: true })`: для role === 'student' && group === null — redirect на страницу/уведомление «Аккаунт не привязан к группе, обратитесь к ведущему» (в Server Actions — AuthError('forbidden')). Применить точечно: lessons/[n]/page.tsx и lessons/[n]/actions.ts, bot/actions.ts createBot (и saveBot при смене slug), studio/page.tsx и /api/generate, /api/studio/*. Оставить без проверки /app (кабинет), /app/gallery, /app/bot/export и /app/bot/leads — они нужны ученикам после удаления/окончания группы. 2) Защита на уровне базы, не зависящая от дашборда: в private.handle_new_user (supabase/migrations) при отсутствии created_by в raw_app_meta_data создавать профиль с disabled = true — тогда getUser вернёт null, а форма входа покажет «Аккаунт отключён. Обратитесь к ведущему» (login/actions.ts:58-61). Побочный эффект: учителей/админа, которых создают вручную в дашборде, нужно включать SQL-ом вместе с повышением роли — дописать это в setup.md рядом с инструкцией о ролях; добавить pgTAP-тест в supabase/tests. 3) Вместо Management API: на /teach показать предупреждение по простому признаку «profiles where role = 'student' and login is null» (учитель всегда задаёт login через app_metadata, teach/actions.ts:233-240; у самозарегистрированного login = null, group_budget.sql:93) — одна выборка через существующий клиент, без новых секретов. 4) В setup.md оставить пункт «Allow new users to sign up — выключить» и добавить «Confirm email — включить» как обязательный чеклист для новых центров.
- **Сложность:** small

### SEC-09 · low · Нет журнала входов и обзора расходов ведущих: деньги видны только в star_ledger/jobs по SQL, админской страницы нет
- **Файлы:** lib/server/audit.ts:10-28, lib/server/audit.ts:30-48, app/(platform)/login/actions.ts:48-63, proxy.ts:15, supabase/migrations/20261101200000_init.sql:737-739, docs/platform/setup.md:233-237
- **Вердикт скептика:** Проверено по коду — факты подтверждаются, но с двумя поправками.

Подтверждено:
- lib/server/audit.ts:10-28 — в AuditAction нет login.*; app/(platform)/login/actions.ts:30-64 ничего не пишет в audit_log ни при успехе, ни при отказе (строки 48-54, 57-61). Единственный след неудачных попыток — HMAC-счётчики rate_limit_hit (lib/server/rate-limit.ts:24-45), которые истекают через 15 минут и не хранят ни время, ни результат.
- lib/server/audit.ts:45-48 — ошибка вставки только в console.error (логи Vercel Hobby живут ~1 час, комментарий audit.ts:7). Потеря записей незаметна.
- proxy.ts:14 и :71 защищают /admin, но `find app -path '*admin*'` пуст: страницы /admin нет. Админ работает через /teach (assertTeachesGroup, lib/server/dal/teacher.ts:208).
