- Деньги: grant → audit_log из SQL (init.sql:737-739, строки верны); траты учеников в звёздах видны учителю через getGroupStarStats (teacher.ts:164-191), но это звёзды, а не кредиты kie. jobs.credits (init.sql, колонка `credits numeric`) пишется (jobs.ts:62, job-progress.ts:199, generate.ts:322, bot/card.ts:204) и нигде не читается ни одной страницей — grep по app/lib находит только писателей. Генерации staff идут с stars=0 (prices.ts:143-151), поэтому их расход виден только через SQL по jobs.credits.
- setup.md:211 — проверка журнала ручным SQL. Поправка к ссылке: в файле нет строк 233-237 (он короче); верные ссылки — setup.md:211 (ручной select из audit_log) и :133 («Админ видит все группы и журнал аудита» — обещание без UI).

Частичное опровержение формулировки «успешные входы нигде не пишутся»: Supabase Auth (GoTrue) сам пишет каждый успешный signInWithPassword в таблицу `auth.audit_log_entries` (action 'login', в payload actor_id и actor_username=синтетический e-mail). Таблица лежит в Postgres и не зависит от срока хранения логов в дашборде (Auth Logs: 1 день на Free, 7 дней на Pro). Ограничения: ip_address там — egress-IP Vercel, а не ученика (вход серверный, см. docs/platform/audit/2026-11-08-research.md:9,13-14); неудачные попытки пароля в audit_log_entries не попадают (только в сервисные логи GoTrue). Уверенность по поведению GoTrue: medium (по знанию исходников, в этой сессии не перепроверялось). Итого реальный пробел — неудачные входы + IP ученика + отсутствие UI, а не «ничего не пишется».

Сценарии: при 10 и при 50 учениках ни одна операция не ломается и деньги не теряются — находка про расследование постфактум (SEC-01) и про обещание в setup.md:133. Это не блокер понедельника. Severity low оставить. Отмечу, что research.md:74 и :86 независимо просят писать статус ошибок входа (429) в audit, чтобы измерить проблему SEC-02 на первом большом воркшопе — это усиливает пользу пункта 1.

Проверка предложенного фикса на побочные эффекты:
- Пункт 1 (login.success/failed) безопасен: check на audit_log.action `^[a-z_.]{2,60}$` (init.sql:259) пропускает такие имена без миграции; RLS — читает только админ (init.sql:421-422), insert только service_role (:379). Объём ограничен самим лимитером (≤20/логин и ≤100/IP за 15 мин), при 50 входах за 2 минуты — ≤~150 вставок, пренебрежимо. HMAC брать той же деривацией, что в rate-limit.ts:24-27 (вынести keyFor), а не новым ключом.
- Пункт 3 («считать неудачи audit() и показывать в /admin») не реализуем: serverless не хранит счётчик между вызовами, а неудачная вставка почти всегда означает недоступную базу — писать счётчик некуда. Vercel Log Drains на Hobby недоступны (уверенность medium). Пункт нужно заменить.
- Пункт 4 («Auth Hooks/логи на Pro») лишний: данные о успешных входах уже лежат в auth.audit_log_entries бессрочно; хуки ничего не добавят к неудачным попыткам.
- Пункт 2 (/admin) корректен, но medium-эффорт; дешёвый первый шаг — SQL-вью и документированные запросы.
- **При 10:** После урока владелец не может быстро ответить «кто из учеников/ведущих сколько кредитов kie сжёг и были ли подозрительные входы» без SQL Editor.
- **При 50:** При нескольких ведущих и 50 учениках расследование утечки пароля или аномальных трат (SEC-01) занимает часы; ошибки записи аудита остаются незамеченными.
- **Как чиним (уточнённый план):** 1) Журнал входов (small): добавить в AuditAction `login.success` и `login.failed`; в login() (actions.ts) после signInWithPassword писать audit({ actorId: data?.user?.id ?? null, action, meta: { ip: hmac(ip), login: hmac(email), status: error?.status ?? null, reason: 'wrong'|'too_many'|'disabled' } }). HMAC брать из общей функции keyFor (вынести из lib/server/rate-limit.ts:24-27 в lib/server/hmac.ts), не хранить логин и IP открыто. Для `too_many` писать тоже — именно это нужно, чтобы измерить 429/лимиты на первом 50-местном воркшопе (research.md:74, 86). Вызов не блокирует вход (audit уже глотает ошибки). Миграция не нужна: check `^[a-z_.]{2,60}$` пропускает имена.

2) Вместо «счётчика неудач audit()»: (а) оставить console.error; (б) для денежных действий журнал уже транзакционный в SQL (grant_stars, init.sql:737) — там потерь нет; (в) для остальных staff-действий сделать audit() возвращающим boolean и в Server Actions учителя показывать мягкое предупреждение «сделано, но запись в журнал не удалась» — это единственный видимый сигнал без внешнего лог-дрейна на Hobby.

3) Обзор расходов без новой страницы (small, первым шагом): миграция с вью `private.kie_spend` (select j.user_id, p.role, j.group_id, date_trunc('day', j.created_at) d, sum(j.credits) credits, count(*) jobs from public.jobs j join public.profiles p ... group by ...) и `private.recent_logins` над auth.audit_log_entries (action = 'login', payload->>'actor_username', created_at). Добавить в docs/platform/setup.md §5 три готовых запроса: кредиты kie по учителям за сутки, последние 50 записей audit_log, последние входы; исправить setup.md:133 — админ видит журнал через SQL Editor, не в интерфейсе. Затем (medium, после понедельника) страница /admin под requireRole(['admin']): те же вью + getGatewayBalance() (gateway.ts:274, сейчас никем не вызывается) + последние audit_log; матчер proxy.ts:71 уже готов.

4) Убрать из плана «включить Supabase Auth Hooks/логи»: успешные входы уже хранятся в auth.audit_log_entries бессрочно; неудачных попыток хуки не дадут — их закрывает пункт 1.
- **Сложность:** medium

### SEC-10 · low · CSP кабинета допускает 'unsafe-inline' для скриптов, а студия открывает connect-src к unpkg/jsdelivr, хотя ядро ffmpeg лежит в /public
- **Файлы:** next.config.ts:33, next.config.ts:37-52, next.config.ts:94, public/ffmpeg/ffmpeg-core.js
- **Вердикт скептика:** Partially confirmed, partially refuted; severity low stands. CONFIRMED: (1) script-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline' at /home/user/workshop/next.config.ts:39 (finding cites :40, which is style-src) — a documented Next.js tradeoff, not a bug: node_modules/next/dist/docs/01-app/02-guides/content-security-policy.md:38,181 state nonces require dynamic rendering, and the same doc (lines 417–435) presents the 'unsafe-inline' header as the supported "Without Nonces" option; with cacheComponents:true + partialPrefetching:true (next.config.ts:86,95) a nonce would force all platform pages dynamic. No XSS sinks exist today: zero matches for dangerouslySetInnerHTML / innerHTML / eval( / new Function / next/script / any markdown renderer in app/, components/, lib/. (2) https://unpkg.com in connect-src (next.config.ts:33, used at :44 — not :46) is referenced nowhere else in the repo — a dead allowance; removing it is safe. REFUTED: https://cdn.jsdelivr.net is NOT redundant. lib/platform/montage/engine.ts:142-150 (loadCore) falls back to jsDelivr when the self-hosted /ffmpeg/ffmpeg-core.{js,wasm} fetch fails (HTTP error / abort), using URLs from lib/platform/montage/core-files.ts:32,39,47, and accepts CDN bytes only if SHA-256 matches pinned hashes (engine.ts:127-131; core-files.ts:37,45), so supply-chain exposure is already bounded to byte-identical builds. The colleague's fix ("убрать unpkg/jsdelivr из connect-src") would silently break this fallback: a CSP-blocked fetch throws TypeError, loadCoreFrom("cdn") rejects, montage fails with no recovery. Scenario at 10 users: no impact unless Vercel fails to serve the 32 MB wasm (git blob, no LFS: `git check-attr` empty). At 50 users: 50×32 MB ≈ 1.6 GB per workshop from Vercel Hobby; a transient 5xx/throttle during the montage lesson is exactly when the jsDelivr fallback saves the lesson — removing it converts a self-healing path into a hard failure. Also verified: /app/studio/:path* (next.config.ts:92, not :94) matches /app/studio itself (headers.md:104, `*` = zero or more), so the studio CSP is applied. Confidence: high (all claims verified in code and bundled Next docs).
- **При 10:** Само по себе не ломает урок; снижает глубину защиты, если где-то появится XSS (сейчас sinks не найдены, React экранирует).
- **При 50:** То же.
- **Как чиним (уточнённый план):** 1. next.config.ts:33 — change `const ffmpegCdn = "https://unpkg.com https://cdn.jsdelivr.net";` to `const ffmpegCdn = "https://cdn.jsdelivr.net";` (drop only unpkg; jsdelivr is the live SHA-256-pinned fallback in lib/platform/montage/engine.ts:142-150). Update the comment at next.config.ts:32 to say "грузится из /public, запасной источник — jsDelivr (sha256-проверка в engine.ts)" and fix the stale mention of unpkg at docs/platform/spec.md:155. Optionally tighten to a path-scoped source, e.g. `https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/@ffmpeg/core@0.12.10/dist/esm/` (CSP source expressions accept paths; import FFMPEG_CORE_VERSION from lib/platform/montage/core-files.ts, which has no imports and is safe to load in next.config.ts) — low value, optional. 2. Do NOT remove cdn.jsdelivr.net unless engine.ts loadCore fallback is removed in the same change, and only after confirming Vercel bandwidth headroom for 50×32 MB per workshop; recommend keeping the fallback. 3. Keep 'unsafe-inline' for script-src: it is the Next-documented pattern for cached/static pages; nonce would require giving up cacheComponents/PPR on all platform routes. Compensating control: add ESLint `react/no-danger: "error"` (eslint-plugin-react) to enforce the existing "no dangerouslySetInnerHTML / no raw HTML rendering" rule mechanically rather than by review habit; currently no violations exist in app/, components/, lib/. 4. Add a one-line check to lib/platform/montage/test/run.mjs (already verifies sha256 of public/ffmpeg files) that CORE_JS.cdn/CORE_WASM.cdn origins are present in the studio CSP string, so a future CSP edit cannot silently break the fallback. Effort: small. No side effects: the studio page is the only route receiving the ffmpeg CSP variant (next.config.ts:81,92).
- **Сложность:** small

### TUX-06 · low · Password reset revokes every session of the student (GoTrue admin UpdatePassword → Logout) and the UI does not say so
- **Файлы:** app/(platform)/teach/actions.ts:307-309, app/(platform)/teach/groups/[id]/StudentList.tsx:305-327, lib/server/dal/auth.ts:59-62
- **Вердикт скептика:** Confirmed from code and GoTrue source (confidence high). app/(platform)/teach/actions.ts:308 calls `auth.admin.updateUserById(student.id, { password })`; GoTrue internal/api/admin.go:268 then calls `user.UpdatePassword(tx, nil)`, and internal/models/user.go UpdatePassword ends with `if sessionID == nil { return Logout(tx, u.ID) }` — every refresh token of the student is revoked. supabase/config.toml:40 sets jwt_expiry = 3600, setup.md:35 mandates asymmetric JWT keys, and lib/server/dal/auth.ts:58 / proxy.ts:57 use getClaims (local signature check), so the still-logged-in device keeps working until the access token expires (up to 1 h), then the refresh fails. The UI text at StudentList.tsx:149 («Старый перестанет работать») does not say that open sessions will close. Two corrections to the scenario: (1) the student is NOT "bounced to /login at an arbitrary moment" during a generation — `/api/*` is not in PROTECTED (proxy.ts:14), so the proxy passes the request through, `/api/jobs/[id]` returns 401 (route.ts:25), `fetchJob` returns null (GenerateClient.ts:52-53) and `useLiveJob` keeps polling every 5 s forever (GenerateClient.ts:79-90) — the student sees a spinner that never completes until they navigate, at which point requireUser/proxy redirects to /login. (2) Lazy polling stops with them: if nobody polls the job within SUBMITTED_HARD_LIMIT_MS = 60 min (job-rules.ts:16, 45), the next poll after re-login refunds the job (job-progress.ts:137-138 / job-rules.ts:97) even if kie.ai finished — stars return to the student but the owner's kie credits are spent. In practice this needs a reset mid-lesson for a student who is still working on another device; typical workshop use (reset before the lesson because the password is lost) is harmless. At 10 users: rare, one confused student + teacher. At 50 users: same mechanism, slightly more frequent; no cascade, no money at risk beyond one generation's kie cost. Severity low is correct.
- **При 10:** A student who is logged in on a tablet asks for a new password for their phone → up to an hour later the tablet silently logs out mid-task; the running video job finishes server-side, but polling stops until re-login.
- **При 50:** Same, more often; the teacher cannot tell why «my tablet logged me out» and suspects the platform.
- **Как чиним (уточнённый план):** 1) StudentList.tsx:149 — replace the hint with: «Новый пароль покажем один раз. Старый перестанет работать, а все открытые сессии ученика закроются в течение часа — пусть войдёт заново с новым паролем. Не сбрасывайте пароль, пока у ученика идёт генерация.» 2) Do NOT add an explicit admin.signOut(scope global): it is redundant (GoTrue already revokes all refresh tokens in UpdatePassword) and would only add a second round-trip. 3) Optional, small: make the revocation immediate rather than "within an hour" is not possible with asymmetric-key local validation unless getUser also checks the session server-side, which would add a GoTrue call per request for 50 students — not worth it. 4) Optional UX hardening, independent of this finding: in useLiveJob (GenerateClient.ts:79-90) stop polling and show «Войдите заново» when fetchJob gets 401 (fetchJob currently collapses every non-OK into null), so a silent infinite spinner cannot happen. 5) docs/platform/setup.md — add one line in the teacher section: a password reset logs the student out of all devices within an hour; do it before the lesson, not during a running generation.
- **Сложность:** small

### TUX-07 · low · For a «Подростки» group the lesson-2/3 tasks are locked unless the teacher ticks «Модели Google для младше 18» — and the hint actively discourages it
- **Файлы:** docs/course/lesson-2.json (tasks l2-t2-sheet, l2-t3-frames: nano-banana-2-1), docs/course/lesson-3.json (l3-t3-talking-head: kling/ai-avatar-standard access adult), lib/server/prices.ts:97-113, lib/server/generate-options.ts:209-219, app/(platform)/teach/CreateGroupForm.tsx:151-157, components/platform/LessonTaskTools.tsx:158-171
- **Вердикт скептика:** Code behaviour is confirmed exactly as described, but it is intended policy, not a defect, and it does not touch the guaranteed Monday scenario — so severity drops from medium to low (teacher-docs / lesson-authoring polish).

Confirmed from code (confidence: high):
- lib/server/prices.ts:104-105 — access=google is denied to any non-adult unless group.allowGoogleMinors; :102-103 access=adult always denies minors; :106-109 adult_or_16_consent returns "adult_only" for u16.
- lib/server/generate-options.ts:30 — describeAction calls canUse(user, action, ai.model) with the lesson's exact model; :32-36 the alternative-model list is built only for staff, so a student gets no fallback and no picker. Result: available=false with reason.
- components/platform/LessonTaskTools.tsx:145-149, 247 — chip disabled; :158-171 lock list with platformErrorMessage(reason).
- docs/course/lesson-2.json:174-180, 212 — l2-t2 and l2-t3 hard-code nano-banana-2-1 (image, image_edit); lesson-3.json:144 voice = google/gemini-3-8-flash-tts (access google), :227-233 l3 talking head = nano-banana-2-1 + kling/ai-avatar-standard (access adult). supabase/migrations/20261101200000_init.sql:863-867 — flux-3/klein-* are access=all but appear in no lesson aiAction.
- app/(platform)/teach/CreateGroupForm.tsx:67-70 — hint "По условиям Google — только с 18." This is a compliance statement consistent with docs/decisions.md:50 and :87 (Gemini API terms), not a UX bug; it should NOT be softened.

Why not medium:
1. For 770 (the scenario the owner needs guaranteed) impact is zero: docs/course/770-brief.md:10-11 and decisions.md:10 say the teacher sets everyone to «взрослый»; decisions.md:98 allows the teacher to set any age band.
2. The student-facing lock text already names the remedy ("Ведущий может включить их для группы", lib/platform/messages.ts:61), and the teacher-side fix is a single checkbox (GroupSettings) or an age change (StudentList.tsx:115-125); students then reload. At 10 or 50 teens this is a few minutes of one-time friction, not errors or lost stars.
3. decisions.md:114 records that reduced features for 14–17 are a known, accepted trade-off ("пока младшие получают только душу бота"), so the behaviour is designed, not accidental.
4. Partially incorrect detail in the finding: for lesson 3 the lipsync chip (kling/ai-avatar-standard, access=adult) stays locked for minors regardless of the checkbox; the finding's fix (2) cannot help there because price_list has no non-staff lipsync alternative (init.sql:879-880). The "adult_only" wording for u16 on motion is imprecise but not misleading for that student — a u16 cannot use it under any flag.

Proposed fix (2) — silent fallback in describeAction — is risky: lesson step texts hard-code "Сгенерировать · 5 ★" (lesson-2.json:167, 196-203), FLUX costs 2★ so instructions would mismatch; l2-t3 and l3 talking head rely on image_edit with a character-sheet reference whose prompts are tuned for Nano Banana; a silent swap hides the age policy from the teacher; and it would not fix voice/lipsync anyway. describeLessonActions already supports several aiActions per task (generate-options.ts:67-73, deduped by key), so an explicit alternative belongs in lesson content, not in a runtime fallback.
- **При 10:** Not for 770. For the next teens group of 10: during lesson 2 the teacher discovers no one can generate the character sheet; he must leave the lesson page, find the checkbox in group settings, and students must reload.
- **При 50:** A 50-teen workshop stalls for several minutes at task 2 while the teacher hunts the setting; some students buy FLUX elsewhere or give up.
- **Как чиним (уточнённый план):** Not needed before Monday. For later centres with teen groups:

1. Lesson content, not runtime fallback: in docs/course/lesson-2.json tasks l2-t2-sheet and l2-t3-frames, and lesson-3.json l3-t3-talking-head, add a second aiAction {type:"image"/"image_edit", model:"flux-3/klein-text-to-image"/"flux-3/klein-edit-image", stars:2}. describeLessonActions (lib/server/generate-options.ts:67-73) will render both chips; the Google one shows locked with its reason, the FLUX one works for minors. Reword step texts that hard-code "· 5 ★" to "кнопку «Сгенерировать»" so they stay true for either model. Verify FLUX Klein Edit handles the character-sheet-reference prompts before shipping (effort: content + a test run, medium).

2. Teacher pre-flight on the group page (app/(platform)/teach/groups/[id]/page.tsx): if any student has age_band != adult and allow_google_minors is false, show one note: "У N учеников возраст до 18 и модели Google выключены: в уроке 2 (задания 2–3) и уроке 3 (озвучка) будут доступны только модели FLUX / недоступна озвучка; говорящая голова (Kling Avatar) — только 18+." Data is already loaded (StudentList ageBand, group.allowGoogleMinors). Keep the existing Google-terms hint in CreateGroupForm.tsx:70 unchanged — it is a compliance statement per docs/decisions.md:50, 87.

3. Age-aware message for adult_or_16_consent: in lib/server/prices.ts:109 return a new reason "teen_consent_only" instead of "adult_only"; add it to the UseDenied union (prices.ts:30), to GenerateReason in lib/platform/generation-types.ts:117-119, to the HTTP status map in lib/server/generate.ts:143 area (403 like adult_only), and to lib/platform/messages.ts:60 with text "С 18 лет, или с 16 лет при отметке согласия родителей у ведущего." Low risk: purely additive string/enum change; grep all switch statements over the reason union so TypeScript exhaustiveness passes.

4. Document in docs/platform/setup.md (teacher onboarding) the one-line rule: "Группа младше 18 → либо галочка «Модели Google для младше 18» (ответственность центра по условиям Google), либо уроки с FLUX-альтернативой."
- **Сложность:** medium

### TUX-08 · low · Budget math is informational only: given > budget, more students than planned, and today-vs-course budget are never flagged
- **Файлы:** app/(platform)/teach/groups/[id]/page.tsx:265-280, app/(platform)/teach/groups/[id]/report/page.tsx:40, lib/server/dal/teacher.ts:252-279, components/platform/BudgetFields.tsx:345-350, app/(platform)/teach/actions.ts:56-58
- **Вердикт скептика:** Confirmed on the code, with corrected line references (the finding's line numbers do not exist in these files — page.tsx has 167 lines, teacher.ts 236, BudgetFields.tsx 96). What holds: (1) `planned_students` is written (teach/actions.ts:57,84,120) and read back only into the settings form (groups/[id]/page.tsx:50 → GroupSettings.tsx:75 → BudgetFields.tsx:13,17-22); the «Звёзды группы» card (page.tsx:96-111) prints Бюджет / Роздано / Потрачено as three plain `<dd>` with no comparison, and `group.studentCount` (page.tsx:62) is never compared with `plannedStudents`. (2) The DB path confirms the 11th-student scenario: `private.apply_student_meta` (group_budget.sql:61-77) credits `groups.stars_per_student` to every new student with no check against `budget_stars`; nothing server-side caps total given. (3) report/page.tsx:40 does pair the daily figure «Потрачено сегодня» with the hint «Бюджет группы: N ★», a course-long number. (4) BudgetFields.tsx:15,18,82: `manual` is set true on any edit of «Каждому ученику» and is never reset, so clearing the field does not restore recalc. Two corrections to the colleague's framing: the «Роздано» figure already sits directly under «Бюджет» on the same card (page.tsx:99-105), so an attentive teacher can see 5720 > 5200 — it is unflagged, not hidden; and «overspend of kie credits» is overstated: stars handed out are not credits spent, kie is charged only when students actually generate (lock_for_spend/create_job), so the money impact is a possible, not guaranteed, 10 % ceiling increase. Severity low is right at both 10 and 50 users — no lesson breaks, no error is shown to students; this is teacher-dashboard polish. One side effect in the proposed fix must be fixed: `getGroupStarStats` (teacher.ts:184-189) counts every ledger row that is not debit/bot_reply/refund as «given», i.e. it includes `quiz_bonus` (+20 ★ per passed quiz, report/page.tsx:42), `grant` and `adjust` (init.sql:155-156). A badge on `given > budgetStars` would therefore fire on a perfectly normal group as soon as students pass quizzes (10 × 520 = 5200 = budget, plus 200 ★ of bonuses → false alarm every time). Confidence: high (all claims verified in code).
- **При 10:** Teacher adds an 11th student → 5720 ★ handed out against a 5200 ★ budget with no warning; «Потрачено сегодня 300 ★ / Бюджет 5200» reads as if 5200 were a daily cap.
- **При 50:** 50 planned, 55 created (late sign-ups) → 10% overspend of kie credits unnoticed until the invoice.
- **Как чиним (уточнённый план):** Keep it small and avoid the false positive: (a) In app/(platform)/teach/groups/[id]/page.tsx «Звёзды группы» card (lines 96-111), add a `<Badge tone="warn">` (Badge already supports `warn`, used at page.tsx:64) when `group.plannedStudents != null && group.studentCount > group.plannedStudents` → «учеников больше плана: N из M», and when `group.starsPerStudent != null && group.budgetStars != null && group.studentCount * group.starsPerStudent > group.budgetStars` → «раздача по плану превышает бюджет». Both values are already in `GroupSummary` (teacher.ts:13-27), so no DAL or DB change. Do NOT compare `stars.given` with `budgetStars` as the finding proposes, because `given` includes quiz_bonus/grant/adjust (teacher.ts:187-188) and would alarm on normal groups; if a stars-based check is wanted, compute given from `reason = 'welcome'` only in getGroupStarStats (add a `welcome` field) and compare that. (b) report/page.tsx:40: change the hint to «Бюджет группы на курс: N ★» (and optionally move it to the «Потрачено за всё время» Stat at line 41, where a course-long reference actually belongs). (c) BudgetFields.tsx:81-84: in the perStudent onChange, `setManual(e.target.value !== "")`, and after clearing call `recalc(budget, planned)` so an empty field re-enables auto-calculation; also show the computed value as a hint under the field when `manual` is true so the teacher sees what the automatic number would have been. No server-side enforcement is needed: decisions.md:99 says the per-student figure «можно поправить» and late sign-ups are a legitimate case, so a warning, not a block, is the right level.
- **Сложность:** small

### TUX-10 · low · Whole platform uses Europe/Berlin for «today» and all timestamps while the centre is in Chișinău (UTC+2)
- **Файлы:** lib/server/group-reports.ts:222, app/(platform)/teach/groups/[id]/report/page.tsx:36, components/platform/GalleryGrid.tsx:14-20, app/(platform)/app/page.tsx:151-159, supabase/migrations/20261105100000_security_hardening.sql:180, lib/server/bot/lifetime.ts:349-358
- **Вердикт скептика:** Confirmed from code and docs, confidence high. The centre is in Moldova (docs/course/770-brief.md:1 «общины «770» (Молдова)»; docs/course/lesson-1.md:41 and docs/research/telegram.md:108 already use Europe/Chisinau and note «в Германии на час раньше»). Europe/Chisinau (EET/EEST) is exactly 1 h ahead of Europe/Berlin (CET/CEST) year-round, same EU DST dates. All 9 hard-coded Europe/Berlin sites exist: lib/server/group-reports.ts:29 (berlinDay → «Потрачено сегодня»), app/(platform)/app/page.tsx:157 (ledger times), app/(platform)/app/bot/leads/page.tsx:124, lib/server/bot/csv.ts:51 (leads CSV), lib/server/bot/lifetime.ts:49 (berlinDate → bot retirement day and, via lib/server/bot/secrets.ts:21, the per-day salt of sender rate-limit hashes), lib/server/gallery.ts:193, components/platform/GalleryGrid.tsx:19, supabase/migrations/20261101200000_init.sql:491 and 20261105100000_security_hardening.sql:180 (lock_for_spend day boundary). The teacher report literally says «День считается по Берлину» (report/page.tsx:36) and spec §5 documents «дата по Берлину» (lifetime.ts:5-6), so it was a conscious choice (the owner is in Germany), but it is wrong for the paying centre and for resale. Corrections to the finding: (1) «every displayed time» is overstated — date-only fields (ends_on, lastDay) are formatted via formatDay with timeZone UTC on a YYYY-MM-DD string (teach/page.tsx:84, teach/groups/[id]/page.tsx:163, lifetime.ts:68) and are correct; only datetime displays and the «сегодня»/retirement boundary are shifted. (2) lock_for_spend's day boundary is dead code today: daily_cap=1,000,000 and global_daily_cap=null (20261103110000_group_budget.sql:12-14). Scenario at 10 users (Monday, 75-min lesson): nothing fails, no money/data risk; gallery/ledger/leads timestamps show 1 h early; «Потрачено сегодня» would split only if a lesson crossed 01:00 Chișinău — not the case. Scenario at 50 users: identical, no scaling component; the visible defect is reputational when another centre (other timezone) is onboarded. Severity low is correct. The proposed fix is over-engineered and has two side effects the colleague missed: GalleryGrid.tsx is a "use client" component that is server-rendered — formatting with the browser's timezone there would produce React hydration-mismatch warnings (the explicit timeZone is what prevents them today); and berlinDate is also the day-salt for hashSender (secrets.ts:21), so moving the day boundary changes when sender rate-limit hashes rotate (harmless one-time shift, but it must stay a single shared function). A groups.timezone column is unnecessary while one centre = one deployment.
- **При 10:** Cosmetic: gallery/ledger times are 1 h early; an evening lesson ending after midnight Chișinău is split across two «days» in «Потрачено сегодня».
- **При 50:** Same; when the platform is sold to a centre in another timezone the hard-coding becomes a visible defect.
- **Как чиним (уточнённый план):** Minimal, low-risk version (effort small, not medium): (1) Add one shared constant in lib/platform (importable from both server and client code), e.g. `export const PLATFORM_TIME_ZONE = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_PLATFORM_TIME_ZONE ?? "Europe/Chisinau"`, so a resold instance sets it per deployment without a schema change; replace the 7 TypeScript occurrences (group-reports.ts:29, app/page.tsx:157, bot/leads/page.tsx:124, bot/csv.ts:51, bot/lifetime.ts:49, gallery.ts:193, GalleryGrid.tsx:19) with the constant and rename berlinDay/berlinDate to platformDay/platformDate. Keep GalleryGrid formatting with the explicit zone (do NOT switch to the browser's zone — it is an SSR'd client component and would hydrate with mismatches; if browser-local time is wanted later, format inside useEffect or mark the node suppressHydrationWarning). (2) Replace the string at teach/groups/[id]/report/page.tsx:36 with «День считается по Кишинёву» (or derive the city from the constant), and update spec §5 and the comment in lifetime.ts:5-6. (3) For lock_for_spend: since daily caps are disabled, either leave the SQL untouched, or in a new migration read the zone from settings (`select value #>> '{}' from public.settings where key = 'time_zone'`, seeded 'Europe/Chisinau') instead of the literal — do not add a per-group column; a global cap needs one zone anyway. Note that berlinDate also salts hashSender (secrets.ts:21): keep every caller on the one renamed function so hashes stay consistent within a day; the only effect is that the daily hash rotation moves from 01:00 to 00:00 Chișinău once at deploy. (4) Record the decision in docs/decisions.md («время платформы — Europe/Chisinau; меняется через NEXT_PUBLIC_PLATFORM_TIME_ZONE») and setup.md so a new centre knows the knob.
- **Сложность:** medium

### TUX-11 · low · Docs drift: setup.md and spec §7 still describe 1000 welcome stars, daily caps and a smaller teacher cabinet
- **Файлы:** docs/platform/setup.md:103-107,209, docs/platform/spec.md:136-146, supabase/migrations/20261103110000_group_budget.sql:12-17, supabase/tests/003_student_meta.test.sql:2,40
- **Вердикт скептика:** Confirmed documentation drift, with two corrections. (1) Drift is real: supabase/migrations/20261103110000_group_budget.sql:12-17 sets daily_cap=1,000,000, global_daily_cap=null and welcome_stars=0, and apply_student_meta (lines 61-64) grants coalesce(groups.stars_per_student, settings.welcome_stars, 0); yet docs/platform/setup.md:103-104 still says «стартовые звёзды нового ученика (по умолчанию 1000)», setup.md:106-107 documents `global_daily_cap = 20000` as a normal knob, setup.md:208-209 tells the checker to create a group by code only and then expect «баланс 1000 ★», and docs/platform/spec.md:136-146 (§7) describes group creation without budget_stars/planned_students/stars_per_student and omits the existing report/gallery/bots pages under app/(platform)/teach/groups/[id]/. docs/decisions.md:99 explicitly says there is no daily limit and no common welcome stars, so setup.md contradicts the accepted decision. The global_daily_cap enforcement path still exists (20261105100000_security_hardening.sql:199-203), so a centre that pastes the documented snippet would actually re-enable a platform-wide daily cap — at 50 students doing 1–3 videos (25★) + images + chat (~100–150★/student/day ≈ 5–8k★) a 20,000★ cap would not trip, but it is a live, undocumented-as-disabled switch. (2) Overstated parts: supabase/tests/003_student_meta.test.sql:2,40 is NOT stale — the test sets groups.stars_per_student=1000 (lines 11-12), so «1000★ один раз» is exactly what is asserted; no change needed. The 50-user scenario «admin creates students without a budget → 0★» is weaker than claimed: components/platform/BudgetFields.tsx:37,58,79 mark the budget fields `required` in CreateGroupForm.tsx:63, and CreateStudents.tsx:69 shows «0 ★ (задайте бюджет группы в настройках)», so the UI already steers the admin; the residual path is only entering 0 or following setup.md §1.6 literally. No runtime effect for 770 (group already configured). Severity low is correct: no lesson breaks, no money/data risk; it is onboarding polish for other centres. Confidence: high (verified against migrations, docs, tests and UI code).
- **При 10:** No runtime effect for 770 (group already configured).
- **При 50:** Another centre's admin follows setup.md, creates students without a group budget → everyone has 0 ★ («Каждому — 0 ★» hint is the only clue).
- **Как чиним (уточнённый план):** 1) docs/platform/setup.md §1.6 (lines 103-107): replace the welcome_stars / global_daily_cap snippets with a short note: «Звёзды даёт группа: учитель задаёт бюджет и число учеников при создании группы, каждый новый ученик получает stars_per_student один раз. `welcome_stars` = 0 и `global_daily_cap` = null — выключены решением от 3 ноября 2026; не включайте их, иначе вернётся общий дневной лимит (проверка в lock_for_spend) или общие стартовые звёзды для групп без бюджета.» Keep the generation_enabled kill switch and price_list examples as they are. Do not delete all mention of the caps — the enforcement code still exists, so admins should know the knobs are intentionally off. 2) setup.md §5 step 2 (line 208): «Создать группу: код `md1`, бюджет, например 10 000 ★ на 10 учеников (каждому — 1000 ★) → добавить 2–3 ника → «Печать карточек»»; step 3 (line 209): «баланс равен «каждому» из настроек группы (в примере 1000 ★)». 3) docs/platform/spec.md §7 (line 138): add «бюджет группы: budget_stars, planned_students, stars_per_student (каждый новый ученик получает stars_per_student один раз; уже созданным — «Начислить»)»; add bullets for the existing pages /teach/groups/[id]/report, /gallery, /bots. Optionally mark spec §2 bullets «Новому ученику — 1000★» and «У группы есть дневной лимит» (lines 26, 28) with «(история)» for clarity, though the §2 note at line 24 already says so. 4) Drop the proposed change to supabase/tests/003_student_meta.test.sql — its comment is accurate (the group is created with stars_per_student=1000). 5) Optional UX hardening (separate, overlaps TUX-03): in BudgetFields show a warning when stars_per_student computes to 0, so an admin cannot create a zero-budget group by accident. Side effects: documentation-only changes, no runtime impact.
- **Сложность:** small

### TUX-12 · low · Small Russian label inconsistencies across teacher pages
- **Файлы:** app/(platform)/teach/groups/[id]/bots/page.tsx:181, app/(platform)/teach/groups/[id]/gallery/page.tsx:54, app/(platform)/teach/groups/[id]/report/page.tsx:11,33,35, app/(platform)/teach/groups/[id]/page.tsx:243, app/(platform)/teach/groups/[id]/StudentList.tsx:258,268, app/(platform)/teach/groups/[id]/report/page.tsx:145,169, lib/server/group-reports.ts:323, app/(platform)/teach/groups/[id]/CreateStudents.tsx:49,96, app/(platform)/teach/groups/[id]/GroupSettings.tsx:368
- **Вердикт скептика:** Every substantive claim is confirmed in the code, but most cited line numbers in the finding are wrong (files are much shorter than the cited lines). Verified against the working tree (/home/user/workshop): (1) Back links differ: app/(platform)/teach/groups/[id]/bots/page.tsx:44 renders «К группе», while gallery/page.tsx:54 and report/page.tsx:33 render «Группа {group.code}». (2) report/page.tsx:11 sets metadata title «Отчёт группы», but the page title (report/page.tsx:35) and the section card on the group page (app/(platform)/teach/groups/[id]/page.tsx:74) say «Прогресс, тесты и звёзды»; bots and gallery pages have metadata equal to their page titles, so report is the odd one out. (3) StudentList.tsx:90 allows min={-10000}, hint at :96 «Минус — снять», button at :106 is «Начислить»; grant_stars (init.sql:735) writes reason 'adjust' for negative amounts and lib/platform/messages.ts:31 labels it «Снял ведущий» — so the button name misdescribes half of its function. (4) lib/server/group-reports.ts:130 increments spent.generations for every ledger row with reason 'debit'; create_job_with_debit (init.sql:599) writes 'debit' for any job with p_stars > 0 regardless of action, so 1-★ chat replies are counted as «Генераций» (report/page.tsx:144-146). Zero-star montage writes no debit row (init.sql guard `if p_stars > 0`), so it is not counted — consistent with the finding. (5) CreateStudents.tsx:49 card «Добавить учеников» with button «Создать» at :96 — confirmed (minor). (6) GroupSettings.tsx:33 says «Пауза сразу останавливает все генерации учеников группы». lock_for_spend in supabase/migrations/20261105100000_security_hardening.sql:187-189 checks g.paused only inside `if p_amount > 0`, so a 0-★ montage (lib/server/studio.ts:363 stars: 0) still passes; nothing in lib/server/job-progress.ts or gateway.ts cancels running jobs on pause (grep for `paused` finds nothing there; only generate.ts:139 maps group_paused→409 for new jobs). The Notice at GroupSettings.tsx:31 («ученики не могут генерировать, боты учеников молчат») is accurate: bots do stop (lib/server/bot/brain.ts:183). Impact: purely wording/consistency; no error path, no money/data risk, identical at 10 and 50 users (the 10/50 scenario dimension does not apply). Severity low is correct. Confidence: high.
- **При 10:** Minor confusion; teacher asks «where is the report?» because the tab says Отчёт and the card says Прогресс.
- **При 50:** Same; matters for the «wow» impression when demoing to other centres.
- **Как чиним (уточнённый план):** Keep the proposed fix with corrected anchors and two refinements; no side effects beyond text (metadata title affects only tab/history, not routing or caching). 1) bots/page.tsx:44 → «Группа {group.code}» to match gallery/page.tsx:54 and report/page.tsx:33 (or extract a shared BackToGroup link component used by all three). 2) report/page.tsx:11 → metadata title «Прогресс, тесты и звёзды» (same string as report/page.tsx:35 and page.tsx:74); optionally use a single constant exported from one place. 3) StudentList.tsx:106 → «Начислить / снять», or compute the label from the sign of the entered amount client-side; keep hint at :96. 4) report/page.tsx:144-146 column header «Генераций» → «Списаний» (matches what group-reports.ts:130 counts); if a real media count is wanted, split in group-reports.ts by jobs.action (chat vs image/video/voice/music/motion/lipsync) using the job_id on the ledger row — this requires an extra join, so only do it if the owner wants it. 5) CreateStudents.tsx:96 → «Добавить». 6) GroupSettings.tsx:33 → «Пауза не даёт запускать новые платные генерации, боты учеников молчат. Уже начатые задания доделаются, монтаж (0 ★) остаётся доступен. Звёзды не сгорают.» Do not change the Notice at :31 — it is already accurate. Do not change lock_for_spend semantics to block 0-★ montage: that would break the montage save path (studio.ts:363) for paused groups and is out of scope for a wording finding.
- **Сложность:** small

### TUX-13 · low · Quiz key edits after attempts: stored score vs re-graded items disagree; answers live in the public repo
- **Файлы:** lib/server/lesson-progress.ts:399-410, docs/course/770-status.md:169, supabase/migrations/20261105100000_security_hardening.sql:17-21, docs/course/lesson-2.json
- **Вердикт скептика:** Mechanism confirmed, line anchors corrected. lib/server/lesson-progress.ts is only 97 lines; recordedQuizResult lives at lines 69-81 (not 399-410). It returns the DB-stored score/max_score/bonus (quiz_attempts, init.sql:175-186) but builds `items` by re-grading the stored answers against the CURRENT key (getQuizKey, lib/server/course.ts:196-199; gradeQuiz, lib/server/quiz.ts:28-44). gradeQuiz only returns null when the answer count or an option index mismatches; commit e2bfe8b (6 Nov 01:18 UTC) rewrote 6 of 10 lesson-2 questions and changed 4 correctIndex values while keeping 10 questions × 4 options, so an attempt stored before that commit re-grades 'successfully' and QuizForm.tsx (QuizResult) shows the old headline score next to check/cross marks, 'Ваш ответ' and 'Верно' taken from the new questions — a genuinely contradictory card. Teacher attempts are never stored (actions.ts:54-56, practice mode), the 770 group was created 5 Nov and starts Monday, so the only accounts that could hold such a row are the two md1 test students (770-status.md:28); nothing in the repo proves they submitted the lesson-2 quiz, so 'md1 testers see a contradictory card' is plausible but unverified (confidence medium). At 10 and at 50 users on Monday: zero impact unless a quiz key is edited after students have attempted it — which the 6 Nov edit shows is a realistic workflow (status #10 says lesson 2 still needs a re-pass). Public answers: confirmed — spec.md:10 ('Репозиторий публичный'), remote github.com/simonspivak/workshop, 10 correctIndex per docs/course/lesson-*.json, acknowledged in 20261105100000_security_hardening.sql:17-19. Farming is blocked by unique(user_id, lesson_id) and star_ledger_quiz_once (init.sql:169,184), so exposure is ≤100★ per student (≈19% of the 520★ budget; 50 students worst case 5000★ ≈ 190 videos only if every adult finds and reads the repo JSON), and the teacher sees quiz scores (spec.md:107). No lesson breaks, no data at risk → low is correct. The colleague's fix is not 'small': a key hash column needs a migration on quiz_attempts, a new record_quiz_attempt signature (security definer, revoked by exact signature at init.sql:843; old overload must be dropped), wallet.ts:156-185, actions.ts, and tests 001_rls/002_wallet/004_security_hardening; hashing the whole quiz would also flag harmless typo fixes in explanations.
- **При 10:** Only md1 testers see a contradictory quiz card; 770 students who google the repo get +20 ★ per lesson (max 100 ★ ≈ 4 videos).
- **При 50:** Same, ×50; bonus cost is bounded (100 ★/student).
- **Как чиним (уточнённый план):** Two independent parts, neither needed before Monday. (1) Consistency — no migration needed: in recordedQuizResult (lib/server/lesson-progress.ts:69-81) compare the re-graded result with the stored one: if `!graded || graded.maxScore !== maxScore || graded.score !== score`, return `items: []` plus a new flag `keyChanged: true` on QuizResultView (lib/platform/lesson-types.ts:54), and in QuizResult (QuizForm.tsx) render «Тест обновлён после вашей попытки — показан засчитанный результат без разбора» when keyChanged. This catches every edit that would make the per-item marks contradict the headline score (the case that is actually confusing); an edit that keeps the same score cannot produce a contradiction. If a hard version is wanted later, store a hash of only (question, options, correctIndex) per item — not explanations — in a new quiz_attempts.key_hash column, add it as a defaulted trailing parameter to record_quiz_attempt (drop the old overload and update the revoke list at init.sql:843 plus tests 001/002/004). Also add a process rule to docs/decisions.md: a quiz key is changed only before the lesson is taught, or together with deleting that lesson's quiz_attempts for the group (service_role has delete, init.sql:377). (2) Answers in the public repo — do not move correctIndex to a settings table (adds a DB read to submitQuiz and getLessonState for no real gain). The cheap, complete fix is to make the GitHub repo private: Vercel and the Supabase GitHub integration keep working with a private repo, and it also protects the course content the owner intends to sell to other centers. If the repo must stay public, accept the ≤100★/student exposure, keep the teacher-side quiz report (spec.md:107) as the detection point, and optionally let the teacher revoke a suspicious bonus via the existing 'adjust' ledger reason.
- **Сложность:** small

### TUX-14 · low · Groups created before the budget migration (md1) cannot save any setting until budget fields are filled
- **Файлы:** components/platform/BudgetFields.tsx:365,386,407, app/(platform)/teach/actions.ts:56-58,103-126
- **Вердикт скептика:** Confirmed in code (confidence: high). (1) supabase/migrations/20261103110000_group_budget.sql:6-9 adds budget_stars / planned_students / stars_per_student as nullable columns with CHECK constraints and NO backfill UPDATE — every group that existed before 3 Nov keeps NULL in all three. (2) components/platform/BudgetFields.tsx:37, :58, :79 (not 365/386/407 — the file is 96 lines) mark all three <input type="number"> as `required`, and the component initialises them from `initial?.budget?.toString() ?? ""` (lines 12-14), so for a legacy group the inputs render empty. (3) app/(platform)/teach/groups/[id]/GroupSettings.tsx:46-108 is ONE <form> containing title, kind, endsOn, BudgetFields (line 75), allowGoogleMinors and shareGallery (line 93) with a single «Сохранить» button — so toggling share_gallery or changing the end date submits the same form and the browser's constraint validation stops the submit on the empty required budget inputs before the server action runs. (4) Even if validation were bypassed, app/(platform)/teach/actions.ts:52-61 defines groupFields with budgetStars/plannedStudents/starsPerStudent as mandatory z.coerce.number() (plannedStudents min 1); updateGroupSchema (line 103) spreads them, so empty strings coerce to 0 and plannedStudents fails min(1) → invalid_input. (5) A legacy group really exists in production: docs/course/770-status.md:28 records the test run in «старой группе md1 (2 ученика … 1000 и 932 ★)» — the 1000★ balance is the pre-budget welcome_stars value, proving md1 predates the migration and therefore has NULL budget fields. Scenario at 10 students (Monday, 770 group): no impact — the 770 group will be created via createGroup after the migration, which always writes all three fields (actions.ts:83-85); only the teacher's demo group md1 is affected, and the workaround (type 0 / N / 0) is trivial, so the lesson does not break. At 50 students / other centres: new deployments run all migrations before creating any group, so no legacy rows exist; impact nil. Severity low is correct. The colleague's FIX is incomplete and slightly risky: making the zod fields nullable does nothing about the HTML `required` attributes that are the actual blocker, and allowing NULL on update means a teacher who clears a field on a properly configured group silently writes stars_per_student = NULL — private.apply_student_meta (group_budget.sql:62-65) then falls back to welcome_stars, which the same migration set to 0, so later-added students would get 0★ without any warning; the «Звёзды группы» stats would also show nulls. Prefilling only `planned` with studentCount still leaves budget and perStudent empty+required.
- **При 10:** Teacher of md1 (demo group) toggles share_gallery → form refuses until he invents a budget.
- **При 50:** Only legacy groups; not an issue for new centres.
- **Как чиним (уточнённый план):** Prefer a one-off data backfill instead of loosening validation. New migration (e.g. supabase/migrations/2026110xxxxxxx_group_budget_backfill.sql): `update public.groups g set budget_stars = coalesce(g.budget_stars, 0), planned_students = coalesce(g.planned_students, greatest(1, (select count(*) from public.profiles p where p.group_id = g.id and p.role = 'student'))), stars_per_student = coalesce(g.stars_per_student, 0) where g.budget_stars is null or g.planned_students is null or g.stars_per_student is null;` — optionally followed by `alter table public.groups alter column budget_stars set not null, alter column planned_students set not null, alter column stars_per_student set not null;` so the invariant "every group has a budget" is enforced at the DB (createGroup already writes all three; no other inserter exists). Then the legacy group renders prefilled 0 / 2 / 0 and the teacher can toggle «Общая галерея» or change anything else without inventing values; the 0★ per new student for md1 is explicit and visible rather than an implicit NULL. Keep groupFields in teach/actions.ts and the `required` attributes in BudgetFields.tsx strict. If a DB migration is not wanted before Monday, a UI-only fallback with the same effect: in GroupSettings pass `initial={{ budget: group.budgetStars ?? 0, planned: group.plannedStudents ?? Math.max(1, studentCount), perStudent: group.starsPerStudent ?? 0 }}` (studentCount is already available in page.tsx line 57 as group.studentCount) and show a hint «Бюджет не задан — заполните» — but note this writes 0/N/0 on the first save, same as the backfill, just later. Do NOT make the schema nullable: it would let a valid budget be erased to NULL and silently zero the stars of later-added students. Also correct the finding's file references to components/platform/BudgetFields.tsx:37,58,79 and app/(platform)/teach/groups/[id]/GroupSettings.tsx:46-108 (single form), plus supabase/migrations/20261103110000_group_budget.sql:6-9 (no backfill).
- **Сложность:** small

## Отклонённые находки

### BP-3 · insertMessage silently falls back to a limit-free direct insert if the SQL function is missing (migration not applied) — limits and dedupe vanish without any error
- Почему отклонено: The finding's precondition — migration 20261105100000 not applied in production — is false, verified against the live Supabase project `workshop` (njmstsfepkrtsixgtbcb) with read-only queries: list_migrations returns 20261101200000 init, 20261103090000, 20261103110000, 20261105100000 security_hardening, 20261106090000 chat_model_label (a later migration applied on top, which the GitHub integration applies in order); pg_proc has public.insert_bot_message(p_bot uuid, p_channel text, p_conversation_id text, p_role text, p_content text, p_ip_hash text, p_telegram_update_id bigint, p_stars integer, p_dedupe_key text, p_sender_per_hour integer, p_bot_per_day integer) — exactly the argument set the RPC sends at lib/server/bot/store.ts:203-215, so PostgREST resolves it and neither PGRST202 nor 42883 is returned; bot_messages.dedupe_key (text) and unique index bot_messages_dedupe_once exist. docs/course/770-status.md:12 independently records '[x] миграция 20261105100000_security_hardening применена'. Therefore the branch at store.ts:217 → insertMessageDirect (store.ts:225-243) is unreachable in the Monday deployment: at 10 and at 50 students every bot message goes through the locked SQL function with dedupe and hour/day counters (security_hardening.sql:57-99), and the scenarios 'two model calls and two 1★ charges per double-click' and 'burst of 100 passes 30/h' do not occur. Even hypothetically (a future deployment for another center where code is deployed before the migration), the damage is smaller than stated: alreadyCharged (brain.ts:147) still blocks sequential re-sends after the first charge, the init-migration unique index bot_messages_tg_update_once (init.sql:241) still dedupes Telegram via the 23505 → 'duplicate' path (store.ts:240), and countRecent (brain.ts:139-144) still caps non-parallel traffic at 30/h and 200/day; only same-window parallel bursts leak. What remains true is code hygiene: a silent degrade-to-weaker-limits path with no log line is a latent trap for the 'sold to other centers' case. That is a low-severity deployment-safety polish, not a Monday risk. Confidence: high (verified in production DB, not inferred).

## Не проверенные (сырые, см. findings.md)
